export default function Layout() {
  const { user, signOut } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const email = user?.email ?? '';
  const name = user?.user_metadata?.full_name || email.split('@')[0] || '?';
  const initial = name ? name[0].toUpperCase() : '?';

  const [xp, setXp] = useState(0);
  const [levelInfo, setLevelInfo] = useState(calcLevel(0));
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [unreadCount, setUnreadCount] = useState(0);
  const [showNotifications, setShowNotifications] = useState(false);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<Notification[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;

    // XP
    const fetchXP = () => getUserXP(user.id).then(v => { setXp(v); setLevelInfo(calcLevel(v)); });
    fetchXP();
    window.addEventListener('xp_updated', fetchXP);

    // Аватарка из профиля
    getProfile(user.id).then(p => { if (p?.avatar_url) setAvatarUrl(p.avatar_url); });

    // Счётчик уведомлений
    getUnreadCount(user.id).then(setUnreadCount);

    // Realtime подписка на новые уведомления
    const unsub = subscribeToNotifications(user.id, () => {
      getUnreadCount(user.id).then(setUnreadCount);
    });

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('xp_updated', fetchXP);
      unsub();
    };
  }, [user?.id]);

  const handleOpenNotifications = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    setShowNotifications(v => !v);
    if (!showNotifications) {
      const notifs = await getNotifications(user.id);
      setNotifications(notifs);
      if (unreadCount > 0) {
        await markAllRead(user.id);
        setUnreadCount(0);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-slate-50">
      {/* ═══ SIDEBAR ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <aside className="w-64 bg-slate-900 flex flex-col shrink-0 hidden md:flex border-r border-slate-800">
        {/* Logo */}
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')}
          className="p-5 flex items-center gap-3 hover:opacity-80 transition w-full text-left"
        >
          <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-indigo-500 flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
            <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-semibold tracking-tight text-white text-lg">StudyForge</span>
        </button>

        {/* XP / Level блок */}
        <div className="mx-4 mt-2 mb-4 p-3 bg-slate-800/50 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/50">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">Level {levelInfo.level}</p>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">{levelInfo.name}</p>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate('/analytics')}
              className="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-300 transition bg-slate-800 p-1.5 rounded-md"
              title="Аналитика"
            >
              <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="h-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-700/50">
            <div
              className="h-full bg-indigo-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-700"
              style={{ width: `${levelInfo.progress}%` }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-between mt-1.5">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500">{xp} XP</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500">{levelInfo.currentXP}/{levelInfo.nextXP}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Navigation */}
        <nav className="flex-1 px-3 pb-6 space-y-5 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
          {navGroups.map((group, idx) => (
            <div key={idx}>
              <h3 className="px-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                {group.title}
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                {group.items.map(item => (
                  <NavLink
                    key={item.to}
                    to={item.to}
                    className={({ isActive }) =>
                      'flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium rounded-lg transition-colors ' +
                      (isActive
                        ? 'bg-slate-800 text-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-white/5'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800/50 hover:text-slate-200')
                    }
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <item.icon className="w-4 h-4 opacity-80" />
                      {item.label}
                    </div>
                    {(item as any).isNew && (
                      <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-400 border border-indigo-500/20 uppercase tracking-widest">
                        New
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </NavLink>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </nav>

        {/* User section */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-800">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {/* Аватарка из профиля */}
            <button onClick={() => navigate('/profile')} className="shrink-0">
              {avatarUrl ? (
                <img src={avatarUrl} alt="avatar" className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full object-cover ring-2 ring-indigo-500/50" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-indigo-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white text-sm font-bold">
                  {initial}
                </div>
              )}
            </button>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-slate-300 text-xs font-medium truncate">{name}</p>
              <p className="text-slate-500 text-[10px] truncate">{email}</p>
            </div>
            {/* Колокольчик уведомлений */}
            <div className="relative">
              <button
                onClick={handleOpenNotifications}
                className="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-200 transition relative"
                title="Уведомления"
              >
                <Bell className="w-4 h-4" />
                {unreadCount > 0 && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-1.5 -right-1.5 w-4 h-4 bg-rose-500 text-white text-[9px] font-black rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                    {unreadCount > 9 ? '9+' : unreadCount}
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>

              {/* Дропдаун уведомлений */}
              {showNotifications && (
                <div className="absolute bottom-8 right-0 w-72 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 z-50 overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="p-3 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">Уведомления</span>
                    <button onClick={() => setShowNotifications(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 text-xs">✕</button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-slate-50">
                    {notifications.length === 0 ? (
                      <p className="p-4 text-sm text-center text-slate-400">Нет уведомлений</p>
                    ) : notifications.map(n => (
                      <div key={n.id} className={`p-3 ${n.is_read ? '' : 'bg-indigo-50/50'}`}>
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-800">{n.title}</p>
                        {n.body && <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 truncate">{n.body}</p>}
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">{new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleString()}</p>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={signOut}
              className="text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-400 transition"
              title="Выйти"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* ═══ MAIN CONTENT ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <main className="flex-1 overflow-auto pb-16 md:pb-0">
        <Outlet />
      </main>

      {/* ═══ MOBILE BOTTOM NAV ════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <nav className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 bg-white border-t border-slate-200 flex md:hidden">
        {mobileNavItems.map(item => (
          <NavLink
            key={item.to}
            to={item.to}
            className={({ isActive }) =>
              'flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-2.5 gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold transition ' +
              (isActive ? 'text-indigo-600' : 'text-slate-400')
            }
          >
            {({ isActive }) => (
              <>
                <item.icon className={`w-5 h-5 ${isActive ? 'text-indigo-600' : 'text-slate-400'}`} />
                {item.label}
              </>
            )}
          </NavLink>
        ))}
      </nav>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { GraduationCap, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Header({ onReset }: { onReset: () => void }) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'ok' | 'error' | 'checking'>('checking');

  useEffect(() => {
    const check = () => {
      fetch('/api/health')
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => setStatus(d.status === 'ok' ? 'ok' : 'error'))
        .catch(() => setStatus('error'));
    };
    check();
    const id = setInterval(check, 30000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  const dotClass = status === 'ok'
    ? 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400'
    : status === 'error'
    ? 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-rose-400 animate-pulse'
    : 'w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-slate-300 animate-pulse';
  const dotTitle = status === 'ok' ? 'Бэкенд работает' : status === 'error' ? 'Бэкенд недоступен' : 'Проверка...';

  return (
    <header className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm sticky top-0 z-50">
      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 h-14 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 flex items-center justify-center">
            <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 text-[15px]">StudyForge</span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 text-xs font-medium">
            Статус системы
            <span className={dotClass} title={dotTitle} />
          </span>
        </div>
        <button onClick={onReset}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 transition text-sm">
          <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Заново
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Upload, FileText, X, Zap } from 'lucide-react';
import SettingsPanel from './SettingsPanel';
import { AnalysisSettings } from '../types';

interface Props {
  settings: AnalysisSettings;
  onSettingsChange: (s: AnalysisSettings) => void;
  onUpload: (f: File) => void;
  onDemo: () => void;
}

export default function UploadZone({ settings, onSettingsChange, onUpload, onDemo }: Props) {
  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [sizeError, setSizeError] = useState('');

  const MAX_SIZE = 50 * 1024 * 1024; // 50MB

  const validateAndSet = (f: File) => {
    if (f.size > MAX_SIZE) {
      setSizeError(`Файл слишком большой (${(f.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} МБ). Максимум — 50 МБ.`);
      setFile(null);
    } else {
      setSizeError('');
      setFile(f);
    }
  };

  const handleDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(false);
    const d = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
    if (d) validateAndSet(d);
  }, []);

  const handleStart = () => { if (file) onUpload(file); };

  return (
    <div className="py-10 space-y-10">
      {/* Hero */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} className="text-center">
        <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-3 leading-tight">
          Превратите любую лекцию<br />в готовый учебный материал
        </h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-lg mb-8">
          Три AI-агента параллельно создают конспект, карточки и тест — за 15 секунд
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
            <span className="text-xl">📝</span>
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Структурированный конспект</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
            <span className="text-xl">🃏</span>
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Карточки для запоминания</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
            <span className="text-xl">✅</span>
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Проверочный тест</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Шаг 1: Загрузка файла */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.1 }}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm flex items-center justify-center font-bold">1</span>
          <span className="font-medium text-slate-800">Выберите файл</span>
        </div>

        {file ? (
          <div className="card p-5 flex items-center gap-4">
            <FileText className="w-10 h-10 text-brand-500 shrink-0" />
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-slate-900 font-medium truncate text-lg">{file.name}</p>
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">{(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB</p>
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">~{Math.round(file.size / 6)} слов · ~{Math.round(Math.round(file.size / 6) / 200)} мин чтения</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => setFile(null)}
              className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition">
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div
            onDragOver={e => { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); }}
            onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
            onDrop={handleDrop}
            onClick={() => document.getElementById('fi')?.click()}
            className={'card p-14 text-center cursor-pointer transition border-2 border-dashed ' +
              (dragOver ? 'border-brand-400 bg-brand-50' : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-brand-300 hover:bg-slate-50')}
          >
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-brand-50 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Upload className="w-7 h-7 text-brand-500" />
            </div>
            <p className="text-slate-800 font-semibold text-lg mb-1">Перетащите файл сюда</p>
            <p className="text-slate-400">или нажмите для выбора</p>
            <p className="text-slate-300 text-sm mt-3">Поддерживаются PDF, DOCX, TXT, MD — до 50 МБ</p>
          </div>
        )}
        <input id="fi" type="file" className="hidden" accept=".pdf,.docx,.txt,.md,.doc"
          onChange={e => { if (e.target.files?.[0]) validateAndSet(e.target.files[0]); }} />
        {sizeError && (
          <p className="mt-3 text-center text-sm text-rose-600">{sizeError}</p>
        )}
        <div className="mt-3 text-center">
          <button onClick={onDemo}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-500 border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:border-slate-300 transition font-medium">
            ▶ Демо без файла
          </button>
        </div>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Шаг 2: Настройки */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.2 }}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm flex items-center justify-center font-bold">2</span>
          <span className="font-medium text-slate-800">Настройте параметры</span>
        </div>
        <div className="card p-6">
          <SettingsPanel settings={settings} onChange={onSettingsChange} />
        </div>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Кнопка запуска */}
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} transition={{ delay: 0.3 }}>
        <button
          onClick={handleStart}
          disabled={!file}
          className={'w-full h-14 rounded-xl font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition text-base ' +
            (file
              ? 'bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white cursor-pointer shadow-sm hover:shadow-md active:scale-[1.01]'
              : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-400 cursor-not-allowed')}
        >
          <Zap className="w-5 h-5" />
          {file ? 'Запустить анализ' : 'Сначала выберите файл'}
        </button>
      </motion.div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Loader2, CheckCircle2, Clock, FileText, HelpCircle, BookOpen, Sparkles, Mic, Compass } from 'lucide-react';
import { AgentStatus } from '../types';

function UnifiedProgressBar({ value }: { value: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-xl mx-auto">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
        <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">Общий прогресс анализа</span>
        <span className="text-sm font-black text-indigo-600 tabular-nums">{value}%</span>
      </div>
      <div className="h-3 w-full bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden p-[2px] border border-slate-200 shadow-inner">
        <motion.div
          className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 via-violet-500 to-emerald-500 rounded-full shadow-sm"
          initial={{ width: '0%' }}
          animate={{ width: `${value}%` }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: 'easeOut' }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AgentTimer({ status }: { status: string }) {
  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
  const startRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const frameRef = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'running') {
      startRef.current = Date.now();
      const tick = () => {
        if (startRef.current) setElapsed((Date.now() - startRef.current) / 1000);
        frameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      };
      frameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frameRef.current);
    } else {
      cancelAnimationFrame(frameRef.current);
    }
  }, [status]);

  if (status === 'idle') return null;
  return status === 'done'
    ? <span className="text-xs text-emerald-600 font-bold tabular-nums">{elapsed.toFixed(1)}с</span>
    : <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold tabular-nums">{elapsed.toFixed(1)}с</span>;
}

        running: 'Создаем проверочные вопросы...',
        done: 'Тест с разбором ошибок готов'
      }
    },
    cheatsheet: {
      label: 'Шпаргалка',
      icon: Sparkles,
      color: 'text-rose-600',
      bgColor: 'bg-rose-50',
      borderColor: 'border-rose-200',
      glowColor: 'shadow-rose-500/5',
      desc: {
        idle: 'Ожидание сжатия материала...',
        running: 'Упаковываем сложные термины в ёмкие формулировки...',

      }
    },
    podcast: {
      label: 'Аудио-подкаст',
      icon: Mic,
      color: 'text-violet-600',
      bgColor: 'bg-violet-50',
      borderColor: 'border-violet-200',
      glowColor: 'shadow-violet-500/5',
      desc: {
        idle: 'Ожидание генерации сценария...',
        running: 'Создаем текстовый скрипт для аудио-подкаста...',
        done: 'Подкаст озвучен и готов к прослушиванию'
      }
    },
    missions: {
      label: 'Учебные квесты',
      icon: Compass,
      color: 'text-cyan-600',
      bgColor: 'bg-cyan-50',
      borderColor: 'border-cyan-200',
      glowColor: 'shadow-cyan-500/5',
      desc: {
        idle: 'Ожидание построения целей...',
        running: 'Формируем индивидуальные миссии для лекции...',
        done: 'Квесты сгенерированы'
      }
    }
  };

  const doneCount = agents.filter(a => a.status === 'done').length;
  // Рассчитываем общий процент выполнения на основе фазы и количества выполненных агентов
  let pct = 0;
  if (phase === 'extracting') {
    pct = 10;
  } else {
    // В процессе анализа: 10% базовые за извлечение + пропорционально выполненным агентам (до 95%)
    pct = 10 + Math.round((doneCount / agents.length) * 85);
  }

  return (
    <div className="py-10 relative">
      {isDemo && (
        <span className="absolute top-0 right-0 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-xs font-black border border-amber-200 tracking-wide uppercase shadow-sm">

        </h2>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm font-medium max-w-md mb-8 leading-relaxed">
          {phase === 'extracting'
            ? 'Пожалуйста, подождите. Мы считываем содержимое и подготавливаем данные для ИИ-агентов.'
            : `Нейросетевые агенты работают параллельно. Завершено: ${doneCount} из ${agents.length}`}
        </p>

        <UnifiedProgressBar value={pct} />
      </div>

      {/* Список Агентов без гонки */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        {agents.map((agent, i) => {
          const config = agentConfigs[agent.id] || {
            label: agent.label,
            icon: FileText,
            color: 'text-indigo-600',
            bgColor: 'bg-indigo-50',
            borderColor: 'border-indigo-200',
            glowColor: 'shadow-indigo-500/5',
            desc: { idle: 'В очереди...', running: 'Обработка...', done: 'Готово' }
          };

          const IconComponent = config.icon;

          return (
            <motion.div
              key={agent.id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: i * 0.08 }}
              className={`p-5 rounded-2xl border bg-white flex flex-col justify-between transition-all duration-300 shadow-sm ${config.glowColor} ${
                agent.status === 'running'
                  ? `border-indigo-300 ring-4 ring-indigo-50`
                  : agent.status === 'done'
                  ? `border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/30`
                  : `border-slate-200 opacity-80`
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 mb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border ${config.borderColor} ${config.bgColor} ${config.color} shadow-sm`}>
                    <IconComponent className="w-5 h-5 animate-pulse-slow" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">{config.label}</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mt-0.5 min-h-[16px]">
                      {config.desc[agent.status]}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 self-start pt-1">
                  {agent.status === 'running' && (
                    <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-500 animate-spin" />
                  )}
                  {agent.status === 'done' && (
                     <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  {agent.status === 'idle' && (
                    <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  <AgentTimer status={agent.status} />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Индикатор прогресса конкретного агента */}
              <div className="w-full bg-slate-100 h-1.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden mt-3 shadow-inner">
                {agent.status === 'idle' && (
                  <div className="h-full bg-slate-200 w-0" />
                )}
                {agent.status === 'running' && (
                  <motion.div
                    className={`h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-violet-500 rounded-full`}
                    initial={{ width: '0%' }}
                    animate={{ width: '90%' }}
                    transition={{ duration: 12, ease: 'easeOut' }}
                  />
                )}
                {agent.status === 'done' && (
                  <motion.div
                    className="h-full bg-emerald-500 rounded-full shadow-sm"
                    initial={{ width: '90%' }}
                    animate={{ width: '100%' }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
                  />
                )}
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Copy, Download, RotateCcw, FileText, Layers, HelpCircle, Hash, Clock, Cpu, Gamepad2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { AnalysisResults } from '../types';
import SummaryView from './SummaryView';
import FlashcardsView from './FlashcardsView';
import QuizView from './QuizView';

type Tab = 'summary' | 'flashcards' | 'quiz' | 'missions';

function buildHtmlExport(results: AnalysisResults, sourceFileName?: string): string {
  const date = new Date().toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
  const time = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  const summaryContent = results.summarizer?.content || results.summarizer?.raw || '<p>Нет данных</p>';
  const flashcards = results.flashcards?.flashcards || [];
  const quiz = results.quiz?.quiz || [];

  const flashcardsHtml = flashcards.map((c: any, i: number) =>
    `<div style="margin-bottom:16px;padding:16px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px">
      <p style="font-weight:600;margin:0 0 8px 0">Вопрос ${i+1}: ${c.question}</p>
      <p style="margin:0;color:#475569;border-top:1px solid #f1f5f9;padding-top:8px">${c.answer}</p>
    </div>`
  ).join('\n');

  const quizHtml = quiz.map((q: any, i: number) => {
    const options = (q.options || []).map((o: string) => {
      const isCorrect = o.startsWith(q.correct);
      return `<li style="padding:6px 12px;margin:4px 0;border-radius:6px;${isCorrect ? 'background:#d1fae5;font-weight:600' : 'background:#f8fafc'}">${o}${isCorrect ? ' ✓' : ''}</li>`;
    }).join('\n');
    return `<div style="margin-bottom:16px;padding:16px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px">
      <p style="font-weight:600;margin:0 0 8px 0">${i+1}. ${q.question}</p>
      <ul style="list-style:none;padding:0;margin:0">${options}</ul>
      ${q.explanation ? `<p style="margin:8px 0 0;color:#64748b;font-style:italic;border-top:1px solid #f1f5f9;padding-top:8px">💡 ${q.explanation}</p>` : ''}
    </div>`;
  }).join('\n');

  return `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ru">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<title>StudyForge — Анализ материала</title>
<style>
  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
  body { font-family: 'Segoe UI', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif; color: #1e293b; line-height: 1.6; max-width: 800px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 40px 24px; background: #fff; }
  h1 { font-size: 24px; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px; color: #0f172a; }
  .meta { color: #94a3b8; font-size: 13px; margin-bottom: 32px; }
  h2 { font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; margin: 32px 0 16px; padding-bottom: 8px; border-bottom: 2px solid #4F46E5; color: #1e293b; }
  .section { margin-bottom: 24px; }
  @media print {
    body { padding: 0; }
    h2 { page-break-after: avoid; }
    .section { page-break-inside: avoid; }
  }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<h1>StudyForge — Анализ материала</h1>
<p class="meta">${sourceFileName || 'Демо'} · ${date} ${time}</p>

<div class="section">
<h2>📝 Конспект</h2>
<div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:15px;color:#334155;line-height:1.7">${summaryContent}</div>
</div>

<div class="section">
<h2>🃏 Карточки (${flashcards.length})</h2>
${flashcardsHtml || '<p style="color:#94a3b8">Нет данных</p>'}
</div>

<div class="section">
<h2>❓ Тест (${quiz.length})</h2>
${quizHtml || '<p style="color:#94a3b8">Нет данных</p>'}
</div>

</body>
</html>`;
}

export default function ResultsPanel({ results, onReset, textLength, analysisDuration, sourceFileName }: {
  results: AnalysisResults; onReset: () => void; textLength?: number; analysisDuration?: number; sourceFileName?: string;
}) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('summary');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const flashcardCount = results.flashcards?.flashcards?.length || 0;
  const quizCount = results.quiz?.quiz?.length || 0;

  const tabs: { id: Tab; label: string; icon: JSX.Element; count?: number }[] = [
    { id: 'summary', label: 'Конспект', icon: <FileText className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'flashcards', label: 'Карточки', icon: <Layers className="w-4 h-4" />, count: flashcardCount },
    { id: 'quiz', label: 'Тест', icon: <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4" />, count: quizCount },
    { id: 'missions', label: 'Миссии', icon: <Gamepad2 className="w-4 h-4" />, count: results.missions?.missions?.length || 0 },
  ];

  const getContent = () => {
    if (tab === 'summary') return results.summarizer?.content || results.summarizer?.raw || '';
    if (tab === 'flashcards') return JSON.stringify(results.flashcards?.flashcards || results.flashcards, null, 2);
    if (tab === 'missions') return JSON.stringify(results.missions?.missions || results.missions, null, 2);
    return JSON.stringify(results.quiz?.quiz || results.quiz, null, 2);
  };

  const handleCopy = () => { navigator.clipboard.writeText(getContent()); setCopied(true); setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000); };

  const handleDownload = () => {
    const baseName = (sourceFileName || 'demo').replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '');
    const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const html = buildHtmlExport(results, sourceFileName);
    const b = new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html' });
    const u = URL.createObjectURL(b);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = u;
    a.download = `studyforge_${baseName}_${dateStr}.html`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
  };

  return (
    <div className="py-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Результаты</h2>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button onClick={handleCopy}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 transition border border-slate-200">
            <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />{copied ? 'Скопировано' : 'Копировать'}
          </button>
          <button onClick={handleDownload}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 transition border border-slate-200">
            <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Скачать HTML
          </button>
          <button onClick={onReset}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-brand-600 bg-brand-50 hover:bg-brand-100 transition">
            <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Новый анализ
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Статистика */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-6">
        <div className="card p-3 flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Hash className="w-4 h-4 text-brand-500" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Символов</p>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">{textLength?.toLocaleString() || '—'}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{textLength ? `~${Math.round(textLength / 5)} слов` : '—'}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="card p-3 flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Время</p>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">{analysisDuration ? `${analysisDuration} сек` : '—'}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="card p-3 flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Cpu className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Агенты</p>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">3 параллельно</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Pill-вкладки */}
      <div className="flex gap-1 mb-6 bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl w-fit">
        {tabs.map(t => (
          <button key={t.id} onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
            className={'flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm transition font-medium ' +
              (tab === t.id
                ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-white')}>
            {t.icon}
            {t.label}
            {t.count !== undefined && (
              <span className={'ml-0.5 text-xs ' + (tab === t.id ? 'text-brand-200' : 'text-slate-400')}>
                ({t.count})
              </span>
            )}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <motion.div key={tab} initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} className="min-h-[300px]">
        {tab === 'summary' && <div className="card p-6"><SummaryView data={results.summarizer} /></div>}
        {tab === 'flashcards' && <FlashcardsView data={results.flashcards} />}
        {tab === 'quiz' && <QuizView data={results.quiz} />}
        {tab === 'missions' && (
          <div className="card p-10 flex flex-col items-center text-center justify-center min-h-[300px]">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
              <Gamepad2 className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-600" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Сюжетные миссии готовы!</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 mb-6 max-w-sm">
              ИИ создал для вас персональный мини-квест на основе этого материала. Сыграем?
            </p>
            <a href="/missions" className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-lg font-medium transition shadow-sm inline-flex items-center gap-2">
              <Gamepad2 className="w-5 h-5" />
              Начать игру
            </a>
          </div>
        )}
      </motion.div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/SummaryView.tsx
================================================================================
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';

function SectionBlock({ icon, title, children }: { icon: string; title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
        <span className="text-lg">{icon}</span>
        <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{title}</h3>
      </div>
      <div className="pl-7 text-slate-700 text-[15px] leading-relaxed">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function SummaryView({ data }: { data: any }) {
  const content = data?.content || data?.raw || '';
  if (!content) return <p className="text-slate-400">Нет данных</p>;

  const sections = content.split(/(?=^## )/gm).filter(Boolean);

  const iconMap: Record<string, string> = {
    '📌': '📌', '🔑': '🔑', '📖': '📖', '📚': '📚', '💡': '💡',
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      {sections.map((sec: string, i: number) => {
        const match = sec.match(/^## (.+)/);
        if (!match) return (
          <div key={i} className="prose prose-slate max-w-none text-slate-700">
            <ReactMarkdown>{sec}</ReactMarkdown>
          </div>
        );
        const title = match[1].trim();
        const body = sec.replace(/^## .+\n?/, '');
        const icon = Object.keys(iconMap).find(k => title.includes(k)) || '📄';
        const cleanTitle = title.replace(/[📌🔑📖📚💡]/g, '').trim();

        return (
          <SectionBlock key={i} icon={icon} title={cleanTitle}>
            <div className="prose prose-slate prose-sm max-w-none">
              <ReactMarkdown>{body}</ReactMarkdown>
            </div>
          </SectionBlock>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/FlashcardsView.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Eye, EyeOff, GraduationCap, ArrowLeft, Check, X, Copy } from 'lucide-react';

export default function FlashcardsView({ data }: { data: any }) {
  const cards = data?.flashcards;

  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  const [studyMode, setStudyMode] = useState(false);
  const [studyIdx, setStudyIdx] = useState(0);
  const [studyRevealed, setStudyRevealed] = useState(false);
  const [studyResults, setStudyResults] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  const [studyDone, setStudyDone] = useState(false);
  const [copiedIdx, setCopiedIdx] = useState(-1);

  const toggle = (i: number) => setRevealed({ ...revealed, [i]: !revealed[i] });
  const revealAll = () => {
    if (!cards || !Array.isArray(cards)) return;
    const r: Record<number, boolean> = {};
    cards.forEach((_: any, i: number) => { r[i] = true; });
    setRevealed(r);
  };
  const hideAll = () => setRevealed({});

  const startStudy = () => {
    setStudyMode(true);
    setStudyIdx(0);
    setStudyRevealed(false);
    setStudyResults({});
    setStudyDone(false);
  };

  const exitStudy = () => setStudyMode(false);

  const markCard = (known: boolean) => {
    if (!cards) return;
    const next = { ...studyResults, [studyIdx]: known };
    if (studyIdx + 1 < cards.length) {
      setStudyResults(next);
      setStudyIdx(studyIdx + 1);
      setStudyRevealed(false);
    } else {
      setStudyResults(next);
      setStudyDone(true);
    }
  };

  if (!cards || !Array.isArray(cards)) return <p className="text-slate-400">Нет данных</p>;

  // Study mode
  if (studyMode) {
    const knownCount = Object.values(studyResults).filter(Boolean).length;

    if (studyDone) {
      return (
        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          className="card p-8 text-center">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
            <GraduationCap className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-500" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">
            Результат: {knownCount} из {cards.length}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 mb-6">
            {knownCount === cards.length ? 'Отлично! Вы знаете все карточки!' :
             knownCount >= cards.length / 2 ? 'Неплохо, но есть что подтянуть.' :
             'Стоит повторить материал ещё раз.'}
          </p>
          <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
            <button onClick={startStudy}
              className="px-5 py-2 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
              Пройти снова
            </button>
            <button onClick={exitStudy}
              className="px-5 py-2 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
              К списку
            </button>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      );
    }

    const card = cards[studyIdx];
    return (
      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <button onClick={exitStudy}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 transition">
            <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />К списку
          </button>
          <span className="text-sm text-slate-400">{studyIdx + 1} / {cards.length}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="card p-8 min-h-[260px] flex flex-col justify-center">
          <p className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 text-center mb-6">{card.question}</p>
          <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
            {studyRevealed ? (
              <motion.p key="answer" initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                className="text-slate-700 text-center border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
                {card.answer}
              </motion.p>
            ) : (
              <motion.button key="reveal" initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                onClick={() => setStudyRevealed(true)}
                className="mx-auto px-5 py-2 text-sm text-brand-600 border border-brand-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-brand-50 transition">
                Показать ответ
              </motion.button>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>

        {studyRevealed && (
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            className="flex gap-3 mt-4">
            <button onClick={() => markCard(true)}
              className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 font-medium hover:bg-emerald-100 transition">
              <Check className="w-5 h-5" />Знаю
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => markCard(false)}
              className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 font-medium hover:bg-rose-100 transition">
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />Ещё раз
            </button>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </motion.div>
    );
  }

  // Normal mode
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
        <button onClick={revealAll} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 transition border border-slate-200">
          <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Показать все
        </button>
        <button onClick={hideAll} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 transition border border-slate-200">
          <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Скрыть все
        </button>
        <button onClick={startStudy}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-brand-600 bg-brand-50 hover:bg-brand-100 transition border border-brand-200">
          <GraduationCap className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Начать обучение
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {cards.map((c: any, i: number) => (
          <motion.div key={i} initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} transition={{ delay: i * 0.05 }}
            className="card p-4 cursor-pointer hover:border-slate-300 transition group"
            onClick={() => toggle(i)}>
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
              <p className="font-medium text-slate-900 mb-2">Q{i+1}: {c.question}</p>
              <button onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); navigator.clipboard.writeText(`Q: ${c.question}\nA: ${c.answer}`); setCopiedIdx(i); setTimeout(() => setCopiedIdx(-1), 1500); }}
                className="shrink-0 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition p-1 text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-500">
                {copiedIdx === i ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              </button>
            </div>
            {revealed[i] ? (
              <p className="text-slate-700 text-sm border-t border-slate-100 pt-2">{c.answer}</p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm border-t border-slate-100 pt-2 italic">Нажмите, чтобы увидеть ответ</p>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/QuizView.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { CheckCircle2, XCircle, RotateCcw, Maximize2, Minimize2 } from 'lucide-react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';

export default function QuizView({ data }: { data: any }) {
  const quiz = data?.quiz;

  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  // Fullscreen mode state (separate from normal)
  const [fsOpen, setFsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [fsIdx, setFsIdx] = useState(0);
  const [fsAnswers, setFsAnswers] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [fsSubmitted, setFsSubmitted] = useState(false);

  const handleSelect = (qi: number, option: string) => {
    if (submitted) return;
    setAnswers({ ...answers, [qi]: option });
  };

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    setSubmitted(true);
    const score = quiz.filter((q: any, i: number) => answers[i]?.startsWith(q.correct)).length;
    if (score === quiz.length) {
      confetti({ particleCount: 120, spread: 80, origin: { y: 0.6 }, colors: ['#4F46E5', '#10b981', '#f59e0b'] });
    }
  };
  const handleRetry = () => { setAnswers({}); setSubmitted(false); };

  if (!quiz || !Array.isArray(quiz)) {
    return (
      <div className="card p-6 bg-slate-50 border-slate-200">
        <p className="text-slate-500 mb-2 font-medium">Тест не удалось сгенерировать корректно</p>
        {data?.error && <p className="text-rose-500 text-xs mb-3">Ошибка: {data.error}</p>}
        {data?.raw && (
          <details className="text-xs">
            <summary className="text-indigo-600 cursor-pointer hover:underline mb-2">Показать сырой ответ AI</summary>
            <pre className="p-3 bg-slate-900 text-slate-300 rounded overflow-x-auto whitespace-pre-wrap max-h-64">
              {data.raw}
            </pre>
          </details>
        )}
        {!data?.raw && !data?.error && <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">Нет данных для отображения</p>}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const correct = quiz.filter((q: any, i: number) => answers[i]?.startsWith(q.correct)).length;
  const total = quiz.length;
  const pct = Math.round((correct / total) * 100);

  const gradeInfo = pct >= 90 ? { label: 'Отлично', color: 'text-emerald-600', bg: 'bg-emerald-50' }
    : pct >= 70 ? { label: 'Хорошо', color: 'text-brand-600', bg: 'bg-brand-50' }
    : pct >= 50 ? { label: 'Удовлетворительно', color: 'text-amber-600', bg: 'bg-amber-50' }
    : { label: 'Нужно подтянуть', color: 'text-rose-600', bg: 'bg-rose-50' };

  // Fullscreen mode
  if (fsOpen && quiz) {
    const fsQ = quiz[fsIdx];
    const fsTotal = quiz.length;
    const fsCorrect = quiz.filter((q: any, i: number) => fsAnswers[i]?.startsWith(q.correct)).length;

    const fsSelect = (opt: string) => {
      if (fsSubmitted) return;
      setFsAnswers({ ...fsAnswers, [fsIdx]: opt });
    };

    const fsNext = () => {
      if (fsIdx + 1 < fsTotal) setFsIdx(fsIdx + 1);
      else setFsSubmitted(true);
    };

    const fsExit = () => { setFsOpen(false); setFsIdx(0); setFsAnswers({}); setFsSubmitted(false); };

    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900 overflow-y-auto">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-8 py-4 border-b border-slate-700">
          <span className="text-white font-semibold text-lg">StudyForge — Тест для аудитории</span>
          <button onClick={fsExit} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-white/70 hover:text-white transition text-sm">
            <Minimize2 className="w-4 h-4" />Выйти
          </button>
        </div>

        {!fsSubmitted ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[calc(100vh-64px)] px-8 py-12">
            <p className="text-slate-400 text-2xl mb-6">Вопрос {fsIdx + 1} / {fsTotal}</p>
            <p className="text-white text-3xl font-bold text-center max-w-3xl mx-auto mb-12">{fsQ.question}</p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 max-w-3xl w-full">
              {fsQ.options?.map((opt: string, j: number) => {
                const selected = fsAnswers[fsIdx] === opt;
                return (
                  <div key={j} onClick={() => fsSelect(opt)}
                    className={'rounded-2xl p-6 text-xl cursor-pointer transition border ' +
                      (selected ? 'bg-brand-600 border-brand-500 text-white' : 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-white hover:bg-slate-700 hover:border-brand-500')}>
                    {opt}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            {fsAnswers[fsIdx] && (
              <motion.button initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                onClick={fsNext}
                className="mt-10 px-8 py-3 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white rounded-xl text-lg font-medium transition">
                {fsIdx + 1 < fsTotal ? 'Следующий вопрос →' : 'Завершить тест'}
              </motion.button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center px-8 py-12">
            <p className="text-4xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Правильных ответов: {fsCorrect} из {fsTotal}</p>
            <p className="text-slate-400 text-lg mb-10">{Math.round((fsCorrect / fsTotal) * 100)}%</p>
            <div className="max-w-3xl w-full space-y-4">
              {quiz.map((q: any, i: number) => {
                const isCorrect = fsAnswers[i]?.startsWith(q.correct);
                return (
                  <div key={i} className={'rounded-xl p-5 border ' + (isCorrect ? 'bg-emerald-900/30 border-emerald-700' : 'bg-rose-900/20 border-rose-800')}>
                    <p className="text-white font-medium mb-2">{i + 1}. {q.question}</p>
                    <p className={isCorrect ? 'text-emerald-400 text-sm' : 'text-rose-400 text-sm'}>
                      {isCorrect ? '✓ Верно' : `✗ Правильный ответ: ${q.correct}) ${q.options?.find((o: string) => o.startsWith(q.correct))?.replace(/^[A-D]\)\s*/, '') || q.correct}`}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <button onClick={fsExit}
              className="mt-8 px-6 py-2.5 bg-white text-slate-900 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-slate-100 transition">
              Закрыть
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      {!submitted && (
        <button onClick={() => setFsOpen(true)}
          className="absolute top-0 right-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-brand-600 border border-brand-200 hover:bg-brand-50 transition">
          <Maximize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Показать аудитории
        </button>
      )}

      {submitted && (
        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          className={'card p-5 mb-6 flex items-center justify-between ' + gradeInfo.bg}>
          <div>
            <p className={'font-bold text-lg ' + gradeInfo.color}>{correct} из {total} — {gradeInfo.label}</p>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">{pct}% правильных ответов</p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={handleRetry}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 transition border border-slate-200">
            <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />Пройти снова
          </button>
        </motion.div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-4">
        {quiz.map((q: any, i: number) => {
          const selected = answers[i];
          const isCorrect = selected?.startsWith(q.correct);
          const isWrong = submitted && selected && !isCorrect;

          return (
            <motion.div key={i} initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: i * 0.05 }}
              className="card p-5">
              <p className="font-medium text-slate-900 mb-3">{i+1}. {q.question}</p>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {q.options?.map((opt: string, j: number) => {
                  const isSelected = selected === opt;
                  const isCorrectOpt = submitted && opt.startsWith(q.correct);
                  const isWrongOpt = submitted && isSelected && !isCorrect;

                  let cls = 'p-3 rounded-lg text-sm cursor-pointer transition border ';
                  if (!submitted) {
                    cls += isSelected ? 'bg-brand-50 border-brand-300 text-slate-900' : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:border-slate-300';
                  } else if (isCorrectOpt) {
                    cls += 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800';
                  } else if (isWrongOpt) {
                    cls += 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-300 text-rose-800 line-through';
                  } else {
                    cls += 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-100 text-slate-400';
                  }

                  return (
                    <div key={j} className={'flex items-center gap-2 ' + cls}
                      onClick={() => handleSelect(i, opt)}>
                      {isCorrectOpt && <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" />}
                      {isWrongOpt && <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />}
                      <span>{opt}</span>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              {submitted && q.explanation && (
                <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-500 italic border-t border-slate-100 pt-3">💡 {q.explanation}</p>
              )}
            </motion.div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {!submitted && (
        <div className="mt-6 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-400 mb-2">
            Отвечено {Object.keys(answers).length} из {total} вопросов
          </p>
          {Object.keys(answers).length === total && (
            <button onClick={handleSubmit}
              className="px-8 py-2.5 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 text-white rounded-lg font-medium transition">
              Проверить ответы
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FileText, X, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { HistoryItem } from '../types';

interface Props {
  items: HistoryItem[];
  onRestore: (item: HistoryItem) => void;
  onRemove: (id: string) => void;
  onClear: () => void;
}

export default function HistoryPanel({ items, onRestore, onRemove, onClear }: Props) {
  if (items.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.35 }}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
        <span className="font-medium text-slate-800">История анализов</span>
        <button onClick={onClear}
          className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-500 transition">
          <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />Очистить
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        {items.map(item => (
          <div key={item.id} className="card p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
            <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-brand-500 shrink-0" />
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 truncate">{item.fileName}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{item.date} · {item.textLength.toLocaleString()} символов · {item.duration} сек</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => onRestore(item)}
              className="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 border border-brand-200 hover:bg-brand-50 transition">
              Открыть
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => onRemove(item.id)}
              className="p-1 text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-500 transition">
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx
================================================================================
import { AnalysisSettings } from '../types';

interface Props {
  settings: AnalysisSettings;
  onChange: (s: AnalysisSettings) => void;
}

const detailLabels: Record<string, string> = { brief: 'Краткий', medium: 'Средний', detailed: 'Подробный' };
const diffLabels: Record<string, string> = { easy: 'Лёгкий', medium: 'Средний', hard: 'Сложный', mixed: 'Смешанный' };
const typeLabels: Record<string, string> = { multiple: 'Выбор ответа', truefalse: 'Верно / Неверно' };

function SegmentedControl({ options, value, onChange }: { options: { key: string; label: string }[]; value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex bg-slate-100 rounded-lg p-0.5">
      {options.map(o => (
        <button key={o.key} onClick={() => onChange(o.key)}
          className={'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-sm transition ' + (value === o.key ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 font-medium shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700')}>
          {o.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function SettingsPanel({ settings, onChange }: Props) {
  const set = (patch: Partial<AnalysisSettings>) => onChange({ ...settings, ...patch });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-2 block">Детализация конспекта</label>
        <SegmentedControl
          options={Object.entries(detailLabels).map(([k, v]) => ({ key: k, label: v }))}
          value={settings.summaryDetail}
          onChange={v => set({ summaryDetail: v as any })}
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-2 block">Количество карточек: {settings.flashcardCount}</label>
        <input type="range" min={3} max={12} value={settings.flashcardCount}
          onChange={e => set({ flashcardCount: +e.target.value })}
          className="w-full accent-brand-600" />
        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1"><span>3</span><span>12</span></div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-2 block">Количество вопросов: {settings.quizCount}</label>
        <input type="range" min={3} max={10} value={settings.quizCount}
          onChange={e => set({ quizCount: +e.target.value })}
          className="w-full accent-brand-600" />
        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1"><span>3</span><span>10</span></div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-2 block">Сложность теста</label>
        <SegmentedControl
          options={Object.entries(diffLabels).map(([k, v]) => ({ key: k, label: v }))}
          value={settings.quizDifficulty}
          onChange={v => set({ quizDifficulty: v as any })}
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-2 block">Тип вопросов</label>
        <SegmentedControl
          options={Object.entries(typeLabels).map(([k, v]) => ({ key: k, label: v }))}
          value={settings.quizType}
          onChange={v => set({ quizType: v as any })}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/ExpressPrepView.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import { Play, Pause, Square, Headphones, FileText, Sparkles, Volume2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { updateQuestProgress } from '../api/quests';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';

interface PodcastLine {
  speaker: 'Макс' | 'Софья' | string;
  text: string;
}

export default function ExpressPrepView({ summaryJson }: { summaryJson: any }) {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  // Вытаскиваем данные из составного JSON
  const cheatsheet = summaryJson?.cheatsheet || '';
  const podcastLines: PodcastLine[] = summaryJson?.podcast || [];

  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'cheatsheet' | 'podcast'>('cheatsheet');
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [currentSpeakingIndex, setCurrentSpeakingIndex] = useState(-1);
  const utteranceRef = useRef<SpeechSynthesisUtterance | null>(null);
  const currentIndexRef = useRef(0);

  // Очистка при размонтировании
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (window.speechSynthesis) {
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const stopPodcast = () => {
    if (window.speechSynthesis) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    }
    setIsPlaying(false);
    setCurrentSpeakingIndex(-1);
    currentIndexRef.current = 0;
  };

  const playPodcast = () => {
    if (!window.speechSynthesis) {
      alert('Синтез речи не поддерживается вашим браузером.');
      return;
    }

    if (isPlaying) {
      // Пауза
      window.speechSynthesis.pause();
      setIsPlaying(false);
      return;
    }

    if (window.speechSynthesis.paused && currentSpeakingIndex !== -1) {
      // Возобновление
      window.speechSynthesis.resume();
      setIsPlaying(true);
      return;
    }

    // Запуск с начала или с текущего индекса
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    
    const speakNext = () => {
      if (currentIndexRef.current >= podcastLines.length) {
        setIsPlaying(false);
        setCurrentSpeakingIndex(-1);
        currentIndexRef.current = 0;
        return;
      }

      const line = podcastLines[currentIndexRef.current];
      setCurrentSpeakingIndex(currentIndexRef.current);

      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(line.text);
      utterance.lang = 'ru-RU';
      utteranceRef.current = utterance;

      // Настройка голосов и тембра
      const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
      const ruVoices = voices.filter(v => v.lang.startsWith('ru'));

      if (line.speaker === 'Макс') {
        const maleVoice = ruVoices.find(v => 
          v.name.toLowerCase().includes('male') || 
          v.name.toLowerCase().includes('dmitry') || 
          v.name.toLowerCase().includes('pavel')
        );
        if (maleVoice) utterance.voice = maleVoice;
        utterance.pitch = 0.95; // Более низкий тембр для Макса
        utterance.rate = 1.0;
      } else {
        const femaleVoice = ruVoices.find(v => 
          v.name.toLowerCase().includes('female') || 
          v.name.toLowerCase().includes('milena') || 
          v.name.toLowerCase().includes('katya')
        );
        if (femaleVoice) utterance.voice = femaleVoice;
        utterance.pitch = 1.22; // Более высокий и звонкий для Софьи
        utterance.rate = 1.05;  // Чуть быстрее
      }

      utterance.onend = () => {
        currentIndexRef.current++;
        speakNext();
      };

      utterance.onerror = (e) => {
        console.error('Speech synthesis error:', e);
        setIsPlaying(false);
        setCurrentSpeakingIndex(-1);
      };

      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    };

    setIsPlaying(true);
    speakNext();
    if (user?.id) updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'podcast', 1);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Переключатель вкладок экспресс-подготовки */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl w-full max-w-sm mx-auto sm:mx-0">
        <button
          onClick={() => setTab('cheatsheet')}
          className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
            tab === 'cheatsheet'
              ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white shadow-sm'
              : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-white'
          }`}
        >
          <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> ⚡ Шпаргалка за 5 минут
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => setTab('podcast')}
          className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
            tab === 'podcast'
              ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white shadow-sm'
              : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-white'
          }`}
        >
          <Headphones className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> 🎙️ AI-Подкаст
        </button>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ВКЛАДКА 1: Шпаргалка */}
        {tab === 'cheatsheet' && (
          <motion.div
            key="cheatsheet"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
            className="space-y-4"
          >
            {!cheatsheet ? (
              <div className="card p-6 text-center text-slate-400 italic text-sm">
                Для этой лекции шпаргалка еще не создана. Перезапустите анализ файла.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="card p-6 md:p-8 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-50/30 to-rose-50/10 border-amber-100 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden">
                <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-48 h-48 bg-amber-500/5 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
                <div className="prose prose-slate max-w-none text-slate-700 prose-sm cheatsheet-style">
                  <ReactMarkdown>{cheatsheet}</ReactMarkdown>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ВКЛАДКА 2: Аудио-подкаст */}
        {tab === 'podcast' && (
          <motion.div
            key="podcast"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            {podcastLines.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="card p-6 text-center text-slate-400 italic text-sm">
                Для этой лекции аудио-подкаст еще не создан. Перезапустите анализ файла.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                {/* Аудио-плеер */}
                <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6 shadow-xl border border-slate-800 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-rose-500/20 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 border border-rose-500/30">
                      <Headphones className="w-6 h-6 text-rose-400" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-center md:text-left">
                      <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-200 flex items-center gap-1.5 justify-center md:justify-start">
                        AI-Подкаст: {summaryJson?.title || 'Разбор лекции'}
                        <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                      </h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Слушайте простое объяснение темы по пути на экзамен</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      onClick={playPodcast}
                      className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-xs transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-lg shadow-rose-950"
                    >
                      {isPlaying ? <Pause className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Play className="w-4 h-4" />}
                      {isPlaying ? 'Пауза' : currentSpeakingIndex !== -1 ? 'Продолжить' : 'Слушать подкаст'}
                    </button>
                    {currentSpeakingIndex !== -1 && (
                      <button
                        onClick={stopPodcast}
                        className="p-2.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-xl transition border border-slate-700"
                        title="Остановить"
                      >
                        <Square className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Бегущий диалог подкаста */}
                <div className="space-y-4 max-h-[500px] overflow-y-auto pr-2 custom-scrollbar">
                  {podcastLines.map((line, idx) => {
                    const isSpeaking = currentSpeakingIndex === idx;
                    const isMax = line.speaker === 'Макс';

                    return (
                      <div
                        key={idx}
                        className={`flex ${isMax ? 'justify-start' : 'justify-end'} transition-all duration-300`}
                      >
                        <div
                          className={`max-w-[85%] sm:max-w-[70%] p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border transition-all duration-300 ${
                            isSpeaking
                              ? 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-200 ring-2 ring-rose-500/20 scale-[1.01]'
                              : isMax
                              ? 'bg-white border-slate-100 text-slate-800 rounded-tl-none'
                              : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-tr-none'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                            <span className={`text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider ${isMax ? 'text-rose-600' : 'text-indigo-600'}`}>
                              {line.speaker}
                            </span>
                            {isSpeaking && (
                              <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-500 animate-bounce" />
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm leading-relaxed font-medium">{line.text}</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/AchievementToast.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Trophy, Star, Flame, Zap, BookOpen, Award, Brain, CheckCircle2, X } from 'lucide-react';

export interface Achievement {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  icon: string;
  xp: number;
}

interface Props {
  achievement: Achievement | null;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const ICON_MAP: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  trophy: Trophy,
  star: Star,
  flame: Flame,
  zap: Zap,
  book: BookOpen,
  award: Award,
  brain: Brain,
  check: CheckCircle2,
};

export default function AchievementToast({ achievement, onClose }: Props) {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!achievement) return;
    setVisible(true);
    const t = setTimeout(() => { setVisible(false); setTimeout(onClose, 400); }, 4500);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [achievement]);

  if (!achievement) return null;

  const Icon = ICON_MAP[achievement.icon] ?? Trophy;

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {visible && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 80, scale: 0.9 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0, scale: 1 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 80, scale: 0.9 }}
          transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 300, damping: 25 }}
          className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-4 bg-slate-900 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-2xl shadow-amber-500/10 max-w-xs"
        >
          {/* Иконка достижения */}
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-400 to-amber-600 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-lg">
            <Icon className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
          </div>

          {/* Текст */}
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">
              🏆 Достижение разблокировано!
            </p>
            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{achievement.title}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 truncate">{achievement.description}</p>
            <div className="mt-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-indigo-500/20 border border-indigo-500/30 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5">
              <Zap className="w-3 h-3 text-indigo-400" />
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-indigo-300">+{achievement.xp} XP</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Кнопка закрыть */}
          <button
            onClick={() => { setVisible(false); setTimeout(onClose, 400); }}
            className="text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-400 transition shrink-0"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          {/* Прогресс-бар автозакрытия */}
          <motion.div
            className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-0.5 bg-amber-400 rounded-full"
            initial={{ scaleX: 1, originX: 0 }}
            animate={{ scaleX: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 4.5, ease: 'linear' }}
          />
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/ConfettiCelebration.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';

interface Props {
  trigger: boolean;
  type?: 'quest' | 'exam5' | 'achievement' | 'level';
}

export default function ConfettiCelebration({ trigger, type = 'quest' }: Props) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!trigger) return;

    const defaults = { startVelocity: 30, spread: 360, ticks: 60, zIndex: 9999 };

    if (type === 'level') {
      // Мощный залп для повышения уровня
      const duration = 3000;
      const end = Date.now() + duration;
      const frame = () => {
        confetti({ ...defaults, particleCount: 7, origin: { x: Math.random(), y: Math.random() * 0.6 } });
        if (Date.now() < end) requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      };
      frame();
    } else if (type === 'exam5') {
      // Двойной залп по бокам для пятёрки
      confetti({ particleCount: 100, angle: 60, spread: 55, origin: { x: 0 }, colors: ['#4f46e5', '#7c3aed', '#06b6d4'] });
      confetti({ particleCount: 100, angle: 120, spread: 55, origin: { x: 1 }, colors: ['#4f46e5', '#7c3aed', '#06b6d4'] });
    } else if (type === 'achievement') {
      confetti({ particleCount: 80, spread: 80, origin: { y: 0.6 }, colors: ['#f59e0b', '#fbbf24', '#fcd34d'] });
    } else {
      // Обычный квест — лёгкий
      confetti({ particleCount: 60, spread: 70, origin: { y: 0.6 }, colors: ['#4f46e5', '#10b981', '#f59e0b'] });
    }
  }, [trigger, type]);

  return null;
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { 
  GraduationCap, Zap, Brain, Calendar, BookOpen,
  MessageSquare, BarChart2, Sparkles, ArrowRight,
  CheckCircle, Upload, Cpu, Trophy, Network, Briefcase, Award, Heart
} from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';

export default function Landing() {

  return (

        </button>
