function Hero() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [rotateX, setRotateX] = useState(0);
  const [rotateY, setRotateY] = useState(0);
  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false);

  const handleMouseMove = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const card = e.currentTarget;
    const box = card.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = e.clientX - box.left - box.width / 2;
    const y = e.clientY - box.top - box.height / 2;
    // Максимальный угол наклона - 12 градусов
    setRotateX(-y / (box.height / 24));
    setRotateY(x / (box.width / 24));
  };

  const handleMouseLeave = () => {
    setIsHovered(false);
    setRotateX(0);
    setRotateY(0);
  };

  return (
    <section className="pt-32 pb-24 px-6 relative max-w-7xl mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-12 items-center">
      
      {/* Левая текстовая колонка */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-6 text-left space-y-6">
        {/* Академический бейдж */}
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 rounded-full text-indigo-700 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide">
          <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>Доказательная педагогика и ИИ</span>
        </div>

        <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-6xl font-black tracking-tight leading-[1.1] text-slate-900">
          Осваивайте лекции <br className="hidden md:block" />
          <span className="text-indigo-600">в 4 раза быстрее</span>
        </h1>

        <p className="text-lg text-slate-600 leading-relaxed max-w-xl">
          Автоматизируйте рутину конспектирования. StudyForge анализирует материалы, создаёт интерактивные конспекты, карточки для интервального повторения по методу Лейтнера и проводит устные экзамены.
        </p>

        {/* Кнопки действий */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-wrap pt-2">
          <button onClick={() => navigate('/register')}
            className="group flex items-center gap-2 px-8 py-4 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-bold text-base rounded-xl transition shadow-xl shadow-indigo-200 hover:shadow-indigo-300 hover:-translate-y-0.5 active:translate-y-0 duration-200">
            Начать бесплатно
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-8 py-4 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold text-base rounded-xl transition shadow-sm hover:shadow hover:-translate-y-0.5 active:translate-y-0 duration-200">
            <Zap className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600" />
            Быстрое демо
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Дополнительные преимущества */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-6 pt-4 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" /> Без привязки карт</span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" /> Сделано для вузов</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Правая колонка с интерактивной 3D сценой */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-6 flex items-center justify-center relative">
        {/* Размытый фон сцены */}
        <div className="absolute w-[400px] h-[400px] bg-indigo-200/40 rounded-full blur-[100px] pointer-events-none" />

        {/* 3D Перспектива */}
        <div 
          className="relative w-full max-w-lg aspect-[4/3] cursor-pointer scale-90 sm:scale-100 origin-center lg:mt-0"
          style={{ perspective: 1200 }}
          onMouseMove={handleMouseMove}
          onMouseEnter={() => setIsHovered(true)}
          onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
        >
          <motion.div
            className="w-full h-full relative"
            animate={{
              rotateX: isHovered ? rotateX : 0,
              rotateY: isHovered ? rotateY : 0,
              scale: isHovered ? 1.03 : 1
            }}
            transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 120, damping: 20 }}
            style={{ transformStyle: "preserve-3d" }}
          >
            {/* Нижний уровень: Тень */}
            <div 
              className="absolute inset-4 bg-indigo-900/5 rounded-3xl blur-2xl transition-all duration-300"
              style={{ transform: "translateZ(-40px)" }}
            />
            
            {/* Базовый слой: Панель Дашборда */}
            <div 
              className="absolute inset-0 bg-white border border-slate-200/80 rounded-3xl shadow-xl p-6 flex flex-col gap-4 select-none"
              style={{ transform: "translateZ(0px)" }}
            >
              {/* Хедер дашборда */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-rose-400" />
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-amber-400" />
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-400" />
                </div>
                <div className="h-4 w-32 bg-slate-100 rounded-full" />
                <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-100" />
              </div>

              {/* Тело дашборда */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-3 flex-1">
                {/* Левое меню */}
                <div className="col-span-3 space-y-2 border-r border-slate-100 pr-2">
                  <div className="h-5 bg-indigo-50 rounded-lg w-full" />
                  <div className="h-5 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg w-5/6" />
                  <div className="h-5 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg w-4/5" />
                  <div className="h-5 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg w-3/4" />
                </div>
                
                {/* Рабочая зона */}
                <div className="col-span-9 space-y-3 pl-2">
                  <div className="h-8 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-xl flex items-center px-3 justify-between">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400">Мой прогресс</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-indigo-600 font-extrabold">+120 XP</span>
                  </div>
                  {/* Фейковый график */}
                  <div className="h-24 bg-slate-50/50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 p-2 flex items-end justify-between gap-1">
                    {[30, 45, 20, 60, 80, 50, 95, 40, 70, 85].map((h, i) => (
                      <div key={i} className="bg-indigo-100 hover:bg-indigo-300 transition-colors w-full rounded-t-sm" style={{ height: `${h}%` }} />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Парящий слой 1: 📝 Конспект (Слева сверху) */}
            <motion.div 
              className="absolute top-[-10%] left-0 md:left-[-10%] w-[55%] md:w-[50%] bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-md border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-lg p-4 flex flex-col gap-2"
              style={{ transform: "translateZ(50px)" }}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 flex items-center gap-1">📝 Конспект</span>
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-800 line-clamp-1">Тема: Дифференциальные уравнения</p>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="h-1.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full w-full" />
                <div className="h-1.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full w-5/6" />
                <div className="h-1.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full w-2/3" />
              </div>
            </motion.div>

            {/* Парящий слой 2: 🃏 Карточки (Справа снизу) */}
            <motion.div 
              className="absolute bottom-[8%] right-0 md:right-[-10%] w-[50%] md:w-[45%] bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md border border-slate-200/80 rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-4 flex flex-col gap-2.5"
              style={{ transform: "translateZ(90px)" }}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600">
                <Brain className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Интервальное повторение</span>
              </div>
              <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-center">

                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">Интеграл Фурье</p>

                <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 border border-emerald-100 rounded">Отлично помню</span>

  return (
    <footer className="py-8 px-6 border-t border-slate-200/50 bg-white text-slate-500 text-sm">
      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="w-7 h-7 bg-indigo-600 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center">
            <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="text-slate-800 font-extrabold tracking-tight">StudyForge</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs font-semibold">
          <span>&copy; {new Date().getFullYear()} StudyForge. Все права защищены.</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Login.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { GraduationCap, Mail, Lock, ArrowRight, CheckCircle2, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { validateEmail } from '../utils/validators';

export default function Login() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [emailError, setEmailError] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  
  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) navigate('/dashboard');
  }, [user, navigate]);

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    
    const ee = validateEmail(email);
    if (ee) {
      setEmailError(ee);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
    if (error) {
      if (error.message.includes('Invalid login credentials')) {
        setError('Неверный email или пароль');
      } else {
        setError('Произошла ошибка при входе. Проверьте данные.');
      }
    } else navigate('/dashboard');
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f8fafc] flex">
      {/* Left Column: Form */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center px-6 sm:px-12 lg:px-24 xl:px-32 relative">
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/')} className="absolute top-8 left-6 sm:left-12 flex items-center gap-2 group">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-indigo-600 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center shadow-md">
            <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-lg tracking-tight group-hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors">StudyForge</span>
        </button>

        <motion.div 
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
          className="w-full max-w-sm mx-auto"
        >
          <div className="mb-8">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight mb-2">С возвращением!</h1>
            <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Войдите, чтобы продолжить обучение</p>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }} className="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-600 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium mb-6">
              {error}
            </motion.div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-5">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Email</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                  <Mail className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                </div>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  autoComplete="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={e => {
                    setEmail(e.target.value);
                    setEmailError(validateEmail(e.target.value));
                  }}
                  className={`block w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 bg-white border ${emailError ? 'border-rose-300 focus:border-rose-500 focus:ring-rose-500' : 'border-slate-200 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500'} rounded-xl text-slate-900 text-sm focus:ring-2 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm`}
                  placeholder="you@example.com"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              {emailError && <p className="text-rose-500 text-xs font-bold mt-1.5">{emailError}</p>}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Пароль</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                  <Lock className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                </div>
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  autoComplete="current-password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className="block w-full pl-11 pr-11 py-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-900 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm"
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  required
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-3.5 flex items-center text-slate-400 hover:text-indigo-600 transition"
                  tabIndex={-1}
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Eye className="w-5 h-5" />}
                </button>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-end mt-2">
                <button 
                  type="button" 
                  onClick={() => navigate('/forgot-password')} 
                  className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-500 transition-colors"
                >
                  Забыли пароль?
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="group relative w-full flex justify-center py-3.5 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-white bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition-all shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 hover:shadow-indigo-300 disabled:opacity-70"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">Входим... <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" /></span>
              ) : (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">Войти <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" /></span>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>

          <p className="mt-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium">
            Нет аккаунта?{' '}
            <button onClick={() => navigate('/register')} className="font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-500 hover:underline transition-all">
              Зарегистрироваться
            </button>
          </p>
        </motion.div>
      </div>

      {/* Right Column: Branded Visual (Hidden on mobile) */}
      <div className="hidden lg:flex flex-1 relative bg-indigo-600 overflow-hidden items-center justify-center p-12">
        {/* Animated background elements */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 to-indigo-800" />
        <div className="absolute top-[-10%] left-[-10%] w-96 h-96 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-3xl" />
        <div className="absolute bottom-[-10%] right-[-10%] w-[500px] h-[500px] bg-indigo-400/20 rounded-full blur-3xl" />
        
        {/* Geometric pattern overlay */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-10" style={{ backgroundImage: 'radial-gradient(white 1px, transparent 1px)', backgroundSize: '24px 24px' }} />

        {/* Floating Card UI */}
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.9, rotateY: -15 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, rotateY: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.8, type: "spring" }}
          className="relative w-full max-w-md bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/20 rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-2xl"
          style={{ transformStyle: "preserve-3d", perspective: 1000 }}
        >
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-white/20 rounded-full text-white text-xs font-bold mb-6">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" /> AI-Ассистент
          </div>
          
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-white leading-tight mb-4 tracking-tight">
            Готовьтесь к экзаменам <br/>
            с максимальной отдачей
          </h2>
          
          <p className="text-indigo-100 leading-relaxed text-sm mb-8">
            StudyForge преобразует ваши лекции в умные конспекты, карточки Лейтнера и тесты за 15 секунд.
          </p>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {[
              { label: "Точность алгоритма", value: "99.8%" },
              { label: "Экономия времени", value: "до 80%" },
              { label: "Запоминание", value: "x4 раза выше" }
            ].map((stat, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex items-center justify-between bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-3">
                <span className="text-indigo-100 text-sm font-medium">{stat.label}</span>
                <span className="text-white font-bold">{stat.value}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Register.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { GraduationCap, Mail, Lock, User, ArrowRight, Sparkles, BookOpen, Eye, EyeOff, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { validateName, validateEmail, validatePassword, getPasswordStrength } from '../utils/validators';

export default function Register() {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [nameError, setNameError] = useState('');
  const [emailError, setEmailError] = useState('');
  const [passwordError, setPasswordError] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [showConfirm, setShowConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [resendMessage, setResendMessage] = useState('');
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  
  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) navigate('/dashboard');
  }, [user, navigate]);

  const handleRegister = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    setSuccess('');
    
    const ne = validateName(name);
    const ee = validateEmail(email);
    const pe = validatePassword(password);
    
    if (ne || ee || pe) {
      setNameError(ne); setEmailError(ee); setPasswordError(pe);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    
    if (password !== confirm) {
      setError('Пароли не совпадают');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    
    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
      email,
      password,
      options: {
        data: { full_name: name },
        emailRedirectTo: window.location.origin + '/dashboard',
      },
    });
    
    if (error) {
      if (error.message.includes('already registered')) {
        setError('Пользователь с таким email уже существует');
      } else {
        setError('Произошла ошибка при регистрации. Проверьте данные.');
      }
    } else if (data?.user?.identities && data.user.identities.length === 0) {
      // Supabase "Prevent email enumeration" is ON, so it returns success but empty identities array
      // for users that already exist in the database.
      setError('Пользователь с таким email уже существует');
    } else {
      if (data.session) {
        navigate('/dashboard');
      } else {
        setSuccess('Успешно! Мы отправили письмо с подтверждением на вашу почту. Пожалуйста, перейдите по ссылке в письме.');
      }
    }
    
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleResend = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    setResendMessage('');
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.resend({
      type: 'signup',
      email: email,
      options: {
        emailRedirectTo: window.location.origin + '/dashboard',
      }
    });
    setLoading(false);
    if (error) {
      setError('Ошибка при повторной отправке. Возможно, вы пытаетесь отправить письмо слишком часто.');
    } else {
      setResendMessage('Письмо отправлено повторно! Пожалуйста, проверьте папку "Спам".');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f8fafc] flex">
      {/* Left Column: Form */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center px-6 sm:px-12 lg:px-24 xl:px-32 relative py-12">
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/')} className="absolute top-8 left-6 sm:left-12 flex items-center gap-2 group">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-indigo-600 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center shadow-md">
            <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-lg tracking-tight group-hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors">StudyForge</span>
        </button>

        <motion.div 
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
          className="w-full max-w-sm mx-auto"
        >
          <div className="mb-8">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight mb-2">Создать аккаунт</h1>
            <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Ваш умный помощник в учебе ждет</p>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }} className="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-600 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium mb-6">
              {error}
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {success && (
            <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }} className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-600 px-4 py-5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold mb-6 flex flex-col items-center text-center shadow-sm shadow-emerald-100">
              <Mail className="w-10 h-10 text-emerald-500 mb-3" />
              <span className="text-emerald-800 text-base mb-1">Успешно! Мы отправили письмо на вашу почту:</span>
              <span className="bg-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 font-black shadow-sm mb-3">
                {email}
              </span>
              <span className="font-medium text-emerald-600/80 mb-4">Пожалуйста, перейдите по ссылке в письме.</span>
              
              <button 
                onClick={handleResend}
                disabled={loading}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-100 hover:bg-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 rounded-lg transition-colors text-xs font-bold disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
                {loading ? 'Отправка...' : 'Отправить письмо еще раз'}
              </button>

              {resendMessage && (
                <span className="mt-3 text-emerald-700 font-bold text-xs bg-emerald-100 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg">
                  {resendMessage}
                </span>
              )}
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {!success && (
            <form onSubmit={handleRegister} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Имя</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                  <User className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                </div>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  autoComplete="name"
                  value={name}
                  onChange={e => {
                    setName(e.target.value);
                    setNameError(validateName(e.target.value));
                  }}
                  className={`block w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 bg-white border ${nameError ? 'border-rose-300 ring-rose-100 focus:border-rose-500 focus:ring-rose-500' : 'border-slate-200 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500'} rounded-xl text-slate-900 text-sm focus:ring-2 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm`}
                  placeholder="Ваше имя"
                />
              </div>
              {nameError && <p className="text-rose-500 text-xs font-bold mt-1.5">{nameError}</p>}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Email</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                  <Mail className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                </div>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  autoComplete="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={e => {
                    setEmail(e.target.value);
                    setEmailError(validateEmail(e.target.value));
                  }}
                  className={`block w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 bg-white border ${emailError ? 'border-rose-300 focus:border-rose-500 focus:ring-rose-500' : 'border-slate-200 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500'} rounded-xl text-slate-900 text-sm focus:ring-2 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm`}
                  placeholder="you@example.com"
                />
              </div>
              {emailError && <p className="text-rose-500 text-xs font-bold mt-1.5">{emailError}</p>}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Пароль</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                  <Lock className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                </div>
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  autoComplete="new-password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={e => {
                    setPassword(e.target.value);
                    setPasswordError(validatePassword(e.target.value));
                  }}
                  className={`block w-full pl-11 pr-11 py-3 bg-white border ${passwordError ? 'border-amber-300 focus:border-amber-500 focus:ring-amber-500' : 'border-slate-200 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500'} rounded-xl text-slate-900 text-sm focus:ring-2 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm`}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-3.5 flex items-center text-slate-400 hover:text-indigo-600 transition"
                  tabIndex={-1}
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Eye className="w-5 h-5" />}
                </button>
              </div>
              {passwordError && <p className="text-amber-500 text-xs font-bold mt-1.5">{passwordError}</p>}
              
              {/* Strength Meter (Light Theme) */}
              <div className="mt-2.5 flex gap-1.5">
                {[1,2,3].map(level => {
                  let colorClass = 'bg-slate-100'; // Default gray
                  if (password.length > 0) {
                    const strength = getPasswordStrength(password);
                    if (strength === 0) colorClass = level === 1 ? 'bg-rose-500' : 'bg-slate-100';
                    else if (strength === 1) colorClass = level === 1 ? 'bg-rose-500' : 'bg-slate-100';
                    else if (strength === 2) colorClass = level <= 2 ? 'bg-amber-400' : 'bg-slate-100';
                    else if (strength >= 3) colorClass = 'bg-emerald-500';
                  }
                  return (
                    <div key={level} className={`h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${colorClass}`} />
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Повтор пароля</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                  <Lock className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                </div>
                <input
                  type={showConfirm ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  autoComplete="new-password"
                  value={confirm}
                  onChange={e => setConfirm(e.target.value)}
                  className="block w-full pl-11 pr-11 py-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-900 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm"
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowConfirm(!showConfirm)}
                  className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-3.5 flex items-center text-slate-400 hover:text-indigo-600 transition"
                  tabIndex={-1}
                >
                  {showConfirm ? <EyeOff className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Eye className="w-5 h-5" />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="group relative w-full flex justify-center py-3.5 px-4 mt-2 border border-transparent rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-white bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition-all shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 hover:shadow-indigo-300 disabled:opacity-70"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">Регистрируем... <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" /></span>
              ) : (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">Зарегистрироваться <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" /></span>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>
          )}

          <p className="mt-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium">
            Уже есть аккаунт?{' '}
            <button onClick={() => navigate('/login')} className="font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-500 hover:underline transition-all">
              Войти
            </button>
          </p>
        </motion.div>
      </div>

      {/* Right Column: Branded Visual (Hidden on mobile) */}
      <div className="hidden lg:flex flex-1 relative bg-indigo-600 overflow-hidden items-center justify-center p-12">
        {/* Animated background elements */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 to-indigo-800" />
        <div className="absolute top-[-20%] right-[-10%] w-[600px] h-[600px] bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-3xl" />
        
        {/* Geometric pattern overlay */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-10" style={{ backgroundImage: 'radial-gradient(white 1px, transparent 1px)', backgroundSize: '24px 24px' }} />

        {/* Floating Abstract Element */}
        <motion.div 
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.8, type: "spring", delay: 0.1 }}
          className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-md"
        >
          <div className="bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/20 rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-2xl relative">
            <div className="absolute -top-6 -right-6 w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-400 rounded-2xl rotate-12 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg border border-white/20">
              <Sparkles className="w-8 h-8 text-white" />
            </div>
            
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-indigo-500/50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mb-6 border border-white/20">
              <BookOpen className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
            </div>

            <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-white leading-tight mb-4 tracking-tight">
              Ваш персональный <br/>учебный центр
            </h2>
            
            <ul className="space-y-4 text-indigo-100 text-sm font-medium">
              <li className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">✓</div>
                Бесконечная загрузка конспектов
              </li>
              <li className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">✓</div>
                Интервальное повторение
              </li>
              <li className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">✓</div>
                Календарь экзаменов
              </li>
              <li className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">✓</div>
                Интерактивная визуализация знаний
              </li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/AuthCallback.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';

export default function AuthCallback() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    // Обрабатываем токены из URL (email confirmation, magic link)
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session } }) => {
      if (session) {
        navigate('/dashboard', { replace: true });
      } else {
        // Слушаем событие входа после обработки токена
        const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, session) => {
          if (event === 'SIGNED_IN' && session) {
            navigate('/dashboard', { replace: true });
            subscription.unsubscribe();
          } else if (event === 'TOKEN_REFRESHED') {
            navigate('/dashboard', { replace: true });
            subscription.unsubscribe();
          }
        });
        // Если через 3 секунды ничего — на логин
        setTimeout(() => {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          subscription.unsubscribe();
        }, 3000);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4">
      <div className="w-10 h-10 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
      <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">Выполняем вход...</p>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { BookOpen, Brain, GraduationCap, Flame, ChevronRight, FileText, Zap, Calendar, Award, Gamepad2, Heart, Trophy, Sparkles, BarChart2, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { format, subDays } from 'date-fns';
import { ru } from 'date-fns/locale';
import { BarChart, Bar, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer } from 'recharts';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';
import { getDueCards } from '../api/flashcards';
import { getQuestsWithProgress, getUserXP, calcLevel } from '../api/quests';

function StatCard({ icon: Icon, label, value, color }: { icon: any; label: string; value: number; color: string }) {
  const bgMap: Record<string, string> = { indigo: 'bg-indigo-50', emerald: 'bg-emerald-50', amber: 'bg-amber-50', rose: 'bg-rose-50' };
  const textMap: Record<string, string> = { indigo: 'text-indigo-600', emerald: 'text-emerald-600', amber: 'text-amber-600', rose: 'text-rose-600' };
  return (
    <div className="card p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
      <div className={`w-10 h-10 ${bgMap[color]} rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center`}>
        <Icon className={`w-5 h-5 ${textMap[color]}`} />
      </div>
      <div>
        <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{value}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [dueCards, setDueCards] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [mood, setMood] = useState<any>(null);
  const [quests, setQuests] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [xp, setXp] = useState(0);
  const [levelInfo, setLevelInfo] = useState(calcLevel(0));
  const [nextExam, setNextExam] = useState<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    Promise.all([
      getMaterials(user.id),
      getDueCards(user.id),
      supabase.from('subjects').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id),
      supabase.from('study_sessions').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('date', { ascending: false }).limit(30),
      supabase.from('mood_log').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('logged_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1).maybeSingle(),
      getQuestsWithProgress(user.id),
      getUserXP(user.id),
      supabase.from('study_plans').select('subjects').eq('user_id', user.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1).maybeSingle(),
    ]).then(([mats, cards, subRes, sessRes, moodRes, questsData, xpVal, planRes]) => {
      setMaterials(mats);
      setDueCards(cards);
      setSubjects(subRes.data || []);
      setSessions(sessRes.data || []);
      setMood(moodRes.data);
      setQuests(questsData);
      const lvl = calcLevel(xpVal);
      setXp(xpVal);
      setLevelInfo(lvl);
      // Ближайший экзамен из плана
      if (planRes.data?.subjects) {
        const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        const upcoming = (planRes.data.subjects as any[])
          .filter(s => s.exam_date >= today)
          .sort((a, b) => a.exam_date.localeCompare(b.exam_date));
        if (upcoming.length > 0) setNextExam(upcoming[0]);
      }
    }).catch(console.error).finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [user?.id]);

  const streak = (() => {
    const dates = new Set(sessions.map((s: any) => s.date));
    let count = 0;
    let d = new Date();
    while (dates.has(format(d, 'yyyy-MM-dd'))) {
      count++;
      d = subDays(d, 1);
    }
    return count;
  })();

  const chartData = (() => {
    const map: Record<string, number> = {};
    sessions.forEach((s: any) => { map[s.date] = s.cards_reviewed; });
    const days = [];
    for (let i = 13; i >= 0; i--) {
      const d = subDays(new Date(), i);
      const key = format(d, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
      days.push({ day: format(d, 'dd.MM'), cards_reviewed: map[key] || 0 });
    }
    return days;
  })();

  if (loading) return (
    <div className="p-6 flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
      <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
    </div>
  );

  const name = user?.user_metadata?.full_name || 'Студент';

  const getRecommendedTool = () => {
    if (!mood) return null;
    const mode = mood.recommended_mode.toLowerCase();
    if (mode.includes('глубокое погружение')) return 'exam';
    if (mode.includes('активное обучение')) return 'play';
    if (mode.includes('повторение')) return 'review';
    if (mode.includes('лёгкий просмотр')) return 'chat';
    if (mode.includes('анти-стресс')) return 'planner';
    return null;
  };

  const recTool = getRecommendedTool();

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      {/* Greeting + XP */}
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-6 flex-wrap gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-0.5">Привет, {name}! 👋</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 capitalize">{format(new Date(), 'EEEE, d MMMM', { locale: ru })}</p>
        </div>
        {/* XP / Level mini-badge */}
        <motion.button
          onClick={() => navigate('/analytics')}
          whileHover={{ scale: 1.02 }}
          className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 hover:shadow-indigo-300 transition-shadow"
        >
          <div className="text-right">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-200 uppercase tracking-widest">Уровень {levelInfo.level} · {levelInfo.name}</p>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
              <div className="w-24 h-1.5 bg-white/20 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <motion.div
                  className="h-full bg-white rounded-full"
                  initial={{ width: 0 }}
                  animate={{ width: `${levelInfo.progress}%` }}
                  transition={{ duration: 1, ease: 'easeOut' }}
                />
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs font-black">{xp} XP</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <BarChart2 className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-200" />
        </motion.button>
      </div>

      {/* Виджет ближайшего экзамена */}
      {nextExam && (() => {
        const daysLeft = Math.ceil((new Date(nextExam.exam_date).getTime() - new Date().getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
        const urgency = daysLeft <= 3 ? 'rose' : daysLeft <= 7 ? 'amber' : 'indigo';
        const urgencyBg = urgency === 'rose' ? 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-200' : urgency === 'amber' ? 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-200' : 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-200';
        const urgencyText = urgency === 'rose' ? 'text-rose-700' : urgency === 'amber' ? 'text-amber-700' : 'text-indigo-700';
        return (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            onClick={() => navigate('/planner')}
            className={`mb-6 p-4 border rounded-2xl cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-4 ${urgencyBg} hover:shadow-md transition`}
          >
            <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center font-black ${urgency === 'rose' ? 'bg-rose-500' : urgency === 'amber' ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-indigo-500'} text-white shrink-0`}>
              <span className="text-lg leading-none">{daysLeft}</span>
              <span className="text-[8px] font-bold uppercase">дн.</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className={`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5 ${urgencyText}`}>
                {daysLeft <= 3 ? '🔥 Срочно!' : daysLeft <= 7 ? '⚡ Скоро экзамен' : '📅 Ближайший экзамен'}
              </p>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">{nextExam.name}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{new Date(nextExam.exam_date).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })}</p>
            </div>
            <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-300" />
          </motion.div>
        );
      })()}

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <StatCard icon={BookOpen} label="Материалов" value={materials.length} color="indigo" />
        <StatCard icon={Brain} label="Карточек сегодня" value={dueCards.length} color="emerald" />
        <StatCard icon={GraduationCap} label="Предметов" value={subjects.length} color="amber" />
        {/* Стрик с анимацией огня */}
        <motion.div
          className="card p-4 flex items-center gap-3 cursor-default"
          whileHover={{ scale: 1.02 }}
        >
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center ${streak >= 3 ? 'bg-rose-500' : 'bg-rose-50'}`}>
            <Flame className={`w-5 h-5 ${streak >= 3 ? 'text-white' : 'text-rose-400'} ${streak >= 3 ? 'animate-pulse' : ''}`} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{streak}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{streak >= 3 ? '🔥 Дней подряд!' : 'Дней подряд'}</p>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </div>

      {/* Mood Recommendation */}
      {mood && (
        <div
          onClick={() => navigate('/mood')}
          className="mt-6 p-4 bg-white border border-indigo-100 rounded-2xl shadow-sm cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-4"
        >
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-indigo-50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Heart className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">Твое состояние & Режим дня</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 font-medium leading-relaxed">{mood.recommended_mode}</p>
          </div>
          <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-300" />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Быстрый повтор за 5 минут */}
      {materials.length > 0 && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          onClick={() => navigate('/quick-prep')}
          className="mt-3 p-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-50 to-orange-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-4"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-amber-400 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-600 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">⚡ Экспресс-режим</p>
            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">Повторить материал за 5 минут</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Шпаргалка + 2 разогревочных вопроса</p>
          </div>
          <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-300" />
        </motion.div>
      )}

      {/* Two columns */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mt-6">
        {/* Left Col: Quests */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-1 space-y-6">
          <div className="card p-5 border-l-4 border-l-indigo-500">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
              <h2 className="font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Trophy className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500" />
                Квесты дня
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {quests.filter(q => q.type === 'daily').map(q => (
                <div key={q.id} className="space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${q.is_completed ? 'text-emerald-600 line-through' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>
                        {q.title}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-normal mt-0.5 leading-snug">
                        {q.description}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-indigo-500 shrink-0">+{q.xp_reward} XP</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="flex-1 h-1.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div 
                        className={`h-full transition-all duration-500 ${q.is_completed ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-indigo-500'}`}
                        style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (q.current_count / q.target_count) * 100)}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 shrink-0">{q.current_count}/{q.target_count}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="card p-5 border-l-4 border-l-purple-500">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
              <h2 className="font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-500" />
                Еженедельные
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {quests.filter(q => q.type === 'weekly').map(q => (
                <div key={q.id} className="space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${q.is_completed ? 'text-emerald-600 line-through' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>
                        {q.title}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-normal mt-0.5 leading-snug">
                        {q.description}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-purple-500 shrink-0">+{q.xp_reward} XP</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="flex-1 h-1.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div 
                        className={`h-full transition-all duration-500 ${q.is_completed ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-purple-500'}`}
                        style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (q.current_count / q.target_count) * 100)}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 shrink-0">{q.current_count}/{q.target_count}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Col: Due and Recent */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            {/* Due cards */}
            <div className="card p-5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">На повторение сегодня</h2>
                <button onClick={() => navigate('/review')} className="text-sm text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700 font-medium">Начать →</button>
              </div>
              {dueCards.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-emerald-600 text-sm">Все карточки повторены! ✅</p>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {dueCards.slice(0, 3).map((c: any) => (
                    <div key={c.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                      <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">{c.question}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  {dueCards.length > 3 && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">+ ещё {dueCards.length - 3}</p>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Recent materials */}
            <div className="card p-5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">Последние материалы</h2>
                <button onClick={() => navigate('/library')} className="text-sm text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700 font-medium">Все →</button>
              </div>
              {materials.length === 0 ? (
                <button onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')} className="text-sm text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700">Загрузи первую лекцию →</button>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {materials.slice(0, 3).map((m: any) => (
                    <div
                      key={m.id}
                      onClick={() => navigate('/library/' + m.id)}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600 cursor-pointer hover:text-slate-900 transition"
                    >
                      <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate flex-1">{m.title}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 shrink-0">{format(new Date(m.created_at), 'dd.MM.yy')}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Activity chart */}
          <div className="card p-5">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-4">Активность за 2 недели</h2>
            <div style={{ height: 200 }}>
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <BarChart data={chartData}>
                  <XAxis dataKey="day" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} stroke="#94a3b8" />
                  <YAxis allowDecimals={false} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} stroke="#94a3b8" />
                  <Tooltip
                    contentStyle={{ borderRadius: 8, border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', fontSize: 13 }}
                    labelFormatter={(l: string) => l}
                    formatter={(v: number) => [v, 'Карточек']}
                  />
                  <Bar dataKey="cards_reviewed" fill="#4F46E5" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
                </BarChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Quick actions */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4 mt-6">
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')}
          className="card p-5 text-center hover:shadow-md transition group"
        >
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-indigo-50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 group-hover:bg-indigo-100 transition">
            <Zap className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Загрузить лекцию</p>
        </button>
        
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/review')}
          className={`card p-5 text-center transition group relative overflow-hidden ${
            recTool === 'review' ? 'ring-2 ring-emerald-500 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100' : 'hover:shadow-md'
          }`}
        >
          {recTool === 'review' && (
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-1">
              <span className="flex h-2 w-2">
                <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-emerald-400 opacity-75"></span>
                <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-emerald-500"></span>
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 transition ${
            recTool === 'review' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 group-hover:bg-emerald-100'
          }`}>
            <Brain className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${recTool === 'review' ? 'text-emerald-900 font-bold' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>
            Повторить карточки
          </p>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/play')}
          className={`card p-5 text-center transition group relative overflow-hidden ${
            recTool === 'play' ? 'ring-2 ring-emerald-500 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100' : 'hover:shadow-md'
          }`}
        >
          {recTool === 'play' && (
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-1">
              <span className="flex h-2 w-2">
                <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-emerald-400 opacity-75"></span>
                <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-emerald-500"></span>
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 transition ${
            recTool === 'play' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 group-hover:bg-emerald-100'
          }`}>
            <Gamepad2 className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${recTool === 'play' ? 'text-emerald-900 font-bold' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>
            Микро-миссии
          </p>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/exam')}
          className={`card p-5 text-center transition group relative overflow-hidden ${
            recTool === 'exam' ? 'ring-2 ring-rose-500 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100' : 'hover:shadow-md'
          }`}
        >
          {recTool === 'exam' && (
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-1">
              <span className="flex h-2 w-2">
                <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-rose-400 opacity-75"></span>
                <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-rose-500"></span>
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 transition ${
            recTool === 'exam' ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white' : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 group-hover:bg-rose-100'
          }`}>
            <Award className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${recTool === 'exam' ? 'text-rose-900 font-bold' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>
            Устный экзамен
          </p>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/planner')}
          className={`card p-5 text-center transition group relative overflow-hidden ${
            recTool === 'planner' ? 'ring-2 ring-amber-500 shadow-lg shadow-amber-100' : 'hover:shadow-md'
          }`}
        >
          {recTool === 'planner' && (
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-1">
              <span className="flex h-2 w-2">
                <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-amber-400 opacity-75"></span>
                <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-amber-500"></span>
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2 transition ${
            recTool === 'planner' ? 'bg-amber-600 text-white' : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 group-hover:bg-amber-100'
          }`}>
            <Calendar className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${recTool === 'planner' ? 'text-amber-900 font-bold' : 'text-slate-700'}`}>
            Планировщик
          </p>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Analyze.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import UploadZone from '../components/UploadZone';
import AgentCards from '../components/AgentCards';
import ResultsPanel from '../components/ResultsPanel';
import { AnalysisState, AgentStatus, DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from '../types';
import { DEMO_TEXT } from '../demo';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { syncFlashcards } from '../api/flashcards';
import { updateQuestProgress, recordStudyActivity } from '../api/quests';

const initAgents: AgentStatus[] = [
  { id: 'summarizer', label: 'Конспект', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'flashcards', label: 'Карточки', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'quiz', label: 'Тест', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'cheatsheet', label: 'Шпаргалка', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'podcast', label: 'Подкаст', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'missions', label: 'Миссии', status: 'idle', progress: 0 },
];

interface Toast {
  id: number;
  text: string;
}

export default function Analyze() {
  const [state, setState] = useState<AnalysisState>({
    phase: 'upload', agents: initAgents, results: null, settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  });
  const [isDemo, setIsDemo] = useState(false);
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<Toast[]>([]);
  const toastId = useRef(0);
  const analysisStartRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const sourceFileNameRef = useRef<string>('');
  const textLengthRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const addToast = (text: string) => {
    const id = ++toastId.current;
    setToasts(prev => [...prev, { id, text }]);
    setTimeout(() => setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id)), 3000);
  };

  const handleUpload = async (file: File) => {
    analysisStartRef.current = Date.now();
    sourceFileNameRef.current = file.name;
    setState({ phase: 'extracting', agents: initAgents.map(a => ({ ...a, status: 'idle' as const })), results: null, settings: state.settings, sourceFileName: file.name });
    const fd = new FormData();
    fd.append('file', file);
    fd.append('summary_detail', state.settings.summaryDetail);
    fd.append('flashcard_count', String(state.settings.flashcardCount));
    fd.append('quiz_count', String(state.settings.quizCount));
    fd.append('quiz_difficulty', state.settings.quizDifficulty);
    fd.append('quiz_type', state.settings.quizType);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/analyze', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
      if (!res.ok) { const e = await res.json(); throw new Error(e.detail || 'Ошибка сервера'); }
      const reader = res.body?.getReader();
      if (!reader) throw new Error('No body');
      const dec = new TextDecoder(); let buf = '';
      while (true) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        buf += dec.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const lines = buf.split('\n'); buf = lines.pop() || '';
        for (const l of lines) {
          if (l.startsWith('data: ')) {
            try { handleSSE(JSON.parse(l.slice(6))); } catch {}
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err: any) { setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'error', error: err.message })); }
  };

  const agentDoneLabels: Record<string, string> = {
    summarizer: '✅ Конспект готов',
    flashcards: '✅ Карточки готовы',
    quiz: '✅ Тест готов',
    cheatsheet: '✅ Шпаргалка готова',
    podcast: '✅ Подкаст готов',
    missions: '✅ Квесты готовы',
  };

  const handleSSE = (d: any) => {
    if (d.type === 'text_extracted') { 
      setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'analyzing', textLength: d.length })); 
      textLengthRef.current = d.length;
      (window as any)._lastExtractedText = d.text; // Сохраняем текст из события
    }
    else if (d.type === 'agent_start') setState(p => ({ ...p, agents: p.agents.map(a => a.id === d.agent ? { ...a, status: 'running' } : a) }));
    else if (d.type === 'agent_done') {
      addToast(agentDoneLabels[d.agent] || '✅ Агент завершён');
      setState(p => ({ ...p, agents: p.agents.map(a => a.id === d.agent ? { ...a, status: 'done', progress: d.progress / d.total } : a) }));
    }
    else if (d.type === 'results') {
      const results = {
        summarizer: d.summarizer,
        flashcards: d.flashcards,
        quiz: d.quiz,
        cheatsheet: d.cheatsheet,
        podcast: d.podcast,
        missions: d.missions
      };
      const dur = analysisStartRef.current
        ? Math.round((Date.now() - analysisStartRef.current) / 1000)
        : 0;
      setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'done', results, analysisDuration: dur || undefined }));

      if (user) {
        const title = sourceFileNameRef.current
          ? sourceFileNameRef.current.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')
          : 'Без названия';

        (async () => {
          const { data: inserted, error } = await supabase
            .from('materials')
            .insert({
              user_id: user.id,
              title,
              subject: results.summarizer?.subject || '',
              original_text: textLengthRef.current > 0 ? (window as any)._lastExtractedText || '' : '',
              summary_json: {
                content: results.summarizer?.content || '',
                terms: results.summarizer?.terms || [],
                cheatsheet: results.cheatsheet?.content || '',
                podcast: results.podcast?.podcast || []
              },
              missions_json: results.missions,
              flashcards_json: results.flashcards,
              quiz_json: results.quiz,
            })
            .select('id')
            .single();

          if (error) {
            console.error('Supabase insert error:', error);
            return;
          }

          const cards = results.flashcards?.flashcards || [];
          if (cards.length > 0 && inserted?.id) {
            await syncFlashcards(user.id, inserted.id, cards);
          }
          await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'analyze', 1);
          await recordStudyActivity(user.id, 0, 1);
        })();
      }
    }
    else if (d.type === 'error') setState(p => ({ ...p, phase: 'error', error: d.message }));
  };

  const reset = () => { setState({ phase: 'upload', agents: initAgents, results: null, settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS }); setIsDemo(false); };

  const handleDemo = () => {
    setIsDemo(true);
    const file = new File([DEMO_TEXT], 'demo_lecture.txt', { type: 'text/plain' });
    handleUpload(file);
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {state.phase === 'upload' && (
          <motion.div key="upload" initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -20 }}>
            <UploadZone settings={state.settings} onSettingsChange={s => setState(p => ({ ...p, settings: s }))} onUpload={handleUpload} onDemo={handleDemo} />
          </motion.div>
        )}
        {(state.phase === 'extracting' || state.phase === 'analyzing') && (
          <motion.div key="analyzing" initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }}>
            <AgentCards agents={state.agents} phase={state.phase} isDemo={isDemo} />
          </motion.div>
        )}
        {state.phase === 'done' && state.results && (
          <motion.div key="results" initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}>
            <ResultsPanel results={state.results} onReset={reset} textLength={state.textLength} analysisDuration={state.analysisDuration} sourceFileName={state.sourceFileName} />
            <div className="mt-4 text-center">
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate('/library')}
                className="px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition"
              >
                📚 Перейти в библиотеку
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
        {state.phase === 'error' && (
          <motion.div key="error" initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} className="text-center py-16">
            <p className="text-4xl mb-3">⚠️</p>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-1">Ошибка</h2>
            <p className="text-slate-500 mb-4 text-sm">{state.error}</p>
            <button onClick={reset} className="px-5 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-brand-700 transition">Попробовать снова</button>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Toasts */}
      <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <AnimatePresence>
          {toasts.map(t => (
            <motion.div key={t.id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 40, scale: 0.95 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0, scale: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 40, scale: 0.95 }}
              className="bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-lg rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-700 font-medium">
              {t.text}
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Library.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Search, BookOpen, Trash2, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { ru } from 'date-fns/locale';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { getMaterials, deleteMaterial } from '../api/materials';

export default function Library() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [subjectFilter, setSubjectFilter] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    getMaterials(user.id)
      .then(data => setMaterials(data))
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [user?.id]);

  const subjects = [...new Set(materials.map(m => m.subject).filter(Boolean))];

  const filtered = materials.filter(m => {
    const matchSearch = m.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
    const matchSubject = !subjectFilter || m.subject === subjectFilter;
    return matchSearch && matchSubject;
  });

  const handleDelete = async (e: React.MouseEvent, id: string) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!confirm('Удалить материал?')) return;
    await deleteMaterial(id);
    setMaterials(prev => prev.filter(m => m.id !== id));
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Библиотека знаний</h1>
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition"
        >
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
          Новый анализ →
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="flex gap-3 mb-6">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            value={search}
            onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Поиск по названию..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition"
          />
        </div>
        {subjects.length > 0 && (
          <select
            value={subjectFilter}
            onChange={e => setSubjectFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition"
          >
            <option value="">Все предметы</option>
            {subjects.map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
          </select>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Loading */}
      {loading && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {[1, 2, 3].map(i => (
            <div key={i} className="animate-pulse bg-slate-200 h-24 rounded-xl" />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Empty */}
      {!loading && materials.length === 0 && (
        <div className="text-center py-20">
          <BookOpen className="w-16 h-16 text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-4" />
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-2">Библиотека пуста</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mb-6">Загрузите первую лекцию, чтобы начать</p>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')}
            className="px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition"
          >
            Загрузить первый материал
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Grid */}
      {!loading && filtered.length > 0 && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {filtered.map(m => {
            const fcCount = m.flashcards_json?.flashcards?.length || 0;
            const qCount = m.quiz_json?.quiz?.length || 0;
            return (
              <div
                key={m.id}
                onClick={() => navigate('/library/' + m.id)}
                className="card p-5 cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition group"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 truncate">{m.title}</h3>
                  <button
                    onClick={e => handleDelete(e, m.id)}
                    className="shrink-0 text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-500 transition opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
                {m.subject && (
                  <span className="inline-block px-2 py-0.5 bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs rounded-md mb-2">{m.subject}</span>
                )}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm text-slate-500 mb-2">
                  <span>📝</span>
                  <span>🃏 {fcCount}</span>
                  <span>❓ {qCount}</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                  {format(new Date(m.created_at), 'dd.MM.yyyy', { locale: ru })}
                </p>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* No results for filter */}
      {!loading && materials.length > 0 && filtered.length === 0 && (
        <p className="text-center text-slate-400 py-10">Ничего не найдено</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/MaterialDetail.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Edit3, Check, X, Globe, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { ru } from 'date-fns/locale';
import ResultsPanel from '../components/ResultsPanel';
import ExpressPrepView from '../components/ExpressPrepView';
import { getMaterialById, updateMaterialSubject, updateMaterialVisibility } from '../api/materials';

export default function MaterialDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [material, setMaterial] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [editingSubject, setEditingSubject] = useState(false);
  const [subjectInput, setSubjectInput] = useState('');
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'full' | 'express'>('full');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!id) return;
    getMaterialById(id)
      .then(data => {
        setMaterial(data);
        setSubjectInput(data.subject || '');
      })
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [id]);

  const saveSubject = async () => {
    if (!id) return;
    await updateMaterialSubject(id, subjectInput);
    setMaterial((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, subject: subjectInput }));
    setEditingSubject(false);
  };

  const togglePublic = async () => {
    if (!id || !material) return;
    const newStatus = !material.is_public;
    await updateMaterialVisibility(id, newStatus);
    setMaterial((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, is_public: newStatus }));
  };

  if (loading) return (
    <div className="p-6 flex items-center justify-center">
      <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
    </div>
  );

  if (!material) return (
    <div className="p-6 text-center">
      <p className="text-slate-400">Материал не найден</p>
      <button onClick={() => navigate('/library')} className="mt-4 text-indigo-600 text-sm hover:underline">← Библиотека</button>
    </div>
  );

  const hasFlashcards = material.flashcards_json?.flashcards?.length > 0;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
      {/* Back */}
      <button
