        onClick={() => navigate('/library')}
        className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 text-sm mb-4 transition"
      >
        <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
        Библиотека
      </button>

      {/* Title & Subject */}
      <div className="mb-2">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{material.title}</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-2">
        {editingSubject ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <input
              value={subjectInput}
              onChange={e => setSubjectInput(e.target.value)}
              className="px-3 py-1 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
              placeholder="Предмет"
              autoFocus
            />
            <button onClick={saveSubject} className="text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700"><Check className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
            <button onClick={() => { setEditingSubject(false); setSubjectInput(material.subject || ''); }} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button
            onClick={() => setEditingSubject(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm"
          >
            {material.subject ? (
              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 rounded-md">{material.subject}</span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1"><Edit3 className="w-3 h-3" />Добавить предмет</span>
            )}
          </button>
        )}

        <div className="w-px h-4 bg-slate-300 hidden sm:block" />

        <button 
          onClick={togglePublic}
          className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md transition ${
            material.is_public ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-200' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-200'
          }`}
        >
          {material.is_public ? (
            <><Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> В Маркетплейсе</>
          ) : (
            <><Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Только для меня</>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-6">
        {format(new Date(material.created_at), 'dd.MM.yyyy', { locale: ru })}
      </p>

      {/* Режимы обучения */}
      <div className="flex gap-1.5 bg-slate-200/50 p-1.5 rounded-2xl w-full sm:w-fit mb-6 border border-slate-200">
        <button
          onClick={() => setMode('full')}
          className={`flex-1 sm:flex-initial flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition ${
            mode === 'full'
              ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white shadow-md'
              : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
          }`}
        >
          📚 Полноформатное обучение
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => setMode('express')}
          className={`flex-1 sm:flex-initial flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition ${
            mode === 'express'
              ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white shadow-md'
              : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-rose-600'
          }`}
        >
          ⚡ За 5 минут до экзамена
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Контент в зависимости от выбранного режима */}
      {mode === 'full' ? (
        <ResultsPanel
          results={{
            summarizer: material.summary_json,
            flashcards: material.flashcards_json,
            quiz: material.quiz_json,
          }}
          onReset={() => navigate('/library')}
          textLength={0}
        />
      ) : (
        <ExpressPrepView summaryJson={material.summary_json} />
      )}

      {/* Review CTA */}
      {hasFlashcards && mode === 'full' && (
        <div className="mt-6 text-center">
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/review')}
            className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition"
          >
            🧠 Начать повторение карточек
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Review.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { CheckCircle2, Zap } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { getDueCards, updateCardProgress } from '../api/flashcards';
import { updateQuestProgress, recordStudyActivity } from '../api/quests';

function CircularProgress({ value, size = 100, strokeWidth = 8 }: { value: number; size?: number; strokeWidth?: number }) {
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const offset = circumference - (value / 100) * circumference;
  const color = value >= 80 ? '#10b981' : value >= 50 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} className="transform -rotate-90">
      <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke="#e2e8f0" strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />
      <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        strokeLinecap="round" strokeDasharray={circumference} strokeDashoffset={offset} />
    </svg>
  );
}

export default function Review() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [cards, setCards] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [idx, setIdx] = useState(0);
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState(false);
  const [sessionStats, setSessionStats] = useState({ correct: 0, wrong: 0 });
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const loadCards = () => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    setLoading(true);
    getDueCards(user.id)
      .then(data => { setCards(data); setIdx(0); setRevealed(false); setSessionStats({ correct: 0, wrong: 0 }); setDone(false); })
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadCards(); }, [user?.id]);

  const handleAnswer = async (quality: number) => {
    if (processing || !cards[idx]) return;
    setProcessing(true);
    try {
      await updateCardProgress(cards[idx].id, quality);
      if (user?.id) {
        await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'review', 1);
        await recordStudyActivity(user.id, 1, 0);
      }
      if (quality >= 3) setSessionStats(p => ({ ...p, correct: p.correct + 1 }));
      else setSessionStats(p => ({ ...p, wrong: p.wrong + 1 }));

      if (idx + 1 >= cards.length) setDone(true);
      else { setIdx(p => p + 1); setRevealed(false); }
    } finally {
      setProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) return (
    <div className="p-6 flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
      <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
    </div>
  );

  // No cards due
  if (cards.length === 0) return (
    <div className="p-6 text-center py-20">
      <CheckCircle2 className="w-16 h-16 text-emerald-500 mx-auto mb-4" />
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Отличная работа!</h2>
      <p className="text-slate-500 mb-6">На сегодня карточек нет. Приходите завтра!</p>
      <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/library')} className="px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
          В библиотеку
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/analyze')} className="px-5 py-2 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
          Новый анализ
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  // Session done
  if (done) {
    const total = sessionStats.correct + sessionStats.wrong;
    const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((sessionStats.correct / total) * 100) : 0;
    return (
      <div className="p-6 text-center py-16">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-6">Сессия завершена! 🎉</h2>
        <div className="relative inline-block mb-4">
          <CircularProgress value={pct} size={120} strokeWidth={10} />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
            <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800">{pct}%</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className="text-slate-500 mb-1">Правильно: <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-600">{sessionStats.correct}</span> из {total}</p>
        <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mb-8">Карточки будут показаны снова по расписанию spaced repetition</p>
        <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
          <button onClick={loadCards} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
            <Zap className="w-4 h-4" />Повторить ещё
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')} className="px-5 py-2 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">
            На дашборд
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const card = cards[idx];

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
      {/* Progress bar */}
      <div className="mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-slate-500 mb-2">
          <span>{idx + 1} / {cards.length} карточек</span>
          <span>{sessionStats.correct} ✓ · {sessionStats.wrong} ✗</span>
        </div>
        <div className="h-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
          <div className="h-full bg-indigo-500 rounded-full transition-all" style={{ width: `${((idx) / cards.length) * 100}%` }} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Card */}
      <motion.div
        key={idx}
        initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 30 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
        className="card min-h-48 p-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center cursor-pointer"
        onClick={() => !revealed && setRevealed(true)}
      >
        <span className="text-xs uppercase text-slate-400 tracking-wider mb-3">Вопрос</span>
        <p className="text-xl font-medium text-slate-900 text-center mb-6">{card.question}</p>

        {revealed && (
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} className="w-full border-t border-slate-100 pt-6">
            <span className="text-xs uppercase text-emerald-500 tracking-wider mb-2 block text-center">Ответ</span>
            <p className="text-slate-700 text-center">{card.answer}</p>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </motion.div>

      {/* Actions */}
      <div className="mt-6">
        {!revealed ? (
          <button
            onClick={() => setRevealed(true)}
            className="w-full h-12 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-xl font-semibold transition"
          >
            Показать ответ
          </button>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            <button
              onClick={() => handleAnswer(1)}
              className="h-12 bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 rounded-xl font-medium transition"
            >
              Не знал 😕
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => handleAnswer(3)}
              className="h-12 bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 border border-amber-200 text-amber-700 rounded-xl font-medium transition"
            >
              Затруднялся 🤔
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => handleAnswer(5)}
              className="h-12 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 rounded-xl font-medium transition"
            >
              Знал! 😎
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Planner.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Calendar, Trash2, Sparkles, CheckSquare, Square, Info, X, Clock, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { format, differenceInDays } from 'date-fns';
import { ru } from 'date-fns/locale';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';

export default function Planner() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedMaterials, setSelectedMaterials] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedSubjectIds, setSelectedSubjectIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [goals, setGoals] = useState('');
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [hours, setHours] = useState(2);
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState('');
  const [newDate, setNewDate] = useState('');
  const [newColor, setNewColor] = useState('#4F46E5');
  const [historyPlans, setHistoryPlans] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedSubject, setSelectedSubject] = useState<string>('');
  const [selectedDay, setSelectedDay] = useState<any>(null);
  const [dateError, setDateError] = useState('');

  const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

  const loadData = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    const { data: sub } = await supabase.from('subjects').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('exam_date');
    const { data: mat } = await supabase.from('materials').select('id, title, summary_json').eq('user_id', user.id);
    const { data: plans } = await supabase.from('study_plans').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    
    const subList = sub || [];
    const plansList = plans || [];
    setSubjects(subList);
    setMaterials(mat || []);
    setHistoryPlans(plansList);
    
    if (subList.length === 0) {
      setPlan([]);
      setHistoryPlans([]);
      setSelectedSubjectIds([]);
      setSelectedSubject('');
      return;
    }

    // НЕ выбираем автоматически — пользователь должен сам отметить нужные
    // Только если ровно 1 предмет и ничего не выбрано — выбираем его
    if (subList.length === 1 && selectedSubjectIds.length === 0) {
      setSelectedSubjectIds([subList[0].id]);
    }

    if (plansList.length > 0 && plan.length === 0) {
      setPlan(plansList[0].plan_json);
      setGoals(plansList[0].notes || '');
    } else if (plansList.length === 0) {
      setPlan([]);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadData(); }, [user?.id]);

  const addSubject = async () => {
    if (!newName || !newDate) return;
    if (newDate < todayStr) {
      setDateError('Дата экзамена не может быть в прошлом');
      return;
    }
    setDateError('');
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('subjects').insert({ user_id: user!.id, name: newName, exam_date: newDate, color: newColor }).select().single();
    if (!error && data) {
      setSelectedSubjectIds(prev => [...prev, data.id]);
    }
    setNewName('');
    setNewDate('');
    loadData();
  };

  const deleteSubject = async (id: string) => {
    const subToDelete = subjects.find(s => s.id === id);
    await supabase.from('subjects').delete().eq('id', id);
    
    if (subToDelete?.name === selectedSubject) {
      setSelectedSubject('');
    }
    
    setSelectedSubjectIds(prev => prev.filter(sid => sid !== id));
    loadData();
  };

  const generatePlan = async () => {
    if (selectedSubjectIds.length === 0) return alert('Выберите хотя бы один предмет для планирования');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const selectedContent = materials
        .filter(m => selectedMaterials.includes(m.id))
        .map(m => `Документ "${m.title}": ${m.summary_json?.content || ''}`)
        .join('\n\n');

      const selectedSubs = subjects.filter(s => selectedSubjectIds.includes(s.id));

      // Получаем актуальное настроение пользователя
      const { data: moodData } = await supabase
        .from('mood_log')
        .select('mood, energy_level')
        .eq('user_id', user!.id)
        .order('logged_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle();

      let moodInfo = "";
      if (moodData) {
          moodInfo = `\nСОСТОЯНИЕ СТУДЕНТА: настроение ${moodData.mood}, энергия ${moodData.energy_level}/5. `;
          if (moodData.energy_level <= 2) moodInfo += "Студент очень устал, планируй максимально легкие и короткие задачи.";
          else if (moodData.energy_level >= 4) moodInfo += "Студент полон сил, можно планировать интенсивную работу.";
      }

      const res = await fetch('/api/plan', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          subjects: selectedSubs.map(s => ({ name: s.name, exam_date: s.exam_date, color: s.color })),
          available_hours_per_day: hours,
          context_materials: selectedContent,
          user_goals: goals + moodInfo
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      const generatedPlan = data.plan || [];
      setPlan(generatedPlan);

      if (user && generatedPlan.length > 0) {
        const subNames = selectedSubs.map(s => s.name).join(', ');
        await supabase.from('study_plans').insert({
          user_id: user.id,
          title: `${subNames.length > 20 ? subNames.slice(0, 20) + '...' : subNames} (${format(new Date(), 'dd.MM')})`,
          plan_json: generatedPlan,
          notes: goals
        });
        loadData(); // Reload history
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error generating/saving plan:', err);
      setPlan([]);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const toggleMaterial = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedMaterials(prev => 
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter(x => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
    );
  };

  const toggleSubjectSelect = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedSubjectIds(prev => 
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter(x => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
    );
  };

  const getColorForSubject = (name: string) => {
    const s = subjects.find((s: any) => s.name === name);
    return s?.color || '#4F46E5';
  };

  // Filter plan by selected subject and ensure subject still exists
  const filteredPlan = plan.map(day => ({
    ...day,
    tasks: day.tasks?.filter((t: any) => {
      const exists = subjects.some(s => s.name === t.subject);
      if (!exists) return false;
      return selectedSubject ? t.subject === selectedSubject : true;
    }) || []
  })).filter(day => day.tasks.length > 0);

  const subjectTaskCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
  plan.forEach(day => {
    day.tasks?.forEach((t: any) => {
      subjectTaskCounts[t.subject] = (subjectTaskCounts[t.subject] || 0) + 1;
    });
  });

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6">
        <Calendar className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600" />
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Персональный планировщик</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
        <div className="lg:col-span-3 space-y-6">
          <div className="card p-5">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" /> Экзамены
            </h2>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mb-3">Отметьте галочкой предметы для плана</p>
            <div className="space-y-3 mb-4">
              {subjects.map(s => {
                const taskCount = subjectTaskCounts[s.name] || 0;
                const isSelected = selectedSubject === s.name;
                const isPlanned = selectedSubjectIds.includes(s.id);
                return (
                  <div key={s.id} className="group relative">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setSelectedSubject(isSelected ? '' : s.name)}
                      className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between text-sm p-2 rounded-lg transition border ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-200 text-indigo-700 font-bold'
                          : 'bg-slate-50 border-transparent hover:border-slate-200 text-slate-600'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden">
                        <div 
                          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSubjectSelect(s.id); }}
                          className={`w-4 h-4 rounded border flex items-center justify-center transition ${
                            isPlanned ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-white border-slate-300'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {isPlanned && <CheckSquare className="w-3 h-3" />}
                        </div>
                        <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: s.color }} />
                        <span className="font-medium truncate">{s.name}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        {taskCount > 0 && (
                          <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-600 rounded text-[10px]">{taskCount}</span>
                        )}
                        <button 
                          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); deleteSubject(s.id); }} 
                          className="text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-500 transition opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <input value={newName} onChange={e => setNewName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Название предмета" className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500" />
              <div>
                <input 
                  type="date" 
                  value={newDate} 
                  min={todayStr}
                  onChange={e => { setNewDate(e.target.value); setDateError(''); }} 
                  className={`w-full px-3 py-1.5 border rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 ${dateError ? 'border-rose-300' : 'border-slate-200'}`} 
                />
                {dateError && <p className="text-rose-500 text-[10px] mt-1">{dateError}</p>}
              </div>
              <button onClick={addSubject} className="w-full py-2 bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-slate-900 transition">Добавить предмет</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="card p-5">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" /> Часы в день
            </h2>
            <input type="number" min={1} max={12} value={hours} onChange={e => setHours(Number(e.target.value))} className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500" />
          </div>

          {historyPlans.length > 0 && (
            <div className="card p-5">
              <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" /> История планов
              </h2>
              <div className="space-y-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-1 custom-scrollbar">
                {historyPlans.map(p => (
                  <div key={p.id} className="group relative">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => { setPlan(p.plan_json); setGoals(p.notes || ''); }}
                      className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs transition border ${
                        plan === p.plan_json 
                        ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-200 text-indigo-700 font-bold' 
                        : 'bg-slate-50 border-transparent text-slate-600 hover:border-slate-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p.title}
                    </button>
                    <button 
                      onClick={async () => {
                        await supabase.from('study_plans').delete().eq('id', p.id);
                        loadData();
                      }}
                      className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-500 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-9 space-y-6">
          <div className="card p-6">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-500" /> На чем основывать план?
            </h2>
            
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-4">Выберите материалы из библиотеки, которые нужно изучить:</p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-6 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto pr-2 custom-scrollbar">
              {materials.map(m => (
                <div 
                  key={m.id} 
                  onClick={() => toggleMaterial(m.id)}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border cursor-pointer transition ${
                    selectedMaterials.includes(m.id) 
                    ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-200 text-indigo-700' 
                    : 'bg-white border-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:border-slate-300'
                  }`}
                >
                  {selectedMaterials.includes(m.id) ? <CheckSquare className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" /> : <Square className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />}
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{m.title}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
              {materials.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 col-span-2 italic">В библиотеке пока пусто</p>}
            </div>

            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" /> Ваши цели и пожелания
            </h2>
            <textarea 
              value={goals}
              onChange={e => setGoals(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Например: 'Я плохо знаю тему массивов, удели ей больше времени' или 'Мне нужно выучить только теорию для зачета'"
              className="w-full h-24 px-4 py-3 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition resize-none mb-6"
            />

            <button
              onClick={generatePlan}
              disabled={subjects.length === 0 || loading || selectedSubjectIds.length === 0}
              className="w-full py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-xl font-semibold transition disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              {loading ? <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" /> : <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5" />}
              {loading ? 'Создаем персональный план...' : selectedSubjectIds.length === 0 ? 'Выберите предметы для плана' : `Сформировать план (${selectedSubjectIds.length} предм.)`}
            </button>
          </div>

          {plan.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <h2 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-lg">Ваш план подготовки</h2>
                {selectedSubject && (
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setSelectedSubject('')}
                    className="text-xs text-indigo-600 hover:underline"
                  >
                    Показать все предметы
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                {filteredPlan.map((day: any, i: number) => {
                  const totalHours = day.tasks?.reduce((sum: number, t: any) => sum + (t.hours || 0), 0) || 0;
                  return (
                    <motion.div 
                      key={i} 
                      layoutId={`day-${day.date}`}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedDay(day)}
                      className="card p-5 bg-white border-l-4 border-l-indigo-500 flex flex-col cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition-shadow group"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 shrink-0">
                        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 capitalize text-sm group-hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors">
                          {format(new Date(day.date + 'T12:00:00'), 'EEEE, d MMMM', { locale: ru })}
                        </h3>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full">
                          <Clock className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" />
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">{totalHours}ч</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="space-y-3 flex-1">
                        {day.tasks?.map((task: any, j: number) => (
                          <div key={j} className="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs">
                            <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full mt-1.5 shrink-0" style={{ backgroundColor: getColorForSubject(task.subject) }} />
                            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                              <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 truncate block">{task.subject}</span>
                              <p className="text-slate-500 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed line-clamp-3">{task.task}</p>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </motion.div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Modal for Day Details */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {selectedDay && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[110] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
            <motion.div 
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
              onClick={() => setSelectedDay(null)}
              className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm"
            />
            <motion.div 
              initial={{ scale: 0.9, opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ scale: 0.9, opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden relative z-10 flex flex-col"
            >
              <div className="p-6 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between bg-white sticky top-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-indigo-50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Calendar className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 capitalize">
                      {format(new Date(selectedDay.date + 'T12:00:00'), 'EEEE, d MMMM', { locale: ru })}
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Подробный план на день</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setSelectedDay(null)}
                  className="p-2 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-full transition text-slate-400"
                >
                  <X className="w-6 h-6" />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
                {selectedDay.tasks?.map((task: any, idx: number) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-100">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: getColorForSubject(task.subject) }} />
                        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-lg">{task.subject}</h3>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-500">
                          <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
                          <span className="text-sm font-bold">{task.hours}ч</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-4">
                      <div className="flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 text-slate-400">
                          <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
                          <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Задача на день</span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-slate-700 leading-relaxed bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm">
                          {task.task}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-100 text-center">
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setSelectedDay(null)}
                  className="px-6 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-semibold hover:bg-indigo-700 transition shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100"
                >
                  Понятно
                </button>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          </div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Chat.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { MessageSquare, Send, Trash2, Maximize2, Minimize2 } from 'lucide-react';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';
import { updateQuestProgress } from '../api/quests';

export default function Chat() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState('');
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<{ role: string; content: string }[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [fetchingHistory, setFetchingHistory] = useState(false);
  const [isFullScreen, setIsFullScreen] = useState(false);
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const prevMsgCount = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    getMaterials(user.id).then(setMaterials).catch(console.error);
  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [user?.id]);

  // Загрузка истории при выборе материала
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedId || !user) {
      setMessages([]);
      return;
    }

    const loadHistory = async () => {
      setFetchingHistory(true);
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('chat_messages')
        .select('role, content')
        .eq('material_id', selectedId)
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true });
      
      if (!error && data) {
        setMessages(data);
        prevMsgCount.current = data.length;
      }
      setFetchingHistory(false);
    };

    loadHistory();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [selectedId, user?.id]);

  // Auto-scroll only when a NEW message is added (count increases)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (messages.length > prevMsgCount.current) {
      messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    } else if (messages.length > 0 && prevMsgCount.current === 0) {
      // First load of history — instant scroll
      messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'auto' });
    }
    prevMsgCount.current = messages.length;
  }, [messages]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isFullScreen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
    }
  }, [isFullScreen]);

  const selectedMaterial = materials.find(m => m.id === selectedId);
  
  // Контекст: берем либо оригинал, либо конспект (что есть)
  const getContext = () => {
    if (!selectedMaterial) return '';
    const original = selectedMaterial.original_text || '';
    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string' 
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json 
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    
    // Если есть оригинал — берем его (он полнее), иначе конспект
    return (original.length > 50 ? original : summary).slice(0, 30000);
  };

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!input.trim() || !selectedId || !user || loading) return;
    
    const userMsg = { role: 'user', content: input };
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, userMsg]);
    setInput('');
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      // 1. Сохраняем сообщение пользователя в БД
      await supabase.from('chat_messages').insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        material_id: selectedId,
        role: 'user',
        content: userMsg.content
      });

      await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'chat', 1);

      // Получаем актуальное настроение пользователя
      const { data: moodData } = await supabase
        .from('mood_log')
        .select('mood, energy_level')
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .order('logged_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle();

      // 2. Запрос к AI
      const res = await fetch('/api/chat', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          message: userMsg.content, 
          context: getContext(), 
          history: messages.slice(-6), // Отправляем последние 6 сообщений для памяти
          extra_params: moodData ? { mood: moodData } : {}
        }),
      });
      
      const data = await res.json();
      const assistantMsg = { role: 'assistant', content: data.reply };
      
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, assistantMsg]);

      // 3. Сохраняем ответ AI в БД
      await supabase.from('chat_messages').insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        material_id: selectedId,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: assistantMsg.content
      });

    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'assistant', content: 'Ошибка при обращении к AI. Попробуйте снова.' }]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const clearChat = async () => {
    if (!selectedId || !user || !confirm('Очистить историю чата?')) return;
    await supabase.from('chat_messages').delete().eq('material_id', selectedId).eq('user_id', user.id);
    setMessages([]);
  };

  const chatContent = (
    <div className={`flex flex-col transition-all duration-300 ${
      isFullScreen 
        ? 'fixed inset-0 z-[200] bg-slate-50 p-4 md:p-8 h-screen w-screen' 
        : 'h-full p-4 md:p-6 bg-slate-50/50'
    }`}>
      {/* Header */}
      <div className={`flex items-center justify-between mb-4 ${isFullScreen ? 'max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full' : ''}`}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-indigo-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600" />
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 truncate">AI-чат</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">Задавайте вопросы по вашим материалам</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setIsFullScreen(!isFullScreen)}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-indigo-600 transition rounded-lg hover:bg-indigo-50"
            title={isFullScreen ? "Уменьшить" : "На весь экран"}
          >
            {isFullScreen ? <Minimize2 className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Maximize2 className="w-5 h-5" />}
          </button>
          
          {messages.length > 0 && (
            <button onClick={clearChat} className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-500 transition rounded-lg hover:bg-rose-50" title="Очистить чат">
              <Trash2 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Material select */}
      <div className={`mb-4 ${isFullScreen ? 'max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full' : ''}`}>
        <select
          value={selectedId}
          onChange={e => setSelectedId(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full md:w-72 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition shadow-sm"
        >
          <option value="">Выберите материал...</option>
          {materials.map(m => (
            <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.title}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      {/* Chat area */}
      <div className={`flex-1 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col relative ${isFullScreen ? 'max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full h-full' : ''}`}>
        {!selectedId ? (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-8">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-slate-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
              <MessageSquare className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-300" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-slate-900 font-semibold mb-1">Чат не выбран</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm max-w-xs">Выберите лекцию из списка выше, чтобы начать обсуждение с AI</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4 custom-scrollbar">
              {fetchingHistory ? (
                <div className="flex justify-center py-8">
                  <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                </div>
              ) : messages.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-12">
                  <div className="inline-block px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 rounded-full text-xs font-medium mb-3">
                    Конспект загружен в память
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">
                    Спросите что-нибудь о «{selectedMaterial?.title}»
                  </p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                messages.map((msg, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className={`flex ${msg.role === 'user' ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}>
                    <div
                      className={`max-w-[85%] md:max-w-[75%] lg:max-w-[65%] p-3.5 text-sm rounded-2xl shadow-sm ${
                        msg.role === 'user'
                          ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-tr-none'
                          : 'bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 text-slate-800 rounded-tl-none'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className={`markdown-content ${msg.role === 'user' ? 'white-text' : ''}`}>
                        <ReactMarkdown>{msg.content}</ReactMarkdown>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
              {loading && (
                <div className="flex justify-start">
                  <div className="bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none p-4 flex gap-1.5 shadow-sm">
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-slate-400 rounded-full animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: '0ms' }} />
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-slate-400 rounded-full animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: '150ms' }} />
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-slate-400 rounded-full animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: '300ms' }} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
              <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
            </div>

            {/* Input */}
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-200">
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  value={input}
                  onChange={e => setInput(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); handleSend(); } }}
                  placeholder="Напишите вопрос по материалу..."
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition shadow-sm bg-white"
                  disabled={loading}
                />
                <button
                  onClick={handleSend}
                  disabled={loading || !input.trim()}
                  className="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white p-3 rounded-xl transition disabled:opacity-50 shadow-md shadow-indigo-200 flex-shrink-0"
                >
                  <Send className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return isFullScreen ? createPortal(chatContent, document.body) : chatContent;
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Missions.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Gamepad2, BookOpen, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, ChevronRight, HelpCircle, Trophy, Sparkles, X, ArrowRight, Lock, Unlock } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';
import { updateQuestProgress, checkAndAwardAchievements, recordStudyActivity } from '../api/quests';
import ConfettiCelebration from '../components/ConfettiCelebration';
import AchievementToast from '../components/AchievementToast';
import type { Achievement } from '../components/AchievementToast';

interface Mission {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  explanation: string;
  question: string;
  options: string[];
  correct: string;
  hint: string;
}

export default function Missions() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // Восстановление состояния из sessionStorage
  const savedStateStr = sessionStorage.getItem('studyforge_missions_state');
  const savedState = savedStateStr ? JSON.parse(savedStateStr) : null;

  // Состояние материалов
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedMaterialId, setSelectedMaterialId] = useState<string>(savedState?.selectedMaterialId || '');
  
  // Состояние игры
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<'select' | 'loading' | 'game' | 'completed'>(
    savedState?.phase === 'loading' ? 'select' : (savedState?.phase || 'select')
  );
  const [missions, setMissions] = useState<Mission[]>(savedState?.missions || []);
  const [activeStep, setActiveStep] = useState<number>(savedState?.activeStep !== undefined ? savedState.activeStep : 0); // индекс текущей миссии (0, 1, 2)
  const [selectedOption, setSelectedOption] = useState<string | null>(savedState?.selectedOption || null);
  const [isCorrect, setIsCorrect] = useState<boolean | null>(savedState?.isCorrect !== undefined ? savedState.isCorrect : null);
  const [showHint, setShowHint] = useState<boolean>(savedState?.showHint || false);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showConfetti, setShowConfetti] = useState(false);
  const [achievement, setAchievement] = useState<Achievement | null>(null);

  // Сохранение состояния в sessionStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    sessionStorage.setItem('studyforge_missions_state', JSON.stringify({
      selectedMaterialId,
      phase,
      missions,
      activeStep,
      selectedOption,
      isCorrect,
      showHint
    }));
  }, [selectedMaterialId, phase, missions, activeStep, selectedOption, isCorrect, showHint]);

  // Загрузка материалов
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    getMaterials(user.id).then(setMaterials).catch(console.error);
  }, [user?.id]);

  const selectedMaterial = materials.find(m => m.id === selectedMaterialId);

  // Старт игры: запрос генерации квестов
  const startGame = async () => {
    if (!selectedMaterial) return;
    setPhase('loading');
    setSelectedOption(null);
    setIsCorrect(null);
    setShowHint(false);
    setActiveStep(0);

    // 1. Проверяем, есть ли уже сгенерированные миссии в БД
    if (selectedMaterial.missions_json && selectedMaterial.missions_json.missions && selectedMaterial.missions_json.missions.length > 0) {
      setMissions(selectedMaterial.missions_json.missions);
      setPhase('game');
      return;
    }

    // 2. Если нет (старый материал), генерируем на лету
    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string'
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    const context = (selectedMaterial.original_text || summary || '').slice(0, 15000);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/missions/generate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ context })
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Ошибка генерации игры');
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (data.missions && data.missions.length > 0) {
        setMissions(data.missions);
        setPhase('game');
      } else {
        throw new Error('Пустой список миссий');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Не удалось сформировать игру по материалу. Попробуйте еще раз.');
      setPhase('select');
    }
  };

  // Проверка ответа студента
  const handleOptionClick = (option: string) => {
    if (isCorrect) return; // уже ответил верно

    setSelectedOption(option);
    const correctLetter = missions[activeStep].correct; // 'A', 'B' или 'C'
    const isCorrectAnswer = option.startsWith(correctLetter);

    if (isCorrectAnswer) {
      setIsCorrect(true);
      setShowHint(false);
    } else {
      setIsCorrect(false);
      setShowHint(true);
      setShake(true);
      setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 500); // анимация тряски
    }
  };

  // Переход к следующей миссии
  const handleNextMission = async () => {
    setSelectedOption(null);
    setIsCorrect(null);
    setShowHint(false);

    if (activeStep + 1 >= missions.length) {
      // Все миссии пройдены!
      setShowConfetti(true);
      setPhase('completed');
      setTimeout(() => setShowConfetti(false), 100);
      
      // Запись сессии в Supabase для начисления XP и активности
      if (user) {
        await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'quiz', 1);
        await recordStudyActivity(user.id, 3, 0);
        // Проверяем достижения
        const newAchs = await checkAndAwardAchievements(user.id);
        if (newAchs.length > 0) {
          setAchievement({ id: newAchs[0].id, title: newAchs[0].title, description: newAchs[0].description, icon: newAchs[0].icon, xp: newAchs[0].xp });
        }
      }
    } else {
      setActiveStep(prev => prev + 1);
    }
  };

  const currentMission = missions[activeStep];

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-center">
      <ConfettiCelebration trigger={showConfetti} type="quest" />
      <AchievementToast achievement={achievement} onClose={() => setAchievement(null)} />
      {/* Шапка */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6 justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-emerald-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
            <Gamepad2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Интерактивные миссии</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Геймификация сложных лекций в порции квестов</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {phase !== 'select' && (
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              if (confirm('Вы уверены, что хотите выйти из игры? Весь прогресс будет потерян.')) {
                setPhase('select');
                setMissions([]);
                setActiveStep(0);
                setSelectedOption(null);
                setIsCorrect(null);
                setShowHint(false);
              }
            }}
            className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 rounded-lg text-slate-600 transition"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Выйти из игры
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ФАЗА 1: Выбор материала */}
        {phase === 'select' && (
          <motion.div
            key="select"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -15 }}
            className="card p-6 md:p-8 text-center"
          >
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Gamepad2 className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-500" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Микро-квесты по лекциям</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto mb-6">
              AI-Методист разобьет сложную тему на 3 простые миссии. Изучите порции теории на простом языке и разблокируйте все уровни игры!
            </p>

            <div className="max-w-md mx-auto space-y-4">
              <div className="text-left">
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-2">Выберите лекцию из библиотеки:</label>
                <select
                  value={selectedMaterialId}
                  onChange={e => setSelectedMaterialId(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 transition shadow-sm"
                >
                  <option value="">Выберите лекцию для игры</option>
                  {materials.map(m => (
                    <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.title}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <button
                disabled={!selectedMaterialId}
                onClick={startGame}
                className="w-full py-3.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100"
              >
                <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5" /> Начать игру
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА ЗАГРУЗКИ / ГЕНЕРАЦИИ ИГРЫ */}
        {phase === 'loading' && (
          <motion.div
            key="loading"
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center"
          >
            <div className="relative mb-6">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-emerald-100 border-t-emerald-600 rounded-full animate-spin" />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Gamepad2 className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-800 text-base mb-1">ИИ-Методист готовит сценарий квестов...</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-xs leading-relaxed">
              Сложная научная лекция переводится на игровой язык с метафорами и аналогиями
            </p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА 2: Игровой процесс */}
        {phase === 'game' && currentMission && (
          <motion.div
            key="game"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.98 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            {/* Визуальная шкала миссий */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-100 p-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200">
              {missions.map((m, idx) => {
                const isActive = activeStep === idx;
                const isPassed = activeStep > idx;
                const isLocked = activeStep < idx;

                return (
                  <div key={m.id} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 relative">
                    <div className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition shadow-sm ${
                      isActive 
                        ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' 
                        : isPassed 
                        ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' 
                        : 'bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-400'
                    }`}>
                      {isPassed ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : isLocked ? <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Unlock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                      <span>Миссия {idx + 1}</span>
                    </div>
                    {idx < missions.length - 1 && (
                      <div className="hidden sm:block absolute right-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/2 z-10">
                        <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-300" />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {/* Игровой блок теории (простым языком) */}
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-900 to-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl relative border border-emerald-900/50 overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 bg-emerald-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
              
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black tracking-wider uppercase">Квест {activeStep + 1}: {currentMission.title}</span>
              </div>
              
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-white leading-snug">
                  📖 Теория на пальцах
                </h3>
                <div className="text-slate-300 text-sm leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line markdown-content white-text">
                  <ReactMarkdown>{currentMission.explanation}</ReactMarkdown>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Проверочный вопрос */}
            <motion.div 
              animate={shake ? { x: [-10, 10, -10, 10, 0] } : {}}
              transition={{ duration: 0.4 }}
              className="card p-5 space-y-4 bg-white"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" />
                <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm uppercase tracking-wider">Испытание для разблокировки следующего уровня:</h4>
              </div>

              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 leading-relaxed mb-4 markdown-content">
                <ReactMarkdown>{currentMission.question}</ReactMarkdown>
              </div>

              {/* Кнопки ответов */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3">
                {currentMission.options.map((opt) => {
                  const isThisSelected = selectedOption === opt;
                  const isCorrectAnswer = opt.startsWith(currentMission.correct);

                  let btnStyle = "bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100";
                  if (selectedOption) {
                    if (isThisSelected) {
                      btnStyle = isCorrectAnswer 
                        ? "bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800 ring-2 ring-emerald-500/20 font-bold" 
                        : "bg-rose-50 border-rose-300 text-rose-800 ring-2 ring-rose-500/20 font-bold";
                    } else if (isCorrectAnswer && !isCorrect) {
                      // подсвечиваем правильный если ответили неверно
                      btnStyle = "bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 opacity-60";
                    } else {
                      btnStyle = "bg-slate-50 border-transparent text-slate-400 opacity-40 cursor-default";
                    }
                  }

                  return (
                    <button
                      key={opt}
                      onClick={() => handleOptionClick(opt)}
                      disabled={isCorrect !== null}
                      className={`w-full p-4 rounded-xl border text-left text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200 flex justify-between items-center ${btnStyle}`}
                    >
                      <span>{opt}</span>
                      {selectedOption && isThisSelected && (
                        isCorrectAnswer 
                          ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" /> 
                          : <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />
                      )}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Вывод подсказки при ошибке */}
              <AnimatePresence>
                {showHint && currentMission.hint && (
                  <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                    style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}
                    className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl"
                  >
                    <div className="p-3.5 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-amber-800">
                      <AlertCircle className="w-4.5 h-4.5 shrink-0 text-amber-500 mt-0.5" />
                      <div>
                        <span className="font-bold block mb-0.5">💡 Подсказка AI-Методиста:</span>
                        {currentMission.hint}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </motion.div>
                )}
              </AnimatePresence>

              {/* Кнопка далее */}
              {isCorrect && (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  className="pt-2"
                >
                  <button
                    onClick={handleNextMission}
                    className="w-full py-3.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100"
                  >
                    {activeStep + 1 >= missions.length ? 'Завершить обучение 🎉' : 'Пройти к миссии ' + (activeStep + 2) + ' →'}
                  </button>
                </motion.div>
              )}
            </motion.div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА 3: Завершение и награда */}
        {phase === 'completed' && (
          <motion.div
            key="completed"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="card p-8 md:p-10 text-center bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50 to-teal-50 border-emerald-100 shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden"
          >
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-64 h-64 bg-emerald-500/5 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
            <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-64 h-64 bg-teal-500/5 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-emerald-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6 text-white shadow-xl relative overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-white/10 rounded-full scale-90 border border-dashed border-white/50 animate-spin-slow" />
              <Trophy className="w-10 h-10 animate-pulse" />
            </div>

            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-800 mb-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
              Тема полностью освоена!
              <Sparkles className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-500 animate-bounce" />
            </h2>
            <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm max-w-md mx-auto mb-8">
              Вы успешно прошли все 3 микро-миссии по лекции «{selectedMaterial?.title}»! Вы поняли суть темы без лишней академической шелухи.
            </p>

            {/* Бейдж награды XP */}
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white font-extrabold px-6 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-md mb-8 ring-4 ring-emerald-600/10">
              <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-300" />
              <span>НАГРАДА: +50 XP</span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center max-w-md mx-auto">
              <button
                onClick={() => setPhase('select')}
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100"
              >
                Выбрать другую лекцию
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')}
                className="px-6 py-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-slate-50 transition"
              >
                Вернуться на дашборд
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/ProfessorExam.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Award, BookOpen, Mic, MicOff, Send, MessageSquare, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, HelpCircle, ArrowRight, RefreshCw, X, Volume2, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';
import { updateQuestProgress, checkAndAwardAchievements, recordStudyActivity } from '../api/quests';
import ConfettiCelebration from '../components/ConfettiCelebration';
import AchievementToast from '../components/AchievementToast';
import type { Achievement } from '../components/AchievementToast';

// Web Speech API
const SpeechRecognition = (window as any).SpeechRecognition || (window as any).webkitSpeechRecognition;

interface EvaluationResult {
  score: number;
  grade: string;
  verdict: string;
  correct_points: string[];
  errors: string[];
  missing_points: string[];
}

export default function ProfessorExam() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // Восстановление состояния из sessionStorage
  const savedStateStr = sessionStorage.getItem('studyforge_professor_exam_state');
  const savedState = savedStateStr ? JSON.parse(savedStateStr) : null;

  // Состояние материалов
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedMaterialId, setSelectedMaterialId] = useState<string>(savedState?.selectedMaterialId || '');
  
  // Состояние экзамена
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<'select' | 'question' | 'evaluation' | 'loading'>(
    savedState?.phase === 'loading' ? 'select' : (savedState?.phase || 'select')
  );
  const [currentQuestion, setCurrentQuestion] = useState<string>(savedState?.currentQuestion || '');
  const [answerText, setAnswerText] = useState<string>(savedState?.answerText || '');
  const [interimText, setInterimText] = useState<string>('');
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<{ question: string; answer: string; grade: string }[]>(savedState?.history || []);
  const [evaluation, setEvaluation] = useState<EvaluationResult | null>(savedState?.evaluation || null);
  const [examHistory, setExamHistory] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [showHistory, setShowHistory] = useState<boolean>(false);
  
  // Голосовой ввод
  const [isListening, setIsListening] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const recognitionRef = useRef<any>(null);
  // Режим экзамена
  const [socraticMode, setSocraticMode] = useState<boolean>(savedState?.socraticMode || false);
  // Конфетти и достижения
  const [showConfetti, setShowConfetti] = useState(false);
  const [achievement, setAchievement] = useState<Achievement | null>(null);

  // Сохранение состояния в sessionStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    sessionStorage.setItem('studyforge_professor_exam_state', JSON.stringify({
      selectedMaterialId,
      phase,
      currentQuestion,
      answerText,
      history,
      evaluation,
      socraticMode
    }));
  }, [selectedMaterialId, phase, currentQuestion, answerText, history, evaluation, socraticMode]);

  // Загрузка материалов и истории экзаменов
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    getMaterials(user.id).then(setMaterials).catch(console.error);
    fetchExamHistory();
  }, [user?.id]);

  const fetchExamHistory = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('exam_sessions')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', user.id)
      .eq('status', 'completed')
      .order('completed_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(10);
    if (data) setExamHistory(data);
  };

  // Настройка голосового распознавания
  const initRecognition = () => {
    if (recognitionRef.current) return recognitionRef.current;
    
    if (!SpeechRecognition) return null;

    const rec = new SpeechRecognition();
    rec.continuous = true;
    rec.lang = 'ru-RU';
    rec.interimResults = true;

    rec.onstart = () => {
      setIsListening(true);
      console.log('Speech recognition started');
    };
    
    rec.onend = () => {
      setIsListening(false);
      setInterimText('');
      console.log('Speech recognition ended');
    };

    rec.onresult = (event: any) => {
      let finalTranscript = '';
      let currentInterim = '';
      for (let i = event.resultIndex; i < event.results.length; ++i) {
        if (event.results[i].isFinal) {
          finalTranscript += event.results[i][0].transcript;
        } else {
          currentInterim += event.results[i][0].transcript;
        }
      }
      if (finalTranscript) {
        setAnswerText(prev => prev ? prev + ' ' + finalTranscript : finalTranscript);
      }
      setInterimText(currentInterim);
    };

    rec.onerror = (e: any) => {
      console.error('Speech recognition error:', e.error);
      setIsListening(false);
      
      if (e.error === 'not-allowed') {
        alert('Доступ к микрофону заблокирован. Разрешите его в настройках браузера (значок замка в адресной строке).');
      } else if (e.error === 'network') {
        alert('Ошибка сети. Проверьте интернет-соединение. Голосовой ввод Google требует стабильной сети.');
      } else if (e.error === 'no-speech') {
        // Просто игнорируем, если не услышал
        console.log('No speech detected');
      } else {
        console.warn('Recognition error:', e.error);
      }
    };

    recognitionRef.current = rec;
    return rec;
  };

  const toggleListening = () => {
    const rec = initRecognition();
    
    if (!rec) {
      alert('Голосовой ввод не поддерживается вашим браузером. Пожалуйста, используйте Google Chrome, Edge или Safari.');
      return;
    }

    if (isListening) {
      try {
        rec.stop();
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Stop error:', e);
      }
    } else {
      try {
        rec.start();
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Start error:', err);
        // Если уже запущено, просто обновляем стейт
        if (err.name === 'InvalidStateError') {
          setIsListening(true);
        }
      }
    }
  };

  // Очистка при размонтировании
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (recognitionRef.current) {
        try {
          recognitionRef.current.stop();
        } catch (e) {}
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const selectedMaterial = materials.find(m => m.id === selectedMaterialId);

  // Начать экзамен / Запросить вопрос
  const startExamOrNextQuestion = async (clearHistory = false) => {
    if (!selectedMaterial) return;
    setPhase('loading');
    setAnswerText('');
    
    let currentHistory = history;
    if (clearHistory) {
      currentHistory = [];
      setHistory([]);
      setEvaluation(null);
    }

    // Собираем историю для контекста
    const historyText = currentHistory
      .map(h => `В: ${h.question}\nО: ${h.answer}\nОценка: ${h.grade}`)
      .join('\n\n');

    // Получаем конспект/оригинал лекции
    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string'
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    const context = (selectedMaterial.original_text || summary || '').slice(0, 20000);

    // Получаем актуальное настроение пользователя
    const { data: moodData } = await supabase
      .from('mood_log')
      .select('mood, energy_level')
      .eq('user_id', user!.id)
      .order('logged_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(1)
      .maybeSingle();

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/professor/question', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          context,
          history_text: historyText,
          mood: moodData,
          socratic_mode: socraticMode,
        })
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Ошибка связи с сервером');
      const data = await res.json();
      setCurrentQuestion(data.question);
      setPhase('question');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Не удалось задать вопрос. Попробуйте еще раз.');
      setPhase(clearHistory ? 'select' : 'evaluation');
    }
  };

  // Отправить ответ на оценку
  const submitAnswer = async () => {
    if (!answerText.trim() || !selectedMaterial) return;
    setPhase('loading');

    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string'
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    const context = (selectedMaterial.original_text || summary || '').slice(0, 20000);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/professor/evaluate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          context,
          question: currentQuestion,
          student_answer: answerText
        })
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Ошибка оценки');
      const evalData: EvaluationResult = await res.json();
      
      setEvaluation(evalData);
      
      // Конфетти при пятёрке!
      if (evalData.grade === '5') {
        setShowConfetti(true);
        setTimeout(() => setShowConfetti(false), 100);
      }
      
      // Добавляем в локальную историю
      setHistory(prev => [
        ...prev,
        { question: currentQuestion, answer: answerText, grade: evalData.grade }
      ]);

      // Запись сессии в Supabase для статистики
      if (user) {
        // Создаем сессию экзамена если ее еще нет
        const { data: sessionData } = await supabase.from('exam_sessions').insert({
          user_id: user.id,
          material_id: selectedMaterial?.id ?? null,
          subject: selectedMaterial?.subject ?? '',
          score: evalData.score,
          questions_json: [{ question: currentQuestion, answer: answerText, evaluation: evalData }],
          total_questions: 1,
          status: 'completed',
          completed_at: new Date().toISOString()
        }).select().single();

        if (sessionData) {
          fetchExamHistory();
          await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'exam', 1);
          // Проверяем достижения
          const newAchs = await checkAndAwardAchievements(user.id);
          if (newAchs.length > 0) {
            setAchievement({ id: newAchs[0].id, title: newAchs[0].title, description: newAchs[0].description, icon: newAchs[0].icon, xp: newAchs[0].xp });
          }
        }

        await recordStudyActivity(user.id, 1, 0);

        // Автоматически сохраняем ошибки в дневник ошибок
        const mistakesToSave = [
          ...evalData.errors.map((err: string) => ({
            user_id: user.id,
            material_id: selectedMaterial?.id ?? null,
            question: currentQuestion,
            correct_answer: err,
            student_answer: answerText,
            topic: selectedMaterial?.subject ?? '',
            is_resolved: false,
          })),
          ...evalData.missing_points.map((miss: string) => ({
            user_id: user.id,
            material_id: selectedMaterial?.id ?? null,
            question: currentQuestion,
            correct_answer: miss,
            student_answer: '(не упомянуто)',
            topic: selectedMaterial?.subject ?? '',
            is_resolved: false,
          })),
        ];

        if (mistakesToSave.length > 0) {
          await supabase.from('mistake_diary').insert(mistakesToSave);
        }
      }

      setPhase('evaluation');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Ошибка при оценке ответа. Пожалуйста, попробуйте отправить заново.');
      setPhase('question');
    }
  };
