  // Цвета для академической оценки
  const getGradeColors = (grade: string) => {
    switch (grade) {
      case '5': return { bg: 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200', text: 'text-emerald-700', sealBg: 'bg-emerald-500', label: 'ОТЛИЧНО (5)' };
      case '4': return { bg: 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-200', text: 'text-indigo-700', sealBg: 'bg-indigo-500', label: 'ХОРОШО (4)' };
      case '3': return { bg: 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-200', text: 'text-amber-700', sealBg: 'bg-amber-500', label: 'УДОВЛЕТВОРИТЕЛЬНО (3)' };
      default: return { bg: 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-200', text: 'text-rose-700', sealBg: 'bg-rose-500', label: 'НЕУД (2)' };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-center">
      <ConfettiCelebration trigger={showConfetti} type="exam5" />
      <AchievementToast achievement={achievement} onClose={() => setAchievement(null)} />
      {/* Заголовок */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6 justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-rose-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
            <Award className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">AI-Экзаменатор</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Устная репетиция сдача сессии профессору</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {/* Переключатель режима */}
          {phase === 'select' && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setSocraticMode(p => !p)}
              className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition border ${
                socraticMode
                  ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 border-purple-200'
                  : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-200'
              }`}
              title="Режим Сократовского диалога: профессор докапывается до сути через уточняющие вопросы"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              {socraticMode ? 'Сократ ВКЛ' : 'Сократ ВЫКЛ'}
            </button>
          )}
          {phase !== 'select' && (
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                if (confirm('Вы уверены, что хотите завершить экзамен?')) {
                  setPhase('select');
                  setCurrentQuestion('');
                  setAnswerText('');
                  setHistory([]);
                  setEvaluation(null);
                }
              }}
              className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 rounded-lg text-slate-600 transition"
            >
              <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Выйти
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ФАЗА 1: Выбор материала */}
        {phase === 'select' && (
          <motion.div
            key="select"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -15 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="card p-6 md:p-8 text-center">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-rose-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                <Award className="w-8 h-8 text-rose-500" />
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Готовы проверить свои знания?</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto mb-6">
                AI-Профессор задаст вам каверзные вопросы на понимание. Вы можете вводить ответ текстом или наговаривать голосом через микрофон.
              </p>

              <div className="max-w-md mx-auto space-y-4">
                <div className="text-left">
                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-2">Выберите лекцию из библиотеки:</label>
                  <select
                    value={selectedMaterialId}
                    onChange={e => setSelectedMaterialId(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-500 transition shadow-sm"
                  >
                    <option value="">Выберите материал для сдачи</option>
                    {materials.map(m => (
                      <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.title}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <button
                  disabled={!selectedMaterialId}
                  onClick={() => startExamOrNextQuestion(true)}
                  className="w-full py-3.5 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100"
                >
                  <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5" /> Войти в аудиторию
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* История экзаменов */}
            {examHistory.length > 0 && (
              <div className="card p-6">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                    <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                    История последних экзаменов
                  </h3>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {examHistory.map(session => (
                    <div key={session.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 truncate">{session.subject || 'Без предмета'}</span>
                          <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-200 text-slate-500 rounded text-[9px] font-bold">
                            {new Date(session.completed_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate">Билет по одной из лекций</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 ml-4">
                        <div className="text-right">
                          <p className="text-xs font-black text-rose-600">{session.score}%</p>
                          <p className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold">Оценка</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА ЗАГРУЗКИ / «Профессор думает...» */}
        {phase === 'loading' && (
          <motion.div
            key="loading"
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center"
          >
            <div className="relative mb-6">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-rose-100 border-t-rose-600 rounded-full animate-spin" />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Award className="w-6 h-6 text-rose-500 animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-800 text-base mb-1">Профессор анализирует материал...</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-xs leading-relaxed">
              Формулируется индивидуальный вопрос для оценки вашего понимания
            </p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА 2: Экзаменационный вопрос */}
        {phase === 'question' && (
          <motion.div
            key="question"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.98 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            {/* Вопрос Профессора */}
            <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl relative border border-slate-800 overflow-hidden">
              <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 bg-rose-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
              
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/30 rounded text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase">Билет №{history.length + 1}</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Лекция: {selectedMaterial?.title}</span>
              </div>
              
              <div className="flex gap-4 items-start">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-slate-800 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 border border-slate-700">
                  <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-400" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-300 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Вопрос профессора:</h3>
                  <div className="text-base font-medium text-white leading-relaxed markdown-content white-text professor-question">
                    <ReactMarkdown>{currentQuestion}</ReactMarkdown>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Ввод ответа студента */}
            <div className="card p-5 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Ваш устный ответ:</label>
                {SpeechRecognition && (
                  <button
                    onClick={toggleListening}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition ${
                      isListening
                        ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 animate-pulse'
                        : 'bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {isListening ? <MicOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Mic className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-500" />}
                    {isListening ? 'Слушаю вас...' : 'Говорить в микрофон'}
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <textarea
                value={answerText + (interimText ? (answerText ? ' ' : '') + interimText : '')}
                onChange={e => {
                  setAnswerText(e.target.value);
                  setInterimText('');
                }}
                placeholder="Сформулируйте ваш развернутый ответ на вопрос профессора..."
                className="w-full h-36 px-4 py-3.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-500 transition resize-none bg-slate-50/50"
              />

              <div className="flex gap-3">
                <button
                  disabled={!answerText.trim()}
                  onClick={submitAnswer}
                  className="flex-1 py-3 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100"
                >
                  <Send className="w-4 h-4" /> Ответить Профессору
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФАЗА 3: Оценка и глубокий разбор пробелов */}
        {phase === 'evaluation' && evaluation && (
          <motion.div
            key="evaluation"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-6"
          >
            {/* Оценка и вердикт */}
            {(() => {
              const colors = getGradeColors(evaluation.grade);
              return (
                <div className={`border rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center gap-6 ${colors.bg}`}>
                  {/* Академическая печать оценки */}
                  <div className="shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
                    <div className={`w-20 h-20 rounded-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-white font-black text-3xl shadow-md ${colors.sealBg} relative overflow-hidden`}>
                      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-white/10 rounded-full scale-90 border border-dashed border-white/50" />
                      {evaluation.grade}
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 mt-2 tracking-widest uppercase">Академическая печать</span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex-1 text-center md:text-left">
                    <h3 className={`text-sm font-black tracking-wider uppercase mb-1 ${colors.text}`}>{colors.label}</h3>
                    <p className="text-slate-700 font-medium text-sm leading-relaxed mb-2">"{evaluation.verdict}"</p>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center md:justify-start gap-1.5">
                      <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400">Точность ответа:</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">{evaluation.score}%</span>
                      <div className="w-20 h-1.5 bg-slate-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                        <div className="h-full bg-rose-500 rounded-full" style={{ width: `${evaluation.score}%` }} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })()}

            {/* Когнитивный разбор: что правильно, ошибки и пробелы */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              {/* Зеленый блок: Что сказали правильно */}
              <div className="card p-5 border-l-4 border-l-emerald-500 bg-white">
                <h4 className="font-bold text-emerald-800 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" />
                  Усвоено верно
                </h4>
                {evaluation.correct_points.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs italic">Нет полностью верных фактов в ответе.</p>
                ) : (
                  <ul className="space-y-2">
                    {evaluation.correct_points.map((p, i) => (
                      <li key={i} className="text-slate-600 text-xs leading-relaxed flex items-start gap-1.5">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-emerald-400 rounded-full shrink-0 mt-1.5" />
                        {p}
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Красный блок: Допущенные ошибки */}
              <div className="card p-5 border-l-4 border-l-rose-500 bg-white">
                <h4 className="font-bold text-rose-800 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />
                  Фактические ошибки
                </h4>
                {evaluation.errors.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-emerald-600 text-xs font-medium">Ошибок в ответе не обнаружено! Отличная точность.</p>
                ) : (
                  <ul className="space-y-2">
                    {evaluation.errors.map((p, i) => (
                      <li key={i} className="text-slate-600 text-xs leading-relaxed flex items-start gap-1.5">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-rose-400 rounded-full shrink-0 mt-1.5" />
                        {p}
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Желтый/серый блок: Упущенные знания */}
              <div className="card p-5 border-l-4 border-l-amber-500 bg-white">
                <h4 className="font-bold text-amber-800 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 shrink-0" />
                  Когнитивные пропуски
                </h4>
                {evaluation.missing_points.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-emerald-600 text-xs font-medium">Ничего не упущено. Исчерпывающий ответ!</p>
                ) : (
                  <ul className="space-y-2">
                    {evaluation.missing_points.map((p, i) => (
                      <li key={i} className="text-slate-600 text-xs leading-relaxed flex items-start gap-1.5">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-amber-400 rounded-full shrink-0 mt-1.5" />
                        {p}
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Действия */}
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 pt-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => startExamOrNextQuestion(false)}
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" /> Следующий билет
              </button>
              
              <button
                onClick={() => setPhase('select')}
                className="px-6 py-3 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                Завершить экзамен
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/MistakeDiary.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { BookMarked, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, RefreshCw, Trash2, Filter, ChevronDown, Zap } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { addXP } from '../api/quests';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';

interface Mistake {
  id: string;
  question: string;
  correct_answer: string;
  student_answer: string;
  topic: string;
  is_resolved: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  material_id: string | null;
}

export default function MistakeDiary() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [mistakes, setMistakes] = useState<Mistake[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'all' | 'open' | 'resolved'>('all');
  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Состояния для AI-Работы над ошибками
  const [isPracticing, setIsPracticing] = useState(false);
  const [practiceLoading, setPracticeLoading] = useState(false);
  const [practiceQuestions, setPracticeQuestions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [currentQIndex, setCurrentQIndex] = useState(0);
  const [selectedOption, setSelectedOption] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showExplanation, setShowExplanation] = useState(false);
  const [correctCount, setCorrectCount] = useState(0);
  const [practiceFinished, setPracticeFinished] = useState(false);

  const startPractice = async () => {
    const openMistakes = mistakes.filter(m => !m.is_resolved);
    if (openMistakes.length === 0) return;
    
    // Берём до 5 неисправленных ошибок
    const toPractice = openMistakes.slice(0, 5);
    
    setPracticeLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/mistakes/practice', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ mistakes: toPractice }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
      const data = await res.json();
      setPracticeQuestions(data.practice || []);
      setIsPracticing(true);
      setCurrentQIndex(0);
      setSelectedOption(null);
      setShowExplanation(false);
      setCorrectCount(0);
      setPracticeFinished(false);
    } catch (e) {
      alert('Не удалось запустить работу над ошибками. Попробуйте еще раз.');
    } finally {
      setPracticeLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const checkAnswer = (opt: string) => {
    if (selectedOption) return; // Запрещаем повторный выбор
    setSelectedOption(opt);
    setShowExplanation(true);
    const q = practiceQuestions[currentQIndex];
    if (opt.startsWith(q.correct)) {
      setCorrectCount(prev => prev + 1);
    }
  };

  const nextQuestion = async () => {
    if (currentQIndex + 1 < practiceQuestions.length) {
      setCurrentQIndex(prev => prev + 1);
      setSelectedOption(null);
      setShowExplanation(false);
    } else {
      setPracticeFinished(true);
      
      const openMistakes = mistakes.filter(m => !m.is_resolved);
      const toPractice = openMistakes.slice(0, 5);
      const successRate = (correctCount + (selectedOption?.startsWith(practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.correct) ? 1 : 0)) / practiceQuestions.length;
      
      // Считаем финальный correctCount с учётом последнего вопроса, если он правильный
      const finalCorrect = correctCount + (selectedOption?.startsWith(practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.correct) ? 1 : 0);
      const finalRate = finalCorrect / practiceQuestions.length;

      if (finalRate >= 0.6) {
        const ids = toPractice.map(m => m.id);
        const resolvedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        
        await supabase
          .from('mistake_diary')
          .update({ is_resolved: true, resolved_at: resolvedAt })
          .in('id', ids);
          
        setMistakes(prev => prev.map(m => ids.includes(m.id) ? { ...m, is_resolved: true } : m));
        
        if (user) {
          await addXP(user.id, 50);
        }
        
        confetti({
          particleCount: 120,
          spread: 70,
          origin: { y: 0.6 }
        });
      }
    }
  };

  const fetchMistakes = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    setLoading(true);
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('mistake_diary')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', user.id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (!error && data) setMistakes(data);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchMistakes();
  }, [user?.id]);

  const markResolved = async (id: string) => {
    await supabase
      .from('mistake_diary')
      .update({ is_resolved: true, resolved_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('id', id);
    setMistakes(prev => prev.map(m => m.id === id ? { ...m, is_resolved: true } : m));
  };

  const deleteMistake = async (id: string) => {
    await supabase.from('mistake_diary').delete().eq('id', id);
    setMistakes(prev => prev.filter(m => m.id !== id));
  };

  const filtered = mistakes.filter(m => {
    if (filter === 'open') return !m.is_resolved;
    if (filter === 'resolved') return m.is_resolved;
    return true;
  });

  const openCount = mistakes.filter(m => !m.is_resolved).length;
  const resolvedCount = mistakes.filter(m => m.is_resolved).length;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
      {/* ═══ РЕЖИМ ПРАКТИКИ ════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      {isPracticing ? (
        <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto py-8">
          <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
            {!practiceFinished ? (
              <motion.div
                key={`q-${currentQIndex}`}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
                className="space-y-6"
              >
                {/* Хедер прогресса */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg">
                      Вопрос {currentQIndex + 1} из {practiceQuestions.length}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                      Верных ответов: {correctCount}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (confirm('Вы уверены, что хотите прервать работу над ошибками? Прогресс не сохранится.')) {
                        setIsPracticing(false);
                      }
                    }}
                    className="text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition"
                  >
                    Прервать
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Карточка вопроса */}
                <div className="card p-6 md:p-8 space-y-6">
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-800 leading-relaxed">
                    {practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.question}
                  </h3>

                  {/* Опции */}
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.options.map((opt: string) => {
                      const isSelected = selectedOption === opt;
                      const isCorrect = opt.startsWith(practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.correct);
                      const hasAnswered = selectedOption !== null;

                      let btnStyle = 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700';
                      if (hasAnswered) {
                        if (isCorrect) {
                          btnStyle = 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800';
                        } else if (isSelected) {
                          btnStyle = 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-300 text-rose-800';
                        } else {
                          btnStyle = 'border-slate-100 text-slate-400 opacity-60';
                        }
                      }

                      return (
                        <button
                          key={opt}
                          disabled={hasAnswered}
                          onClick={() => checkAnswer(opt)}
                          className={`w-full p-4 text-left text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl border-2 transition-all duration-200 ${btnStyle} flex justify-between items-center`}
                        >
                          <span>{opt}</span>
                          {hasAnswered && isCorrect && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold bg-emerald-600 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md shrink-0">Верно</span>
                          )}
                          {hasAnswered && isSelected && !isCorrect && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold bg-rose-600 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md shrink-0">Ошибка</span>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>

                  {/* Объяснение */}
                  {showExplanation && (
                    <motion.div
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                      style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}
                      className="p-4 bg-amber-50/50 border border-amber-100 rounded-xl space-y-1"
                    >
                      <h4 className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-amber-700">Разбор и пояснение</h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                        {practiceQuestions[currentQIndex]?.explanation}
                      </p>
                    </motion.div>
                  )}

                  {/* Кнопка далее */}
                  {selectedOption && (
                    <motion.button
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 5 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                      onClick={nextQuestion}
                      className="w-full py-3.5 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100"
                    >
                      {currentQIndex + 1 < practiceQuestions.length ? 'Следующий вопрос' : 'Завершить практику'}
                    </motion.button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            ) : (
              <motion.div
                key="finished"
                initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                className="card p-8 md:p-10 text-center space-y-6"
              >
                {/* Иконка */}
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-100 to-rose-100">
                  <Zap className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-500 animate-bounce" />
                </div>

                {/* Тексты результатов */}
                {correctCount / practiceQuestions.length >= 0.6 ? (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-800">Отличная работа! 🎉</h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto">
                      Вы ответили верно на {correctCount} из {practiceQuestions.length} вопросов. AI подтверждает: ваши слабые места усвоены!
                    </p>
                    <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs font-bold rounded-full px-4 py-2 mt-2">
                      <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-ping" />
                      Ошибки исправлены &amp; Получено +50 XP
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">Попробуйте еще раз 💔</h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto">
                      Вы ответили верно на {correctCount} из {practiceQuestions.length} вопросов. Нужно хотя бы 2 правильных ответа, чтобы исправить ошибки в дневнике.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Кнопка выхода */}
                <button
                  onClick={() => {
                    setIsPracticing(false);
                    setPracticeFinished(false);
                    fetchMistakes();
                  }}
                  className="px-8 py-3 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs font-black rounded-xl transition"
                >
                  Вернуться в дневник
                </button>
              </motion.div>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* ═══ ОБЫЧНЫЙ РЕЖИМ ДНЕВНИКА ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */
        <>
          {/* Заголовок */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-amber-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
              <BookMarked className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Дневник ошибок</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Все слабые места после AI-Экзаменатора</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Кнопка работы над ошибками */}
          {openCount > 0 && (
            <button
              onClick={startPractice}
              disabled={practiceLoading}
              className="w-full mb-6 py-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-rose-600 text-white rounded-2xl font-black text-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-rose-100"
            >
              {practiceLoading ? (
                <>
                  <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                  AI анализирует ошибки и готовит тест...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Zap className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-200 animate-pulse" />
                  ⚡ Запустить AI-работу над ошибками (+50 XP)
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          )}

          {/* Статистика */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-6">
            <div className="card p-4 text-center">
              <p className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">{mistakes.length}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Всего ошибок</p>
            </div>
            <div className="card p-4 text-center border-l-4 border-l-rose-400">
              <p className="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600">{openCount}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Требуют внимания</p>
            </div>
            <div className="card p-4 text-center border-l-4 border-l-emerald-400">
              <p className="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-600">{resolvedCount}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Исправлено</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Фильтры */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <Filter className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
            {(['all', 'open', 'resolved'] as const).map(f => (
              <button
                key={f}
                onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition ${
                  filter === f
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                {f === 'all' ? 'Все' : f === 'open' ? '🔴 Открытые' : '✅ Исправленные'}
              </button>
            ))}
            <button
              onClick={fetchMistakes}
              className="ml-auto p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 transition"
              title="Обновить"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Список ошибок */}
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="card p-10 text-center">
              <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-emerald-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-7 h-7 text-emerald-500" />
              </div>
              <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                {filter === 'open' ? 'Отличная работа!' : 'Пока пусто'}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">
                {filter === 'open'
                  ? 'Все ошибки исправлены. Продолжайте в том же духе!'
                  : 'Пройдите AI-Экзамен, и ваши ошибки появятся здесь.'}
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <AnimatePresence>
                {filtered.map(mistake => (
                  <motion.div
                    key={mistake.id}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0, margin: 0 }}
                    className={`card overflow-hidden ${
                      mistake.is_resolved ? 'opacity-60' : ''
                    }`}
                  >
                    {/* Заголовок карточки */}
                    <div
                      className="p-4 cursor-pointer flex items-start gap-3"
                      onClick={() => setExpandedId(expandedId === mistake.id ? null : mistake.id)}
                    >
                      <div className={`mt-0.5 shrink-0 w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${
                        mistake.is_resolved ? 'bg-emerald-100' : 'bg-rose-100'
                      }`}>
                        {mistake.is_resolved
                          ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                          : <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-600" />
                        }
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 leading-snug line-clamp-2">
                          {mistake.question}
                        </p>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                          {mistake.topic && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium">
                              {mistake.topic}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                            {new Date(mistake.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0 transition-transform ${
                        expandedId === mistake.id ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                      }`} />
                    </div>

                    {/* Раскрытое содержимое */}
                    <AnimatePresence>
                      {expandedId === mistake.id && (
                        <motion.div
                          initial={{ height: 0 }}
                          animate={{ height: 'auto' }}
                          exit={{ height: 0 }}
                          className="overflow-hidden"
                        >
                          <div className="px-4 pb-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-3 space-y-3">
                            {/* Ответ студента */}
                            <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-100 rounded-xl">
                              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-rose-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                                Мой ответ
                              </p>
                              <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">
                                {mistake.student_answer || '— (пусто)'}
                              </p>
                            </div>

                            {/* Правильный ответ */}
                            <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 rounded-xl">
                              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                                Правильный ответ
                              </p>
                              <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">
                                {mistake.correct_answer}
                              </p>
                            </div>

                            {/* Действия */}
                            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
                              {!mistake.is_resolved && (
                                <button
                                  onClick={() => markResolved(mistake.id)}
                                  className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition"
                                >
                                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                  Отметить как исправленную
                                </button>
                              )}
                              <button
                                onClick={() => deleteMistake(mistake.id)}
                                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 text-slate-500 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition"
                              >
                                <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                Удалить
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </motion.div>
                      )}
                    </AnimatePresence>
                  </motion.div>
                ))}
              </AnimatePresence>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/MoodTracker.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Heart, Zap, Brain, Coffee, Frown, Smile, Meh, SmilePlus, CheckCircle2, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { updateQuestProgress } from '../api/quests';

type MoodType = 'great' | 'good' | 'okay' | 'tired' | 'stressed';

interface MoodEntry {
  id: string;
  mood: MoodType;
  energy_level: number;
  notes: string;
  recommended_mode: string;
  logged_at: string;
}

interface MoodConfig {
  label: string;
  emoji: string;
  color: string;
  bg: string;
  border: string;
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}

const MOODS: Record<MoodType, MoodConfig> = {
  great: { label: 'Отлично!', emoji: '🚀', color: 'text-emerald-700', bg: 'bg-emerald-50', border: 'border-emerald-300', icon: SmilePlus },
  good: { label: 'Хорошо', emoji: '😊', color: 'text-indigo-700', bg: 'bg-indigo-50', border: 'border-indigo-300', icon: Smile },
  okay: { label: 'Нормально', emoji: '😐', color: 'text-amber-700', bg: 'bg-amber-50', border: 'border-amber-300', icon: Meh },
  tired: { label: 'Устал(а)', emoji: '😴', color: 'text-slate-700', bg: 'bg-slate-100', border: 'border-slate-300', icon: Coffee },
  stressed: { label: 'Стресс', emoji: '😰', color: 'text-rose-700', bg: 'bg-rose-50', border: 'border-rose-300', icon: Frown },
};

const ENERGY_LABELS: Record<number, string> = {
  1: 'Совсем нет сил',
  2: 'Слабая энергия',
  3: 'Средняя',
  4: 'Бодрый(ая)',
  5: 'Полон(на) сил!'
};

function getRecommendation(mood: MoodType, energy: number): string {
  if (mood === 'great' && energy >= 4) {
    return '🔥 Режим «Глубокое погружение» — идеальный день для изучения сложных тем и AI-Экзаменатора!';
  }
  if ((mood === 'good' || mood === 'great') && energy >= 3) {
    return '📚 Режим «Активное обучение» — хорошо зайдёт изучение новых материалов и интерактивные миссии.';
  }
  if (mood === 'okay' || energy === 3) {
    return '🔄 Режим «Повторение» — самое время для спейсд-репетишн и флэш-карточек. Никаких подвигов!';
  }
  if (mood === 'tired' || energy <= 2) {
    return '🌙 Режим «Лёгкий просмотр» — просто перечитай конспект или послушай AI-чат. Мозг сегодня заслужил отдых.';
  }
  if (mood === 'stressed') {
    return '🧘 Режим «Анти-стресс» — начни с планировщика, разбей задачи на мелкие шаги и сделай только одно дело.';
  }
  return '📖 Выбери любой режим учёбы, который кажется тебе комфортным сегодня.';
}

export default function MoodTracker() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<'check' | 'form' | 'done'>('check');
  const [todayEntry, setTodayEntry] = useState<MoodEntry | null>(null);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<MoodEntry[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Form state
  const [selectedMood, setSelectedMood] = useState<MoodType | null>(null);
  const [energy, setEnergy] = useState(3);
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    loadData();
  }, [user?.id]);

  const loadData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('mood_log')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', user!.id)
      .order('logged_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(14);

    if (data) {
      const todayLog = data.find(d => d.logged_at === today);
      if (todayLog) {
        setTodayEntry(todayLog);
        setPhase('done');
      } else {
        setPhase('form');
      }
      setHistory(data.filter(d => d.logged_at !== today));
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const saveMood = async () => {
    if (!selectedMood || !user) return;
    setSaving(true);

    const rec = getRecommendation(selectedMood, energy);

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('mood_log')
      .insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        mood: selectedMood,
        energy_level: energy,
        notes: notes.trim(),
        recommended_mode: rec,
        logged_at: today,
      })
      .select()
      .single();

    if (!error && data) {
      setTodayEntry(data);
      setPhase('done');
      await updateQuestProgress(user.id, 'mood', 1);
    }
    setSaving(false);
  };

  const moodToEmoji = (m: MoodType) => MOODS[m]?.emoji ?? '•';
  const energyToBar = (e: number) => Math.round((e / 5) * 100);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
      {/* Заголовок */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-pink-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
          <Heart className="w-5 h-5 text-pink-600" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Трекер состояния</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Как ты себя чувствуешь? ИИ подберёт режим учёбы</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ФОРМА ВВОДА НАСТРОЕНИЯ */}
        {phase === 'form' && (
          <motion.div
            key="form"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -15 }}
            className="space-y-5"
          >
            <div className="card p-6">
              <h2 className="font-bold text-slate-900 mb-1">Как ты сегодня?</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-5">Выбери своё состояние прямо сейчас</p>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-2 mb-6">
                {(Object.entries(MOODS) as [MoodType, MoodConfig][]).map(([key, cfg]) => {
                  const Icon = cfg.icon;
                  const isSelected = selectedMood === key;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={key}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedMood(key)}
                      className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 p-3 rounded-xl border-2 transition-all ${
                        isSelected
                          ? `${cfg.bg} ${cfg.border} scale-105 shadow-md`
                          : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="text-2xl">{cfg.emoji}</span>
                      <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold ${isSelected ? cfg.color : 'text-slate-500'}`}>
                        {cfg.label}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Уровень энергии */}
              <div className="mb-5">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Уровень энергии
                  </label>
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-600">
                    {ENERGY_LABELS[energy]}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Zap className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-300" />
                  <input
                    type="range"
                    min={1}
                    max={5}
                    value={energy}
                    onChange={e => setEnergy(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="flex-1 accent-indigo-600 h-2 rounded-full cursor-pointer"
                  />
                  <Zap className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between mt-1">
                  {[1,2,3,4,5].map(n => (
                    <span key={n} className={`text-[10px] font-bold ${energy === n ? 'text-indigo-600' : 'text-slate-300'}`}>{n}</span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Заметка */}
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-2">
                  Заметка (необязательно)
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={notes}
                  onChange={e => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Что беспокоит? Есть дедлайн? Хочется конкретного?"
                  className="w-full h-20 px-4 py-3 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition resize-none bg-slate-50/50"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Превью рекомендации */}
              {selectedMood && (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                  style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}
                  className="mt-4 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 rounded-xl"
                >
                  <div className="p-3.5">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Brain className="w-3 h-3" /> Рекомендация ИИ:
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-indigo-800 font-medium leading-snug">
                      {getRecommendation(selectedMood, energy)}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              )}

              <button
                disabled={!selectedMood || saving}
                onClick={saveMood}
                className="mt-5 w-full py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100"
              >
                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                {saving ? 'Сохраняем...' : 'Получить план на сегодня'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* РЕЗУЛЬТАТ ДНЯ */}
        {phase === 'done' && todayEntry && (
          <motion.div
            key="done"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-5"
          >
            {/* Сегодняшний результат */}
            <div className={`card p-6 border-l-4 ${MOODS[todayEntry.mood]?.border ?? 'border-l-indigo-400'}`}>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4">
                <span className="text-4xl">{moodToEmoji(todayEntry.mood)}</span>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Сегодня ты сказал(а)</p>
                  <h2 className={`text-lg font-black ${MOODS[todayEntry.mood]?.color ?? 'text-slate-900'}`}>
                    {MOODS[todayEntry.mood]?.label}
                  </h2>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">Энергия:</span>
                    <div className="w-20 h-1.5 bg-slate-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div
                        className="h-full bg-indigo-500 rounded-full transition-all"
                        style={{ width: `${energyToBar(todayEntry.energy_level)}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-600">{todayEntry.energy_level}/5</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Рекомендация */}
              <div className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-50 to-purple-50 border border-indigo-100 rounded-xl">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Brain className="w-3 h-3" /> Рекомендованный режим учёбы:
                </p>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-800 font-medium leading-relaxed">
                  {todayEntry.recommended_mode}
                </p>
              </div>

              {todayEntry.notes && (
                <div className="mt-3 p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl">
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 italic">«{todayEntry.notes}»</p>
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                onClick={() => { setSelectedMood(null); setEnergy(3); setNotes(''); setPhase('form'); }}
                className="mt-4 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 flex items-center gap-1 transition"
              >
                Изменить состояние на сегодня
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* История последних 14 дней */}
            {history.length > 0 && (
              <div className="card p-5">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm mb-4">История настроений (последние 14 дней)</h3>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {history.slice(0, 7).map(entry => (
                    <div key={entry.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 py-2 border-b border-slate-50 last:border-0">
                      <span className="text-lg w-8 text-center">{moodToEmoji(entry.mood)}</span>
                      <div className="flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">
                            {MOODS[entry.mood]?.label}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                            {new Date(entry.logged_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="w-full h-1 bg-slate-100 rounded-full mt-1">
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-indigo-400 rounded-full"
                            style={{ width: `${energyToBar(entry.energy_level)}%` }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Profile.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { getProfile, updateProfile, UserProfile } from '../api/social';
import { getFriends, sendFriendRequest, respondFriendRequest, Friendship } from '../api/chat';
import { UserCircle2, GraduationCap, Trophy, BookOpen, Loader2, UserPlus, Check, Edit3, X, UserCheck, UserX } from 'lucide-react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';

export default function Profile() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { id: profileId } = useParams();
  const targetId = profileId || user?.id;
  
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  
  const [friendStatus, setFriendStatus] = useState<'none' | 'pending' | 'accepted'>('none');
  const [incomingRequests, setIncomingRequests] = useState<Friendship[]>([]);
  
  const isMyProfile = user?.id === targetId;

  // Edit State
  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState({ full_name: '', university: '', bio: '', avatar_url: '' });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!targetId) return;
    
    getProfile(targetId).then(p => {
      setProfile(p);
      if (p) {
        setEditForm({
          full_name: p.full_name || '',
          university: p.university || '',
          bio: p.bio || '',
          avatar_url: p.avatar_url || ''
        });
      }
      setLoading(false);
    });

    if (user) {
      getFriends(user.id).then(friends => {
        if (!isMyProfile) {
          const friendship = friends.find(f => f.requester_id === targetId || f.addressee_id === targetId);
          if (friendship) setFriendStatus(friendship.status as 'pending' | 'accepted');
        } else {
          // Входящие заявки мне
          const incoming = friends.filter(f => f.addressee_id === user.id && f.status === 'pending');
          setIncomingRequests(incoming);
        }
      });
    }
  }, [targetId, user, isMyProfile]);

  const handleAddFriend = async () => {
    if (!user || !targetId) return;
    const ok = await sendFriendRequest(user.id, targetId);
    if (ok) setFriendStatus('pending');
  };

  const handleRespondRequest = async (id: string, status: 'accepted' | 'declined') => {
    await respondFriendRequest(id, status);
    setIncomingRequests(prev => prev.filter(req => req.id !== id));
  };

  const handleSaveProfile = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    const ok = await updateProfile(user.id, editForm);
    if (ok) {
      setProfile(prev => prev ? { ...prev, ...editForm } : null);
      setIsEditing(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="flex min-h-[85vh] items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-500 animate-spin" /></div>;
  if (!profile) return <div className="p-8 text-center text-slate-500">Профиль не найден.</div>;

  return (
    <div className="p-8 max-w-4xl mx-auto min-h-[85vh]">
      <motion.div 
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-8 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden mb-8"
      >
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-32 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-purple-600" />
        
        <div className="relative pt-12 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center md:items-end gap-6 mb-8">
          {profile.avatar_url ? (
            <img src={profile.avatar_url} alt="avatar" className="w-32 h-32 rounded-full border-4 border-white shadow-lg bg-white object-cover" />
          ) : (
            <div className="w-32 h-32 rounded-full border-4 border-white shadow-lg bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center">
              <UserCircle2 className="w-16 h-16 text-slate-400" />
            </div>
          )}
          
          <div className="flex-1 text-center md:text-left mb-2">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">{profile.full_name || 'Студент'}</h1>
            {profile.university && (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center md:justify-start gap-2 text-slate-500 mt-2 font-medium">
                <GraduationCap className="w-5 h-5" />
                {profile.university}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {!isMyProfile ? (
            <div className="mb-2">
              {friendStatus === 'none' && (
                <button onClick={handleAddFriend} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 hover:bg-indigo-700 transition shadow-sm">
                  <UserPlus className="w-5 h-5" /> Добавить в друзья
                </button>
              )}
              {friendStatus === 'pending' && (
                <button disabled className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 cursor-not-allowed">
                  <Check className="w-5 h-5" /> Заявка отправлена
                </button>
              )}
              {friendStatus === 'accepted' && (
                <button className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Check className="w-5 h-5" /> Уже в друзьях
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="mb-2">
              <button onClick={() => setIsEditing(!isEditing)} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-white text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2 hover:bg-slate-50 transition shadow-sm">
                {isEditing ? <X className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Edit3 className="w-4 h-4" />}
                {isEditing ? 'Отмена' : 'Редактировать'}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Форма редактирования */}
        {isEditing && (
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }} className="mb-8 p-6 bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 mb-4">Редактирование профиля</h3>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Имя и Фамилия</label>
                <input type="text" value={editForm.full_name} onChange={e => setEditForm({...editForm, full_name: e.target.value})} className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Университет / Школа</label>
                <input type="text" value={editForm.university} onChange={e => setEditForm({...editForm, university: e.target.value})} className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Ссылка на аватарку (URL)</label>
                <input type="text" value={editForm.avatar_url} onChange={e => setEditForm({...editForm, avatar_url: e.target.value})} className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none" placeholder="https://example.com/avatar.jpg" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">О себе (Био)</label>
                <textarea value={editForm.bio} onChange={e => setEditForm({...editForm, bio: e.target.value})} className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none" rows={3} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
                <button onClick={handleSaveProfile} className="px-6 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-indigo-700">Сохранить</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {!isEditing && profile.bio && (
          <div className="mb-8 p-5 bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">
            {profile.bio}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <div className="p-5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center shadow-sm">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-indigo-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <Trophy className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">{profile.streak_days || 0}</div>
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">Дней подряд</div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="p-5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center shadow-sm">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-emerald-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">{profile.total_materials || 0}</div>
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">Конспектов</div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center shadow-sm">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-amber-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <GraduationCap className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">{profile.total_reviews || 0}</div>
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">Повторений</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </motion.div>

      {/* Входящие заявки в друзья */}
      {isMyProfile && incomingRequests.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-4">Входящие заявки в друзья ({incomingRequests.length})</h2>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {incomingRequests.map(req => (
              <div key={req.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  {req.friend_avatar ? (
                    <img src={req.friend_avatar} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover" alt="avatar" />
                  ) : (
                    <UserCircle2 className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-400" />
                  )}
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-800">{req.friend_name}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => handleRespondRequest(req.id, 'accepted')} className="p-2 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-200 rounded-lg transition" title="Принять">
                    <UserCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => handleRespondRequest(req.id, 'declined')} className="p-2 bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-200 rounded-lg transition" title="Отклонить">
                    <UserX className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/UpdatePassword.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { GraduationCap, Lock, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';

export default function UpdatePassword() {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    // Listen for auth state changes to make sure we are handling the password recovery event
    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(
      async (event, session) => {
        if (event == 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY') {
          // User is ready to update their password
        }
      }
    );
    return () => {
      authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  const handleUpdate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    if (password.length < 6) {
      setError('Пароль должен содержать минимум 6 символов');
      return;
    }
    
    if (password !== confirm) {
      setError('Пароли не совпадают');
      return;
    }
    
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({
      password: password
    });
    
    if (error) {
      setError('Произошла ошибка при обновлении пароля.');
    } else {
      setSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        navigate('/dashboard');
      }, 2000);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f8fafc] flex">
      {/* Left Column: Form */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center px-6 sm:px-12 lg:px-24 xl:px-32 relative">
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/')} className="absolute top-8 left-6 sm:left-12 flex items-center gap-2 group">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-indigo-600 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center shadow-md">
            <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-lg tracking-tight group-hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors">StudyForge</span>
        </button>

        <motion.div 
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
          className="w-full max-w-sm mx-auto"
        >
          <div className="mb-8">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight mb-2">Новый пароль</h1>
            <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Придумайте надежный пароль для вашего аккаунта</p>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }} className="bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-600 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium mb-6">
              {error}
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {success ? (
            <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }} className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-600 px-4 py-6 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold mb-6 flex flex-col items-center text-center gap-3 shadow-sm shadow-emerald-100">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-1">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <p>Пароль успешно изменён! Перенаправляем...</p>
            </motion.div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleUpdate} className="space-y-5">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Новый пароль</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                    <Lock className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="block w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-900 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm"
                    placeholder="••••••••"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Подтвердите пароль</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                    <Lock className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400" />
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    value={confirm}
                    onChange={e => setConfirm(e.target.value)}
                    className="block w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-900 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 outline-none transition-all shadow-sm"
                    placeholder="••••••••"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="group relative w-full flex justify-center py-3.5 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-white bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition-all shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 hover:shadow-indigo-300 disabled:opacity-70 mt-4"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2">Сохраняем... <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white rounded-full animate-spin" /></span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2">Сохранить <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" /></span>
                )}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}
        </motion.div>
      </div>

      {/* Right Column: Branded Visual (Hidden on mobile) */}
      <div className="hidden lg:flex flex-1 relative bg-indigo-600 overflow-hidden items-center justify-center p-12">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 to-indigo-800" />
        <div className="absolute top-[-10%] right-[-10%] w-96 h-96 bg-white/10 rounded-full blur-3xl" />
        
        <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-10" style={{ backgroundImage: 'radial-gradient(white 1px, transparent 1px)', backgroundSize: '24px 24px' }} />

        <motion.div 
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.9, rotateY: -15 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, rotateY: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.8, type: "spring" }}
          className="relative w-full max-w-md bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/20 rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-2xl"
          style={{ transformStyle: "preserve-3d", perspective: 1000 }}
        >
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-white/20 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-6 border border-white/20 shadow-inner">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
          </div>
          
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-white leading-tight mb-4 tracking-tight">
            Финальный шаг
          </h2>
          
          <p className="text-indigo-100 leading-relaxed text-sm mb-6">
            Ваши данные будут надежно зашифрованы. Для большей безопасности используйте разные пароли на разных сервисах.
          </p>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
