FILE: frontend/src/pages/SocialHub.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Globe, Trophy, Heart, Download, UserCircle2, Loader2, Search, X, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { getLeaderboard, getPublicMaterials, toggleLike, copyMaterialToLibrary, LeaderboardEntry, PublicMaterial } from '../api/social';
import { searchUsers } from '../api/chat';

export default function SocialHub() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'feed' | 'leaderboard' | 'search'>('feed');

  const [feed, setFeed] = useState<PublicMaterial[]>([]);
  const [leaderboard, setLeaderboard] = useState<LeaderboardEntry[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Поиск пользователей
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);

  // Уведомления о копировании
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    setLoading(true);
    if (activeTab === 'feed') {
      getPublicMaterials(user.id).then(data => { setFeed(data); setLoading(false); });
    } else if (activeTab === 'leaderboard') {
      getLeaderboard().then(data => { setLeaderboard(data); setLoading(false); });
    } else {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [user, activeTab]);

  // Поиск с дебаунсом
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user || activeTab !== 'search') return;
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) { setSearchResults([]); return; }
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      setSearching(true);
      const results = await searchUsers(searchQuery, user.id);
      setSearchResults(results);
      setSearching(false);
    }, 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [searchQuery, user, activeTab]);

  const handleLike = async (m: PublicMaterial) => {
    if (!user) return;
    const isLiked = m.is_liked_by_me;
    setFeed(prev => prev.map(item =>
      item.id === m.id ? { ...item, is_liked_by_me: !isLiked, likes_count: item.likes_count + (isLiked ? -1 : 1) } : item
    ));
    await toggleLike(user.id, m.id, !!isLiked);
  };

  const handleCopy = async (m: PublicMaterial) => {
    if (!user) return;
    const ok = await copyMaterialToLibrary(m.id, user.id);
    if (ok) {
      setCopiedId(m.id);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedId(null), 3000);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-8 max-w-5xl mx-auto min-h-[85vh]">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-8">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 tracking-tight flex items-center gap-3">
            <Globe className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-600" />
            Study Network
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 mt-1">Глобальное сообщество студентов</p>
        </div>

        {/* Табы */}
        <div className="flex bg-slate-200 p-1 rounded-xl">
          {[
            { key: 'feed', label: 'Маркетплейс' },
            { key: 'leaderboard', label: 'Лидерборд' },
            { key: 'search', label: '🔍 Найти' },
          ].map(tab => (
            <button
              key={tab.key}
              onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.key as any)}
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition-colors ${
                activeTab === tab.key ? 'bg-white text-indigo-600 shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700'
              }`}
            >
              {tab.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Поиск пользователей ── */}
      {activeTab === 'search' && (
        <div>
          <div className="relative mb-6">
            <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-5 h-5 text-slate-400" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Найдите студентов по имени..."
              className="w-full pl-12 pr-4 py-3.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/50 text-slate-900 shadow-sm"
              autoFocus
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')} className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600">
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {searching && <div className="flex justify-center py-10"><Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-500 animate-spin" /></div>}

          {!searching && searchResults.length === 0 && searchQuery && (
            <p className="text-center text-slate-400 py-10">Никого не найдено по запросу «{searchQuery}»</p>
          )}

          {!searching && searchResults.length === 0 && !searchQuery && (
            <p className="text-center text-slate-400 py-10">Введите имя студента, чтобы найти его</p>
          )}

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {searchResults.map(u => (
              <motion.div
                key={u.id}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 5 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow"
              >
                <button onClick={() => navigate(`/profile/${u.id}`)} className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-1 text-left">
                  {u.avatar_url ? (
                    <img src={u.avatar_url} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover" alt="avatar" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-slate-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                      <UserCircle2 className="w-7 h-7 text-slate-400" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-slate-900">{u.full_name}</div>
                    {u.university && <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">{u.university}</div>}
                  </div>
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => navigate(`/profile/${u.id}`)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 font-bold text-sm rounded-xl hover:bg-indigo-100 transition"
                >
                  Профиль →
                </button>
              </motion.div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Лента / Лидерборд ── */}
      {activeTab !== 'search' && (
        loading ? (
          <div className="flex justify-center py-20">
            <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-500 animate-spin" />
          </div>
        ) : activeTab === 'feed' ? (
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            {feed.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="col-span-2 text-center py-20 text-slate-500">
                <Globe className="w-12 h-12 text-slate-200 mx-auto mb-4" />
                Пока никто не опубликовал материалы. Откройте свои в Библиотеке!
              </div>
            ) : (
              feed.map((m, i) => (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  transition={{ delay: i * 0.04 }}
                  key={m.id}
                  className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center mb-4">
                    <button onClick={() => navigate(`/profile/${m.user_id}`)} className="flex items-center gap-3 group flex-1">
                      {m.author_avatar ? (
                        <img src={m.author_avatar} alt="avatar" className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-slate-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                          <UserCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-400" />
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div className="text-left">
                        <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors text-sm">{m.author_name}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">{new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ru')}</div>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mb-5">
                    <div className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{m.subject || 'Без предмета'}</div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 line-clamp-2">{m.title}</h3>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-slate-100">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleLike(m)}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-2 font-medium transition-colors text-sm ${
                        m.is_liked_by_me ? 'text-pink-600' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-pink-600'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <Heart className={`w-4 h-4 ${m.is_liked_by_me ? 'fill-pink-600' : ''}`} />
                      {m.likes_count}
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleCopy(m)}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-sm transition-colors px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${
                        copiedId === m.id
                          ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700'
                          : 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-100'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {copiedId === m.id ? (
                        <><CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" /> Сохранено!</>
                      ) : (
                        <><Download className="w-4 h-4" /> Сохранить себе</>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Лидерборд */
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
            <div className="p-6 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Trophy className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-500" />
                Топ 50 студентов
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {leaderboard.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-10 text-center text-slate-500">Нет данных для рейтинга.</div>
              ) : (
                leaderboard.map((u, i) => (
                  <div key={u.id} className="p-4 sm:px-6 flex items-center gap-4 hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors">
                    <div className={`w-8 font-black text-center shrink-0 ${
                      i === 0 ? 'text-amber-500 text-2xl' :
                      i === 1 ? 'text-slate-400 text-xl' :
                      i === 2 ? 'text-amber-700 text-xl' : 'text-slate-300 text-base'
                    }`}>
                      {i < 3 ? ['🥇','🥈','🥉'][i] : `#${i+1}`}
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={() => navigate(`/profile/${u.id}`)} className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1 text-left">
                      {u.avatar_url ? (
                        <img src={u.avatar_url} alt="avatar" className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full object-cover shadow-sm" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-11 h-11 bg-slate-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
                          <UserCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-500" />
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors">{u.full_name}</div>
                        <div className="text-sm font-medium text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md inline-block mt-0.5">
                          {u.total_xp.toLocaleString()} XP
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/QuickPrep.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Zap, BookOpen, HelpCircle, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, ArrowRight, ChevronRight, X, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';

interface QuickPrepData {
  key_points: string[];
  cheatsheet: string;
  questions: { question: string; answer: string }[];
}

type Phase = 'select' | 'loading' | 'cheatsheet' | 'quiz' | 'done';

export default function QuickPrep() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // Восстановление состояния из sessionStorage
  const savedStateStr = sessionStorage.getItem('studyforge_quickprep_state');
  const savedState = savedStateStr ? JSON.parse(savedStateStr) : null;

  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string>(savedState?.selectedId || '');
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>(
    savedState?.phase === 'loading' ? 'select' : (savedState?.phase || 'select')
  );
  const [data, setData] = useState<QuickPrepData | null>(savedState?.data || null);
  const [quizIndex, setQuizIndex] = useState<number>(savedState?.quizIndex || 0);
  const [showAnswer, setShowAnswer] = useState<boolean>(savedState?.showAnswer || false);
  const [timer, setTimer] = useState<number>(savedState?.timer !== undefined ? savedState.timer : 300); // 5 минут

  // Сохранение состояния в sessionStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    sessionStorage.setItem('studyforge_quickprep_state', JSON.stringify({
      selectedId,
      phase,
      data,
      quizIndex,
      showAnswer,
      timer
    }));
  }, [selectedId, phase, data, quizIndex, showAnswer, timer]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    getMaterials(user.id).then(setMaterials).catch(console.error);
  }, [user?.id]);

  // Таймер
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== 'cheatsheet' && phase !== 'quiz') return;
    if (timer <= 0) return;
    const t = setInterval(() => setTimer(p => p - 1), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [phase, timer]);

  const selectedMaterial = materials.find(m => m.id === selectedId);

  const startPrep = async () => {
    if (!selectedMaterial) return;
    setPhase('loading');
    setTimer(300);
    setQuizIndex(0);
    setShowAnswer(false);

    const summary = typeof selectedMaterial.summary_json === 'string'
      ? selectedMaterial.summary_json
      : (selectedMaterial.summary_json?.content || '');
    const context = (selectedMaterial.original_text || summary || '').slice(0, 15000);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/quick-prep', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ context }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
      const d = await res.json();
      setData(d);
      setPhase('cheatsheet');
    } catch {
      alert('Не удалось подготовить материал. Попробуйте ещё раз.');
      setPhase('select');
    }
  };

  const formatTime = (s: number) => `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col justify-center">
      {/* Заголовок */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6 justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-amber-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
            <Zap className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Повтор за 5 минут</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Быстрое освежение памяти перед парой</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {phase !== 'select' && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold ${timer < 60 ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700'}`}>
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
              {formatTime(timer)}
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setPhase('select');
                setSelectedId('');
                setData(null);
                setQuizIndex(0);
                setShowAnswer(false);
                setTimer(300);
              }}
              className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 rounded-lg text-slate-600 transition"
            >
              <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Выйти
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {/* ВЫБОР МАТЕРИАЛА */}
        {phase === 'select' && (
          <motion.div
            key="select"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -15 }}
            className="card p-8 text-center"
          >
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-amber-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Zap className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-500" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Осталось 5 минут до пары?</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 max-w-sm mx-auto mb-6">
              Выберите лекцию — AI мгновенно создаст шпаргалку и 2 разогревочных вопроса. Идеально для освежения памяти.
            </p>
            <div className="max-w-sm mx-auto space-y-4">
              <select
                value={selectedId}
                onChange={e => setSelectedId(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 transition"
              >
                <option value="">Выберите лекцию</option>
                {materials.map(m => (
                  <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.title}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <button
                disabled={!selectedId}
                onClick={startPrep}
                className="w-full py-3.5 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-amber-100"
              >
                <Zap className="w-5 h-5" /> Подготовить за 5 минут
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ЗАГРУЗКА */}
        {phase === 'loading' && (
          <motion.div
            key="loading"
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center"
          >
            <div className="relative mb-6">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-amber-100 border-t-amber-500 rounded-full animate-spin" />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Zap className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-500 animate-pulse" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">AI составляет экспресс-шпаргалку...</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-xs">Выжимаем самое главное из лекции — займёт пару секунд</p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ШПАРГАЛКА */}
        {phase === 'cheatsheet' && data && (
          <motion.div
            key="cheatsheet"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.98 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-5"
          >
            {/* Прогресс */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              {[
                { label: '1. Шпаргалка', active: true, done: false },
                { label: '2. Вопросы', active: false, done: false },
                { label: '3. Готов!', active: false, done: false },
              ].map((step, i) => (
                <div key={i} className={`flex-1 text-center text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition ${step.active ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400'}`}>
                  {step.label}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Ключевые тезисы */}
            <div className="card p-5">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
                Топ-3 самых важных тезиса
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {data.key_points.map((point, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3">
                    <span className="w-6 h-6 bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-black shrink-0 mt-0.5">{i + 1}</span>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">{point}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Мини-шпаргалка */}
            <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl p-5">
              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">⚡ Экспресс-шпаргалка</p>
              <div className="text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed markdown-content white-text">
                <ReactMarkdown>{data.cheatsheet}</ReactMarkdown>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={() => { setPhase('quiz'); setQuizIndex(0); setShowAnswer(false); }}
              className="w-full py-3.5 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-amber-100"
            >
              Перейти к вопросам <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ВОПРОСЫ */}
        {phase === 'quiz' && data && (
          <motion.div
            key={`quiz-${quizIndex}`}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="space-y-5"
          >
            {/* Прогресс */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              {[
                { label: '1. Шпаргалка', active: false, done: true },
                { label: `2. Вопрос ${quizIndex + 1}/${data.questions.length}`, active: true, done: false },
                { label: '3. Готов!', active: false, done: false },
              ].map((step, i) => (
                <div key={i} className={`flex-1 text-center text-xs font-bold py-2 rounded-lg transition ${step.active ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white' : step.done ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400'}`}>
                  {step.done ? '✓ ' : ''}{step.label}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="card p-6 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500" />
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Разогревочный вопрос</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-800 leading-relaxed">
                {data.questions[quizIndex]?.question}
              </p>

              {!showAnswer ? (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setShowAnswer(true)}
                  className="w-full py-3 border-2 border-dashed border-amber-300 hover:border-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 rounded-xl font-bold transition text-sm"
                >
                  Показать ответ
                </button>
              ) : (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                  style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}
                  className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl"
                >
                  <div className="p-4">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Правильный ответ:
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">{data.questions[quizIndex]?.answer}</p>
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              )}

              {showAnswer && (
                <motion.button
                  initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (quizIndex + 1 >= data.questions.length) {
                      setPhase('done');
                    } else {
                      setQuizIndex(q => q + 1);
                      setShowAnswer(false);
                    }
                  }}
                  className="w-full py-3 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  {quizIndex + 1 >= data.questions.length ? 'Я готов! 🎯' : 'Следующий вопрос'} <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </motion.button>
              )}
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {/* ФИНАЛ */}
        {phase === 'done' && (
          <motion.div
            key="done"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            className="card p-10 text-center bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-50 to-orange-50 border-amber-100"
          >
            <div className="text-6xl mb-4">🎯</div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-amber-800 mb-2">Ты готов к паре!</h2>
            <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm max-w-sm mx-auto mb-6">
              Главное — помни ключевые тезисы. Ты потратил менее 5 минут и освежил память по «{selectedMaterial?.title}».
            </p>
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center">
              <button
                onClick={() => { setPhase('select'); setSelectedId(''); }}
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition"
              >
                Другая лекция
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')}
                className="px-6 py-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-slate-50 transition"
              >
                На дашборд
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/pages/Messages.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { getFriends, Friendship, getMessages, sendMessage, subscribeToMessages, DirectMessage } from '../api/chat';
import { Send, UserCircle2, MessageSquare, Loader2, BookOpen, Link2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { getMaterials } from '../api/materials';

export default function Messages() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [friends, setFriends] = useState<Friendship[]>([]);
  const [activeFriend, setActiveFriend] = useState<Friendship | null>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<DirectMessage[]>([]);
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showInvite, setShowInvite] = useState(false);
  const [myMaterials, setMyMaterials] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    getFriends(user.id).then(f => {
      setFriends(f.filter(x => x.status === 'accepted'));
      setLoading(false);
    });
    getMaterials(user.id).then(setMyMaterials);
  }, [user]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user || !activeFriend) return;
    const friendId = activeFriend.friend_id!;
    getMessages(user.id, friendId).then(setMessages);

    const unsubscribe = subscribeToMessages(user.id, (msg) => {
      if (
        (msg.sender_id === user.id && msg.receiver_id === friendId) ||
        (msg.sender_id === friendId && msg.receiver_id === user.id)
      ) {
        setMessages(prev => prev.find(m => m.id === msg.id) ? prev : [...prev, msg]);
      }
    });
    return () => { unsubscribe(); };
  }, [user, activeFriend]);

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  const handleSend = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!user || !activeFriend || !newMessage.trim()) return;
    const content = newMessage.trim();
    setNewMessage('');
    const msg = await sendMessage(user.id, activeFriend.friend_id!, content);
    if (msg) setMessages(prev => [...prev, msg]);
  };

  const handleSendInvite = async (material: any) => {
    if (!user || !activeFriend) return;
    const inviteText = `📚 Приглашаю изучить материал: «${material.title}» (${material.subject || 'Без предмета'}) — открой в своей библиотеке или найди в Study Network!`;
    setShowInvite(false);
    const msg = await sendMessage(user.id, activeFriend.friend_id!, inviteText);
    if (msg) setMessages(prev => [...prev, msg]);
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex h-[85vh] items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-500 animate-spin" /></div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-[85vh] bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden m-6">

      {/* Левая панель — список диалогов */}
      <div className="w-80 border-r border-slate-200 flex flex-col bg-slate-50 shrink-0">
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white">
          <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-2">
            <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-500" />
            Сообщения
          </h2>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {friends.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-6 text-center">
              <UserCircle2 className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-200 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">У вас пока нет друзей.</p>
              <button
                onClick={() => navigate('/network')}
                className="mt-3 text-sm text-indigo-600 font-bold hover:text-indigo-700"
              >
                Найти в Хабе →
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            friends.map(f => (
              <button
                key={f.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveFriend(f)}
                className={`w-full p-4 flex items-center gap-3 text-left transition-colors border-b border-slate-100 ${
                  activeFriend?.id === f.id ? 'bg-indigo-50' : 'hover:bg-white'
                }`}
              >
                {f.friend_avatar ? (
                  <img src={f.friend_avatar} alt="avatar" className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover shrink-0" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <UserCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-500" />
                  </div>
                )}
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm truncate">{f.friend_name}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">Нажмите, чтобы открыть чат</div>
                </div>
              </button>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Правая панель — чат */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col bg-white relative min-w-0">
        {activeFriend ? (
          <>
            {/* Хедер */}
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center gap-3 bg-white shadow-sm z-10">
              {activeFriend.friend_avatar ? (
                <img src={activeFriend.friend_avatar} alt="avatar" className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <UserCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-400" />
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex-1">
                <div className="font-bold text-slate-800">{activeFriend.friend_name}</div>
              </div>
              {/* Кнопка "Пригласить к материалу" */}
              <div className="relative">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setShowInvite(v => !v)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 transition px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg"
                  title="Поделиться материалом"
                >
                  <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
                  Поделиться материалом
                </button>

                {showInvite && (
                  <div className="absolute right-0 top-10 w-72 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-slate-200 z-20 overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="p-3 border-b border-slate-100">
                      <p className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-800">Отправить материал в чат</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-slate-50">
                      {myMaterials.length === 0 ? (
                        <p className="p-4 text-sm text-slate-400 text-center">У вас нет материалов</p>
                      ) : myMaterials.map(m => (
                        <button
                          key={m.id}
                          onClick={() => handleSendInvite(m)}
                          className="w-full p-3 text-left hover:bg-indigo-50 transition"
                        >
                          <div className="font-medium text-slate-800 text-sm truncate">{m.title}</div>
                          <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">{m.subject || 'Без предмета'}</div>
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Сообщения */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-3 bg-slate-50/50" onClick={() => setShowInvite(false)}>
              {messages.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 text-sm">
                  Напишите первое сообщение!
                </div>
              ) : (
                messages.map(msg => {
                  const isMe = msg.sender_id === user?.id;
                  const isInvite = msg.content.startsWith('📚');
                  return (
                    <motion.div
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 5 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                      key={msg.id}
                      className={`flex ${isMe ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}
                    >
                      <div className={`max-w-[72%] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-sm ${
                        isMe
                          ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-tr-sm'
                          : isInvite
                            ? 'bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-slate-800 rounded-tl-sm shadow-sm'
                            : 'bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-800 rounded-tl-sm shadow-sm'
                      }`}>
                        {isInvite && !isMe && (
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-600 font-bold text-xs mb-1">
                            <Link2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Приглашение к материалу
                          </div>
                        )}
                        {msg.content}
                        <div className={`text-[10px] mt-1 text-right ${isMe ? 'text-indigo-200' : 'text-slate-400'}`}>
                          {new Date(msg.created_at).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </motion.div>
                  );
                })
              )}
              <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
            </div>

            {/* Ввод сообщения */}
            <div className="p-4 bg-white border-t border-slate-200">
              <form onSubmit={handleSend} className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newMessage}
                  onChange={e => setNewMessage(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Написать сообщение..."
                  className="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/50 transition-all text-sm"
                />
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={!newMessage.trim()}
                  className="p-3 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl hover:bg-indigo-700 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors shadow-sm"
                >
                  <Send className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-400">
            <MessageSquare className="w-16 h-16 mb-4 text-slate-200" />
            <p className="font-medium">Выберите диалог слева</p>
            <p className="text-sm mt-1">чтобы начать общение</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/utils/validators.ts

const BAD_WORDS_ROOTS = [
  'fuck', 'shit', 'bitch', 'asshole', 'cunt', 'dick', 'pussy', 'whore', 'slut', 'fag', 'nigg', 'crap', 'bastard',
  'хуй', 'хуе', 'пизд', 'ебат', 'ебан', 'ёбан', 'бляд', 'блят', 'шлюх', 'мудак', 'мудил', 'пидор', 'педик', 'залуп', 'хуйн', 'гондон', 'гандон', 'сука', 'сучк', 'дроч', 'хер'
];

export const validateName = (name: string) => {
  if (!name) return '';
  const trimmed = name.trim();
  if (trimmed.length < 2) return 'Имя должно содержать минимум 2 символа';
  if (trimmed.length > 50) return 'Имя слишком длинное (макс. 50)';
  
  if (!/^[a-zA-Zа-яА-ЯёЁ\s\-]+$/.test(trimmed)) {
    return 'Разрешены только буквы, пробелы и дефисы (без цифр)';
  }

  const lowerName = trimmed.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-zа-яё]/g, '');
  for (const root of BAD_WORDS_ROOTS) {
    if (lowerName.includes(root)) {
      return 'Недопустимое имя пользователя';
    }
  }

  return '';
};

export const validateEmail = (email: string) => {
  if (!email) return '';
  const trimmed = email.trim();
  // Requires at least 2 characters for TLD, prevents .r
  if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[a-zA-Z]{2,}$/.test(trimmed)) {
    return 'Некорректный формат email';
  }

  const parts = trimmed.split('@');
  if (parts.length === 2) {
    const domain = parts[1].toLowerCase();
    
    // Smart Typo Detection for popular Russian and Global providers
    if (domain.startsWith('gmail.') && domain !== 'gmail.com') return 'Опечатка? Возможно, вы имели в виду gmail.com';
    if (domain.startsWith('mail.') && domain !== 'mail.ru' && domain !== 'email.com') return 'Опечатка? Возможно, вы имели в виду mail.ru';
    if (domain.startsWith('yandex.') && !['yandex.ru', 'yandex.com', 'yandex.kz', 'yandex.by'].includes(domain)) return 'Опечатка? Возможно, вы имели в виду yandex.ru';
    if (domain.startsWith('bk.') && domain !== 'bk.ru') return 'Опечатка? Возможно, вы имели в виду bk.ru';
    if (domain.startsWith('inbox.') && domain !== 'inbox.ru') return 'Опечатка? Возможно, вы имели в виду inbox.ru';
    if (domain.startsWith('list.') && domain !== 'list.ru') return 'Опечатка? Возможно, вы имели в виду list.ru';
    if (domain.startsWith('ya.') && domain !== 'ya.ru') return 'Опечатка? Возможно, вы имели в виду ya.ru';
    if (domain.startsWith('icloud.') && domain !== 'icloud.com') return 'Опечатка? Возможно, вы имели в виду icloud.com';
  }

  return '';
};

export const validatePassword = (pwd: string) => {
  if (!pwd) return '';
  if (pwd.length < 8) return `Минимум 8 символов (не хватает ${8 - pwd.length})`;
  if (!/[A-ZА-ЯЁ]/.test(pwd)) return 'Нужна хотя бы одна заглавная буква';
  if (!/[0-9]/.test(pwd)) return 'Нужна хотя бы одна цифра';
  return '';
};

// Helper for UI password strength bar
export const getPasswordStrength = (pwd: string): number => {
  if (!pwd) return 0;
  let score = 0;
  if (pwd.length >= 8) score++;
  if (/[A-ZА-ЯЁ]/.test(pwd)) score++;
  if (/[0-9]/.test(pwd)) score++;
  if (/[^a-zA-Z0-9а-яА-ЯёЁ]/.test(pwd)) score++; // Special char bonus
  return Math.min(3, score); // Max 3 for the UI
};

-- 1. materials — Проанализированные материалы
CREATE TABLE materials (
  id              uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title           text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  subject         text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  original_text   text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  summary_json    jsonb,
  flashcards_json jsonb,
  quiz_json       jsonb,
  created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE materials ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can read own materials" ON materials FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own materials" ON materials FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can update own materials" ON materials FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can delete own materials" ON materials FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_materials_user_id ON materials(user_id);

-- 2. profiles — Профиль пользователя (автосоздание при регистрации)
CREATE TABLE profiles (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  full_name        text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  streak_days      int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_active_date date,
  total_reviews    int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  total_materials  int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can read own profile" ON profiles FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can update own profile" ON profiles FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own profile" ON profiles FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = id);

-- 3. flashcard_progress — Spaced repetition (SM-2)
CREATE TABLE flashcard_progress (
  id              uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  material_id     uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES materials(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  question        text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  answer          text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  ease_factor     real NOT NULL DEFAULT 2.5,
  interval_days   int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  repetitions     int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  next_review     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_reviewed_at timestamptz
);
ALTER TABLE flashcard_progress ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can read own progress" ON flashcard_progress FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own progress" ON flashcard_progress FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can update own progress" ON flashcard_progress FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can delete own progress" ON flashcard_progress FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_flashcard_progress_user ON flashcard_progress(user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_flashcard_progress_next ON flashcard_progress(user_id, next_review);

-- 4. subjects — Предметы для планировщика
CREATE TABLE subjects (
  id         uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  name       text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  exam_date  date,
  color      text NOT NULL DEFAULT '#4F46E5',
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE subjects ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can read own subjects" ON subjects FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own subjects" ON subjects FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can update own subjects" ON subjects FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can delete own subjects" ON subjects FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_subjects_user ON subjects(user_id);

-- 5. study_plans — Планы подготовки
CREATE TABLE study_plans (
  id         uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title      text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  subject    text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  exam_date  date,
  notes      text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE study_plans ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can read own plans" ON study_plans FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own plans" ON study_plans FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can update own plans" ON study_plans FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can delete own plans" ON study_plans FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_study_plans_user ON study_plans(user_id);

-- 6. chat_messages — AI-чат по материалам
CREATE TABLE chat_messages (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id     uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  material_id uuid REFERENCES materials(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  role        text NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('user', 'assistant')),
  content     text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE chat_messages ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can read own messages" ON chat_messages FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own messages" ON chat_messages FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can delete own messages" ON chat_messages FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_chat_messages_user ON chat_messages(user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_chat_messages_material ON chat_messages(material_id);

-- 7. study_sessions — Сессии повторения (для streak и графиков)
CREATE TABLE study_sessions (
  id              uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  date            date NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
  cards_reviewed  int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  materials_added int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE(user_id, date)
);
ALTER TABLE study_sessions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can read own sessions" ON study_sessions FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own sessions" ON study_sessions FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can update own sessions" ON study_sessions FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users can delete own sessions" ON study_sessions FOR DELETE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_study_sessions_user ON study_sessions(user_id);

-- 8. exam_sessions — Сессии AI-экзаменатора
CREATE TABLE exam_sessions (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  material_id      uuid REFERENCES materials(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  subject          text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  questions_json   jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
  total_questions  int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  score            int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  status           text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'in_progress' CHECK (status IN ('in_progress', 'completed')),
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  completed_at     timestamptz
);
ALTER TABLE exam_sessions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can manage own exams" ON exam_sessions FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- 9. mistake_diary — Дневник ошибок
CREATE TABLE mistake_diary (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  material_id      uuid REFERENCES materials(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  exam_session_id  uuid REFERENCES exam_sessions(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  question         text NOT NULL,
  correct_answer   text NOT NULL,
  student_answer   text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  topic            text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  is_resolved      boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  resolved_at      timestamptz,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE mistake_diary ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can manage own mistakes" ON mistake_diary FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- 10. mood_log — Трекер настроения
CREATE TABLE mood_log (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  mood             text NOT NULL CHECK (mood IN ('great', 'good', 'okay', 'tired', 'stressed')),
  energy_level     int NOT NULL DEFAULT 3 CHECK (energy_level >= 1 AND energy_level <= 5),
  notes            text DEFAULT '',
  recommended_mode text DEFAULT '',
  logged_at        date NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE(user_id, logged_at)
);
ALTER TABLE mood_log ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can manage own mood" ON mood_log FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- 11. quests — Справочник квестов
CREATE TABLE quests (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  title       text NOT NULL,
  description text NOT NULL,
  xp_reward   int NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,
  type        text NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('daily', 'weekly')),
  target_type text NOT NULL, -- 'analyze', 'quiz', 'chat', 'mood', 'exam', 'review', 'podcast'
  target_count int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- 12. user_quest_progress — Прогресс пользователя в квестах
CREATE TABLE user_quest_progress (
  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  quest_id      uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES quests(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  current_count int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  is_completed  boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  completed_at  timestamptz,
  reset_at      date NOT NULL, -- Дата, когда квест должен сброситься
  updated_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE(user_id, quest_id, reset_at)
);

ALTER TABLE quests ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Quests are readable by all authenticated users" ON quests FOR SELECT USING (auth.role() = 'authenticated');

ALTER TABLE user_quest_progress ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Users can manage own quest progress" ON user_quest_progress FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- Триггер: автосоздание профиля при регистрации
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user()
RETURNS trigger AS 
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, full_name)
  VALUES (NEW.id, COALESCE(NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', ''));
  RETURN NEW;
END;
 LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER on_auth_user_created
  AFTER INSERT ON auth.users
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user();
